# End-to-end flows

12 flows traced across frontend, API, services, worker and SQL, each fact-checked by an independent agent. Entry point: [`../CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md`](../CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md).

Anchors are `file:line` as of 2026-09-22 and will drift; re-grep rather than assume a contradiction.

1. [Guest asks a question from the public landing page](#1-guest-ask)
2. [Authenticated user asks a substantive question (the full RAG path)](#2-auth-ask)
3. [Follow-up referencing an earlier numbered source, ordinal, or author](#3-followup)
4. [Refusal, notice and capacity-degradation paths](#4-refusals)
5. [Single PDF submitted through the upload wizard](#5-upload-submit)
6. [Worker picks up a queued job and makes the paper searchable](#6-worker-ingest)
7. [Batch upload and admin job monitoring](#7-batch-upload)
8. [On-demand novelty / duplication scan](#8-novelty-scan)
9. [Account lifecycle: sign-up, approval, MFA, privileged access](#9-account-lifecycle)
10. [Browsing, filtering and reading the archive](#10-archive-browse)
11. [Admin console: analytics, operations, maintenance, settings](#11-admin-ops)
12. [Producing defense evidence: evaluation, fingerprint, load test, deck](#12-evidence-pipeline)

## 1. Guest asks a question from the public landing page

<a id="1-guest-ask"></a>

Flow key: `guest-ask`

### Overview

Guest asks a question from the public site and gets a grounded, cited answer — landing page → /chat (guest) → Turnstile gate → POST /chat → guest budget → department pinning → pgvector retrieval → Gemini generation → citation validation → rendered bubble + source cards. Nothing is persisted for a guest except an activity_log row.

**Trigger:** A visitor on `/` clicks one of the four guest-chat CTAs (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/landing/Hero.jsx:154, Hero.jsx:187, LandingNav.jsx:86, FinalCTA.jsx:54, Footer.jsx:19), lands on the `/chat` React route, types a question and presses Enter / the send button. NOTE: the "Ask like a student" demo the task names as the entry point (AskDemo.jsx) is a pure animation and is NOT part of this flow — see surprises.

**Actors, in order:** Landing page (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Landing.jsx) -> Chat page (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx) -> useGuestChatGate + SecurityCheck + TurnstileWidget -> axios instance (rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js) -> Vite dev proxy / nginx (vite.config.js:95) -> FastAPI middleware stack (rag-thesis-backend/main.py:153-201) -> dependencies/auth.require_chat_access -> services/turnstile.ensure_guest_chat_verification -> Cloudflare siteverify -> routers/chat._chat_impl_unstamped -> services/guards.prohibited_reason -> services/guest_budget (limits storage / Redis) -> services/embedder -> Gemini embeddings -> Supabase RPC match_chunks + check_topic_duplication (pgvector/HNSW) -> services/retriever.search_chunks -> services/prompts + gemini_pool -> Gemini chat -> services/citations -> services/activity.log_activity -> activity_log table

**Latency:** Serial cost of a grounded guest turn: one Gemini embedding call (`embed_text`, retried up to 3 times with 0.5 s exponential backoff) -> `match_chunks` and `check_topic_duplication` run CONCURRENTLY via asyncio.gather on the same vector (chat.py:1979-1985) -> up to two extra Supabase round trips for legacy chunk metadata and paper metadata -> one Gemini chat completion (60 s `gemini_timeout_seconds`, `max_retries=1`, `thinking_level='low'`, 2000 output tokens direct / 6000 via gateway, run alongside the duplication summary when an alert exists). A follow-up that needs rewriting or a non-English question each add one full serial Gemini call BEFORE the embedding. A failed citation validation adds one more after generation. Every Supabase call is a thread hop (`asyncio.to_thread`) since supabase-py is synchronous. Axios gives up at 180 s. `gemini_pool.attempt_chain` puts the gateway first when configured and skips it entirely while cooling, specifically because an unreachable gateway can burn the full 60 s timeout before Google is even tried (gemini_pool.py:224-229). The pre-retrieval `guest_budget.is_exhausted` check exists so an out-of-allowance guest is refused before the embedding, not after (chat.py:2215-2223). `GET /settings/public` is cached with `staleTime: Infinity` so it costs one request per page load.

| 2 | frontend | `/chat` is a lazy route wrapped in `ProtectedRoute isAllowed={canChat} allowGuest={true}`. For a guest `user` is null and ProtectedRoute returns children before it ever reads `isAllowed`, so `canChat` is irrelevant to guests. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/App.jsx:48` |

The rewritten question is re-run through `prohibited_reason`. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2430-2553` |
| 34 | service | Cross-lingual step: `query_translation.looks_non_english()` (pure token-marker heuristic) can trigger one extra Gemini call to translate the QUERY ONLY. SQL joins chunks->papers->paper_index_versions, requires `c.index_version = p.active_index_version`, `ingestion_status='ready'`, matching embedding model+dims, provenance in ('verified','legacy_assumed'), and sets `hnsw.ef_search = 100` on the function. | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260903_hnsw_ef_search.sql:35-69` |
| 38 | service | `search_chunks` selection stage: `top_similarity` = max over the WHOLE pool; `rerank_candidates` blends 0.75*cosine + 0.25*(normalized lexical score, section-title hits worth 3x); `apply_paper_diversity` picks exactly 5 with at most `retrieval_per_paper_cap`=3 per paper, backfilling over-cap chunks and re-sorting into rank order. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/retriever.py:764-817, rag-thesis-backend/services/retriever.py:705-761` |

| 40 | service | Citation ids are stamped in rank order BEFORE `long_context_reorder`, which then interleaves blocks so the best evidence sits at both ends of the window. No PDF URL, storage path or chunk text ever leaves the server. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/retriever.py:44-75, rag-thesis-backend/services/retriever.py:909-922` |

A grounded answer gets no chip. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/messageNotice.js:29-35` |
| 59 | frontend | Flow terminates: response actions (Redo / Copy / Listen / Copy with sources / Download research note / Ask a follow-up / See response details) attach to the bubble. Nothing else is written; a page reload loses the whole guest transcript. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:579-724, rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/responseActions.js:56-71` |

### Branches

- TURNSTILE OFF (default, `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY=''`): `guard_enabled()` is False and `ensure_guest_chat_verification` returns immediately — services/turnstile.py:36-38, :117. The frontend gate is independently off whenever `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` is empty (turnstileConfig.js:9-11), so the question goes straight out with only X-Guest-ID.
- TURNSTILE ON, first message: `isReady()` false -> question parked in the composer, no bubble; the composer button switches to the 'verifying' state (spinner, disabled) — Chat.jsx:1102-1109, :164-192. The widget solves, `releaseParkedSend` re-fires `send()` automatically.
- TURNSTILE ON, second and later messages in the same tab: `markPassed()` cleared `needed`, so `active` is false, `tokenForRequest()` returns null and no X-Turnstile-Token is sent; the backend passes on its in-process verified-guest cache alone (services/turnstile.py:120).
- AUTHENTICATED instead of guest: `department` is resolved in the wrapper via `resolve_effective_department(user, filter)` (non-superadmin pinned to their profile department, 403 on mismatch), history is loaded from the saved session, and the exchange is persisted through `save_chat_exchange` with `p_kind` — chat.py:2027-2031, :2042-2065.
- CONVERSATIONAL FAST PATH (greeting/thanks/identity/capability/model/origin): returns before any budget check, embedding or Gemini call, with `notice_type='conversation'` and zero sources — chat.py:2126-2205. The UI renders it with no chip at all.
- CATALOG FAST PATH (inventory / count / 'the remaining 6'): pure Postgres, `archive_current=True`, real source cards, no model call — chat.py:2286-2323.
- AUTHOR FAST PATH: `_extract_author_name` or a `what about <name>` token resolved against `papers.authors` metadata; taken only when the archive confirms a match, or when the wording is an explicit author-identity question — chat.py:2382-2424.
- AGGREGATE question type: `per_paper_cap=1` so the 5 blocks come from 5 distinct theses, and the prompt gains the sample-scoped TASK block — chat.py:2612, prompts.py:255-265.
- NON-ENGLISH question: one extra Gemini call translates the QUERY for embedding only; the generation prompt still sees the original wording — chat.py:2576-2593.
- CAPACITY COOLDOWN ACTIVE: any question in the 60 s window returns HTTP 200 + CAPACITY_MESSAGE with no provider call — chat.py:2426-2428.
- DUPLICATION HIT (>=0.85): a second concurrent Gemini call summarizes the matched study; the banner renders above the response actions with the exact similarity percentage — chat.py:2713-2729, Chat.jsx:365-405.
- ROLLBACK LEVER: setting `RETRIEVAL_CANDIDATE_POOL == RETRIEVAL_MATCH_COUNT` disables rerank + diversity entirely and reproduces pure cosine order — retriever.py:810-817.
- THESIS CATEGORY FILTER: a guest MAY narrow retrieval to student/faculty theses; the key is omitted from the RPC params entirely when unset so the evaluated call is byte-identical — models.py:25, retriever.py:793-796.

### Failure modes

- TURNSTILE TOKEN MISSING (guard on, no X-Guest-ID or no X-Turnstile-Token): HTTP 403, `detail='Please complete the security check to continue as a guest.'`, header `X-Guest-Verification: required` — services/turnstile.py:122-128. UI: the user bubble is removed (`messages.slice(0,-1)`), the question is restored to the composer, `handleChatError` clears the sessionStorage pass flag and re-arms the widget with a bumped resetKey, and a red banner + toast both read 'IskAI could not answer' with that detail. The question is NOT auto-resent — `awaitingCheckRef` is false by then, so `releaseParkedSend` returns early; the user must press Retry (Chat.jsx:1174-1182, useGuestChatGate.js:66-74, Chat.jsx:1370).
- TURNSTILE VERIFY FAILS OR CLOUDFLARE UNREACHABLE: `verify_turnstile_token` fails closed on httpx.HTTPError/ValueError or `success:false` or action/hostname mismatch -> HTTP 403 `detail='Security check failed or expired. Please try the check again.'`, header `X-Guest-Verification: failed` — turnstile.py:93-108, :131-136. Same UI handling as above.
- TURNSTILE CHALLENGE NEVER ANSWERS: after 12 s SecurityCheck promotes `pending` to the synthetic `unavailable` status, reveals the panel ('Security check did not respond' + a 'Try again' button). BUT `isVerifyingSend` only exempts 'error' and 'unsupported', so the composer's send button stays disabled with a spinner indefinitely, and the panel body says 'You can continue — your sign-in is still verified by the server.' — wrong copy for guest chat, where the server will 403. SecurityCheck.jsx:12, :62, :156-160; Chat.jsx:196-198.
- TURNSTILE SCRIPT BLOCKED / UNSUPPORTED BROWSER: `loadTurnstile()` rejects (and drops the cached promise + dead tag so the next mount retries) -> status 'error' or 'unsupported'. `isVerifyingSend` returns false for both, so the send button re-enables — and the request then goes out with no token and is 403'd by the backend. TurnstileWidget.jsx:40-46, :96-97.
- THEME TOGGLE MID-CHALLENGE: TurnstileWidget's effect depends on `isDark`, so switching theme tears down and re-renders the widget, emitting `onToken(null)` -> `acceptToken(null)` -> tokenRef cleared. A solved gate silently reverts to unsolved and the next send parks. TurnstileWidget.jsx:79, :110.
- GUEST BUDGET EXHAUSTED, pre-retrieval: HTTP **200** with `answer=GUEST_BUDGET_MESSAGE` ("IskAI's shared daily allowance for guest research questions has been used up. Please sign in with your ISU account to continue, or try again tomorrow."), `sources=[]`, `kind='notice'`, `archive_current=false`. Logged as `chat_query_budget_exhausted` stage='pre_retrieval'. UI renders it as an ordinary AI bubble with the gold 'System message · not a research answer.' chip. chat.py:2218-2223, guest_budget.py:51-54.
- GUEST BUDGET EXHAUSTED, pre-generation: identical 200 + notice, stage='pre_generation'. The embedding call, the match_chunks RPC and the duplication RPC have ALREADY been paid for at this point; only the chat completion is refused. chat.py:2676-2691.
- GUEST BUDGET EXHAUSTED mid-turn at the follow-up rewrite or the query translation: same 200 + notice, stage='followup_rewrite' / 'query_translation'. chat.py:2531-2542, :2578-2585.
- GUEST BUDGET STORE UNREACHABLE: both `spent_today` and `charge` catch every exception and fail OPEN (`ALLOWED_UNLIMITED`), logging only a warning — the ceiling silently stops existing. guest_budget.py:116-120, :141-143.
- RATE LIMIT: HTTP 429 with body `{"error": "Rate limit exceeded: 30 per 1 minute"}` from slowapi's stock handler. `apiErrorMessage` looks for `detail` then `message` and finds neither, so the banner and toast read the generic 'Something went wrong. Please try again.' main.py:154, api/lib/apiErrors.js:10-22.
- GUEST SENDS session_id: HTTP 401 'Guest conversations do not have saved sessions.' The axios 401 interceptor then attempts a silent Supabase refresh (fails for a guest), calls `supabase.auth.signOut()` and HARD-NAVIGATES to /login?next=/chat — losing the transcript. chat.py:2115-2117, api.js:127-147.
- PROHIBITED REQUEST (write my chapter 1 / prompt injection, in English, Filipino or Ilocano): HTTP 200 with REFUSAL_MESSAGE, `sources=[]`, `kind='notice'`, no `notice_type` -> the 'System message · not a research answer.' chip. Logged as `chat_query_blocked` with reason 'prompt_injection' or 'academic_content_generation'. chat.py:2207-2213, guards.py:6-10.
- NO CHUNK CLEARS 0.30: HTTP 200, `answer='No relevant thesis was found in the CCSICT archive for that query. Try rephrasing with different technical terms, or ask about another topic.'`, `no_relevant_thesis=true`, `archive_current=true`, sources empty. UI chip: 'Search completed · no qualifying archive evidence.' chat.py:2645-2657.
- OPAQUE SINGLE-TOKEN QUERY: HTTP 200 with UNCLEAR_TOPIC_MESSAGE and no sources. chat.py:2659-2671.
- RETRIEVAL THROWS (Supabase down, RPC missing, embedding failure): if the exception reads as a capacity error it trips the 60 s process cooldown and returns 200 + CAPACITY_MESSAGE; otherwise HTTP 503 'The thesis archive is temporarily unavailable. Please try again in a moment.' chat.py:2628-2636. The UI shows the banner with that detail and a Retry button (503 is retryable per isRetryableFailure).
- GENERATION THROWS: capacity error -> 60 s cooldown + 200 CAPACITY_MESSAGE; anything else -> HTTP 502 'The research AI service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.' chat.py:2732-2740. Note axios only auto-retries GETs on 502/504, so this POST is not retried automatically.
- GENERATION TRUNCATED (finish_reason MAX_TOKENS / length): the fragment is DISCARDED, not repaired — the answer is replaced with `_grounded_retrieval_fallback` (up to 3 closest titles with their citation ids) and sources are filtered to what that text cites. Logged with the active output ceiling and whether the gateway was in play. chat.py:2798-2812.
- CITATION VALIDATION FAILS AND BOTH REPAIRS FAIL: the answer is replaced with the grounded fallback. Because `enforce_citation_coverage` returns unmodified when the context spans more than one paper, this is the common outcome on a multi-thesis grounded answer whose AI repair did not converge. chat.py:2874-2887, citations.py:132-133.
- MODEL REPORTS NO EVIDENCE (sentinel or phrase): the model's own text is KEPT along with whatever it cited, and the repair ladder is deliberately bypassed. Only if nothing survives (`not answer.strip() or not unique_sources`) does it degrade to the generic no-relevant notice with `no_relevant_thesis=True`. chat.py:2813-2833.
- DUPLICATION SUMMARY CALL FAILS: caught and returns '', so the banner renders without the 'About the matched study' paragraph. chat.py:1905-1907.
- ACTIVITY LOG INSERT FAILS: caught, logged as a warning, response unaffected. services/activity.py:36-41.
- USER PRESSES STOP: the AbortController aborts, `dropPendingPrompt` removes the pending user bubble, and a banner reads 'Response stopped — The question is preserved here. The browser stopped waiting, while any secure server cleanup may continue.' The server keeps generating; for a guest `_client_gone` is never even consulted (it is gated on `user`). Chat.jsx:1198-1213, chat.py:2042.
- REQUEST EXCEEDS 180 s: axios times out, `isCancelledRequest` is false, so it lands in the generic catch -> banner 'IskAI could not answer' with the axios timeout message. api.js:10.
- QUESTION > 4000 CHARS: HTTP 422 from pydantic; `apiErrorMessage` renders the FastAPI validation list as 'body.question: String should have at most 4000 characters'. models.py:20.
- BACKEND SECRET SET BUT FRONTEND SITE KEY MISSING: the first request 403s with the header, `handleChatError` sets `needed=true`/`armed=true`, but SecurityCheck and TurnstileWidget both early-return null when `turnstileEnabled` is false. `active` is now true with no widget to ever produce a token, so `isReady()` is permanently false and every subsequent send parks forever behind a disabled spinner with nothing on screen explaining why. useGuestChatGate.js:66-74, SecurityCheck.jsx:162, TurnstileWidget.jsx:112.

### What gets persisted

- `activity_log` — exactly one INSERT per guest turn (including every fast path and every notice branch): `{user_id: NULL, action: 'chat_query' | 'chat_query_blocked' | 'chat_query_budget_exhausted', department: 'CCSICT' or NULL, detail: jsonb}`. Written from a FastAPI BackgroundTask, i.e. AFTER the response is sent. rag-thesis-backend/services/activity.py:30-35, supabase_setup.sql:754-761.
- Guest daily token counter — `guest-token-budget:<YYYY-MM-DD>` in the `limits` storage (memory:// in dev, Redis in production), incremented atomically with a TTL running to just past UTC midnight. Incremented even for refused requests. rag-thesis-backend/services/guest_budget.py:79-92, :138-140.
- Verified-guest cache — `_verified_guests[guest_id] = monotonic + turnstile_guest_ttl_seconds` in a module-level dict inside the API process. Lost on restart, not shared between replicas. rag-thesis-backend/services/turnstile.py:33, :54-58.
- Gemini key cooldowns and the capacity cooldown — `gemini_pool._cooldowns` and `chat_notices._CAPACITY_STATE`, both in-process. rag-thesis-backend/services/gemini_pool.py:62, rag-thesis-backend/services/chat_notices.py:194.
- Browser sessionStorage — `iskai_guest_rate_id` (the UUID sent as X-Guest-ID) and `iskai_guest_gate_ok` ('1' once a guest turn has succeeded). Both per-tab. rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:26-38, rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/useGuestChatGate.js:4, :58.
- NOT written for a guest: no `chat_sessions` row, no `chat_messages` row, no `save_chat_exchange` RPC call. `response.session_id` stays None and `history_saved` stays False. The transcript exists only in React state and dies on reload. rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2042.

### Surprises

- **AskDemo.jsx makes no API calls at all.** It is a scripted four-phase animation with a hard-coded question, a hard-coded answer string, and two invented source cards ('CNN-Based Rice Leaf Disease Detection', 'Maize Disease Image Classification'). The composer is `aria-hidden` and its send button is inert. rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/landing/AskDemo.jsx:15-19, :21-29, :261. The real guest flow starts at a `navigate('/chat')` elsewhere on the page.
- **There is no streaming.** `chatQuery` is one POST that resolves with the complete answer (api.js:179). The word-by-word reveal and the `StreamingRing` on the composer are animations over an already-complete string; `TypingIndicator` shows for the whole round trip. AskDemo's staggered word animation makes the product look like it streams. Chat.jsx:149-162, :778-793.
- **Guest budget exhaustion and provider capacity are HTTP 200, not 429/503.** They come back as a normal ChatResponse carrying a notice string. Anything that checks status codes — a load test, a monitor, the JMeter summarizers — sees a healthy request. chat.py:1360-1365, :2223.
- **A refused guest-budget request still increments the counter**, deliberately, to avoid a check-then-increment race. So a stampede against an already-exhausted budget inflates `spent` far past `budget`. guest_budget.py:25-29, :138-145.
- **The single pre-generation budget charge does not cover the whole turn.** One guest turn can make up to five Gemini calls — follow-up rewrite, query translation, main generation, duplication summary, and one or two repair calls. `_charge_guest_extra` books the extras but the caller ignores the decision for anything after generation has started, so the counter is honest only for the NEXT request. chat.py:1378-1400, :2717, :2783, :2843.
- **A guest's `department_filter` is silently discarded, not rejected.** `resolve_effective_department` returns `settings.thesis_evaluation_department` on its first line before looking at `requested`. A signed-in student sending the wrong department gets 403; a guest sending anything gets a silent override. dependencies/auth.py:105-108.
- **`TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` is not required in production.** `validate_production_services` enforces Redis, MFA, ClamAV and a non-zero guest budget, but never the Turnstile secret — so a production API can boot with the guest bot guard entirely off. config.py:242-263.
- **The frontend Turnstile switch and the backend Turnstile switch are unrelated.** One is `VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` baked in at build time (turnstileConfig.js:9-11), the other is `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY` read from the backend env (turnstile.py:36-38). The backend-on/frontend-off combination produces a permanently disabled composer with no visible widget and no error text.
- **`enforce_citation_coverage` is a no-op on the ordinary grounded path.** It returns the answer unchanged unless every selected source belongs to a single identified paper (`len(paper_ids) != 1` -> return). Since the grounded path selects up to 5 chunks across up to 5 papers, the 'deterministic' rung of the repair ladder almost never fires there; it exists for the overview and exact-paper paths. citations.py:120-143.
- **Citation numbers do not match the order of the context blocks the model sees.** Ids are stamped in post-rerank quality order, then `long_context_reorder` interleaves the blocks so the best sit at both ends of the window — so `[1]` is neither the first nor necessarily an early block in the prompt. retriever.py:864-869, :28-41.
- **The visible citation numbers are frequently non-contiguous.** `filter_cited_sources` drops every source the model did not cite, but the surviving cards keep their original `citation_id`, so a reader can see 'Evidence sources' cards labelled 2 and 5 with no 1, 3 or 4 anywhere. citations.py:29-36, Chat.jsx:313-322.
- **`top_similarity` is the best cosine over the whole 15-candidate pool, not over the 5 blocks that reached the prompt.** It is reported to LangSmith and to the evaluation trace as the turn's similarity. retriever.py:806-808.
- **The 85% duplication check runs on every ordinary guest question**, concurrently with retrieval, using the same embedding — so an ordinary research question can produce the topic-duplication banner and a second billed Gemini call. chat.py:1965-1985, :2713-2717.
- **`_chat_impl` overwrites `ChatResponse.kind` unconditionally**, so the explicit `kind='notice'` on the unclear-topic branch (chat.py:2670) is a dead assignment that happens to agree with what `response_kind` computes. chat.py:2094.
- **The response's `kind` field collides with the frontend's own `kind`.** `setMessages` spreads `...res` and then re-forces `kind:'ai'`, carrying the server value as `messageKind` — if the order were reversed the server's 'answer'/'notice' would overwrite the message role and break every render that branches on it. Chat.jsx:1161-1163.
- **A 429 reaches the user as 'Something went wrong. Please try again.'** slowapi's stock handler emits `{"error": ...}` while `apiErrorMessage` only understands `detail` and `message`. There is no wording anywhere telling a rate-limited guest to slow down. main.py:154, lib/apiErrors.js:10-22.
- **The verified-guest cache is in-process**, documented as matching a single-replica deployment. Behind more than one API replica a guest re-solves the challenge once per replica, and the frontend's `iskai_guest_gate_ok` flag suppresses the widget until a 403 arrives to re-arm it. turnstile.py:11-13.
- **`get_optional_user` does not degrade an invalid token to guest** — a supplied-but-expired JWT raises 401 rather than being treated as anonymous, which is why the axios interceptor's sign-out-and-redirect path exists on a page that is supposed to work without an account. auth.py:243-260.
- **`match_chunks` carries `SET hnsw.ef_search = 100` on the function itself**, which disables SQL inlining for the planner — a deliberate trade documented in the migration, made because six post-filters applied after a 40-candidate HNSW scan could return fewer than `match_count` rows even when qualifying chunks existed. migrations/20260903_hnsw_ef_search.sql:1-31.
- **Guests never send the assistant's answers back.** `guest_history` is user questions only; continuity is carried entirely by `guest_source_ids` (re-fetched and department-scoped server-side) plus the prior questions, and the model's `{chat_history}` block for a guest therefore contains questions with no answers. Chat.jsx:1121-1126, chat.py:2257-2262.
- **`_client_gone` is only consulted for authenticated users**, so a guest pressing Stop still burns the full generation and the full budget charge — there is simply nothing to persist. chat.py:2042.
- **`conversation_replies` exists only to stop canned greetings repeating.** The 500-char cap is enforced client-side by `withinReplyLimit` because Redo used to append a full grounded answer and earn a 422 for the whole turn. Chat.jsx:60-72, models.py:42-44.

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 10 correction(s), 7 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** Step 10 anchor: `rag-thesis-backend/../rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1096-1109`
  **Actual:** Malformed path (backend prefix with a `..` escape). The real anchor is rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1096-1109; the line range itself is correct (`send` at :1096, park-and-return at :1102-1109). (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1096`)
- **Wrong:** Step 27: "Zero-cost conversational fast paths, in order: model question, capability question, thanks/farewell, greeting/identity, self-origin" (chat.py:2126-2205) — presented as the complete set.
  **Actual:** Incomplete. Four more zero-cost fast paths exist, and three of them run AFTER the pre-retrieval budget check and after `reference_sources` is fetched, so they are reachable on a guest's first question: `_is_ambiguous_system_identity_question` (chat.py:2329-2341), `_is_self_platform_reference` (chat.py:2346-2361), `_is_ambiguous_system_origin_question` (chat.py:2369-2380), plus a SECOND author fast-path attempt on the rewritten question (chat.py:2556-2559). The trace's step 31 covers only inventory + author. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2329`)
- **Wrong:** Step 5: GET /settings/public is "Unauthenticated, undecorated by any named rate limit (config.rate_limit_public exists but is not applied here)" — implying no ceiling.
  **Actual:** A ceiling does apply. `Limiter(..., default_limits=['120/minute'])` (rag-thesis-backend/services/rate_limiting.py:41-45) is enforced by SlowAPIMiddleware on any route with no decorator: slowapi adds `_default_limits` when `route_limits` is empty (.venv/.../slowapi/extension.py:615-633). So /settings/public is limited to 120/minute keyed by `rate_limit_key` (guest:<sha256> or ip:). Conversely POST /chat gets ONLY its two decorated limits, because `limiter.limit()` defaults `override_defaults=True` — the 120/minute default does not stack there. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/rate_limiting.py:43`)
- **Wrong:** Step 22: "Success also requires `action == 'guest_chat'`".
  **Actual:** It requires `action == settings.turnstile_expected_action.strip()` and the check is skipped entirely when that setting is empty (services/turnstile.py:77-82). 'guest_chat' is only the default (config.py:176), so the assertion is configuration-dependent, not a hardcoded constant. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/turnstile.py:77`)
- **Wrong:** Step 52 / persistence: activity_log row described as "action 'chat_query', user_id NULL, department 'CCSICT', detail {...}" with `log_activity` taking department.
  **Actual:** Two details off. (1) chat.py never passes the `department` argument; `log_activity` recovers it from `detail['department']` (services/activity.py:19), which is why the fast-path, blocked and budget-exhausted logs (chat.py:2127-2132, :2209-2212, :2219-2222) write department NULL — they omit that key and user_id is None so the profile lookup is skipped. (2) `activity_log.department` carries a NOT VALID foreign key to `departments(name)` (supabase_setup.sql:823-825), so a department string with no matching row would fail the insert (swallowed at activity.py:36). (`rag-thesis-backend/services/activity.py:19`)
- **Wrong:** Surprise: "One guest turn can make up to five Gemini calls — follow-up rewrite, query translation, main generation, duplication summary, and one or two repair calls."
  **Actual:** Up to six, and the trace's own step-50 ladder omits one whole rung: the multi-paper coverage repair at chat.py:2772-2789 is a separate `_repair_multi_paper_coverage` generation call that runs BEFORE the misdirected-greeting check and before the citation ladder, booked-but-not-refused via `_charge_guest_extra` at :2783. (The code's own docstring at chat.py:1382 saying "A turn can make up to four" is itself stale.) (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2783`)
- **Wrong:** Step 51: `filter_cited_sources` cited at chat.py:2888 as the single filtering point.
  **Actual:** It runs at three sites on three different branches: :2812 (truncated → grounded fallback), :2827 (model reports no evidence), :2888 (ordinary path). The truncated and no-evidence branches never reach :2888. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2812`)
- **Wrong:** Surprise: "`enforce_citation_coverage` is a no-op on the ordinary grounded path ... citations.py:120-143".
  **Actual:** Substance is correct (the gate is `if len(paper_ids) != 1 or not next(iter(paper_ids)): return repaired` at citations.py:132-133), but the anchor range is wrong: the function is defined at citations.py:93-143; lines 120-131 are still docstring and the `allowed`/`repaired` setup. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/citations.py:93`)
- **Wrong:** Step 40: each block is `[n] Title: ... | Authors: ... | Pages: ... | Section: ...`.
  **Actual:** The meta_bits list can also carry Track, Year, Department, Program and Specialization between Authors and Pages (retriever.py:877-897), so the block header is wider than the trace shows. Everything else in step 40 (ids stamped pre-reorder, `safe_chunk_text` rewriting line-initial `[7]` to `(7)`) is exact. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/retriever.py:877`)
- **Wrong:** Step 15: X-Guest-ID is "a `crypto.randomUUID()` persisted in sessionStorage ... (throws TypeError if no secure RNG)".
  **Actual:** There is an intermediate fallback: `crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16))` hex-encoded, and only then the TypeError (api.js:31-35). The 32-hex-char fallback still satisfies the backend's 16-128 alnum guest-key check, so the `guest:` rate-limit bucket still applies. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:31`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- ProtectedRoute has a `loading` gate BEFORE the guest passthrough: `if (loading) return <RouteSkeleton />` — a guest sees the route-shaped skeleton until AuthContext resolves the (absent) session, and only then does :21-24 return children. rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx:17-19.
- REAL INCONSISTENCY the trace did not catch: the two origin fast paths return the SAME string (`_origin_response()` -> `chat_notices.SYSTEM_ORIGIN_MESSAGE`, chat.py:847-851) but classify differently in the UI. The early one sets `notice_type='conversation'` (chat.py:2200-2205) so `messageNoticeLabel` returns null and no chip renders; the later ambiguous-origin one omits it (chat.py:2376-2380), so `response_kind` stamps 'notice' by exact equality (chat_notices.py:262) and the UI paints the gold 'System message · not a research answer.' chip on identical text. messageNotice.js:32-33.
- `match_chunks` / `check_topic_duplication` in 20260903_hnsw_ef_search.sql are the LATEST definitions — no later migration re-creates either function (only 20260717_*, 20260720_index_embedding_provenance.sql, 20260819_thesis_category.sql and supabase_setup.sql define them, all earlier by filename order). The trace asserts the SQL but never establishes that it is the surviving body, which is the load-bearing fact given the replay hazard documented at migrations/20260903_hnsw_ef_search.sql:28-31.
- The `send()` error path does not reset `sending` before the finally block, and `stopWaiting` nulls `requestControllerRef` itself — so the finally guard `if (requestControllerRef.current === controller)` (Chat.jsx:1184) is what prevents a Stop-then-late-response from clobbering the new state. The trace describes Stop but not this reconciliation.
- `conversation_replies` is not simply 'assistant replies already shown': the client filters to `notice_type === 'conversation'` messages only (Chat.jsx:1147) BEFORE applying `withinReplyLimit`, so a grounded answer can never enter the array on the normal path — the 500-char filter is a second belt for the `options.conversationReplies` override used by Redo. Chat.jsx:1146-1150.
- `estimate_charge` tokenizes with `services.chunker.count_tokens` (guest_budget.py:45,105) — the same cl100k_base proxy the frozen chunker uses, not the Gemini tokenizer. The booked number is therefore an approximation of Gemini spend by construction, on top of being a worst-case over-estimate.
- Verified as stated and worth recording: `guest_daily_token_budget` defaults to 0 (config.py:112) = unlimited, so in every non-production environment the entire budget ladder the trace describes (steps 29, 45 and four failure modes) is inert — `is_enabled()` returns False and both `is_exhausted` and `charge` short-circuit (guest_budget.py:95-96, :126, :132-133).

## 2. Authenticated user asks a substantive question (the full RAG path)

<a id="2-auth-ask"></a>

Flow key: `auth-ask`

### Overview

Authenticated user asks a substantive research question in /chat and receives a markdown answer with `[n]` citation markers plus per-passage source cards, persisted to chat_messages as kind='answer'

**Trigger:** User types a question into the composer textarea on /chat and presses Enter (or Ctrl/Cmd+Enter when `sendKey === 'ctrlEnter'`), or clicks the circular submit button, or clicks one of the four STARTER chips. All three converge on `send()` at rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1096.

**Actors, in order:** Browser: Chat.jsx React component (optimistic transcript, AbortController, React Query cache) -> Browser: src/api.js axios instance (JWT attach, 401 silent refresh, GET-only gateway retry) -> Supabase Auth (token validation via sb.auth.get_user) -> Vite dev proxy (spaAwareApiProxy) or nginx in the container -> FastAPI middleware stack: CORS -> BodySizeLimitMiddleware -> security_headers -> GZip -> SlowAPIMiddleware -> routers/chat.py::chat route wrapper (rate limits, Turnstile, department, persistence) -> dependencies/auth.py (require_chat_access, get_optional_user, get_user_role, resolve_effective_department) -> routers/chat.py::_chat_impl_unstamped (fast paths, guards, follow-up resolution, generation, citation ladder) -> services/embedder.py + services/gemini_pool.py (Gemini embedding, 768-d) -> Postgres/pgvector RPC match_chunks and check_topic_duplication (service_role only) -> services/retriever.py (rerank, per-paper cap, LongContextReorder, context assembly, public_source) -> services/prompts.py (composed rule blocks, PROMPT_VERSION iskai-prompt-v4) -> Gemini gemini-3.6-flash via gemini_pool.arun (optional OpenAI-compatible gateway first, then primary key, then reserve keys) -> services/citations.py (marker normalisation, validation, coverage enforcement) -> Postgres RPC save_chat_exchange (SECURITY DEFINER) -> BackgroundTasks -> services/activity.py::log_activity -> activity_log table

**Latency:** Axios timeout is 180s (api.js:10); gemini_timeout_seconds is 60 with max_retries 1, so a single hop can burn 120s and the gateway hop adds another 60 before Google is even tried. Before the first model call an authenticated turn with an open session makes roughly: 1 Supabase Auth call (sb.auth.get_user), 1-3 `profiles` reads (_ensure_approved_account always; get_user_role behind a 60s cache; get_user_scope with NO cache), 2 `chat_sessions` reads (_ensure_session_owner then again inside _load_chat_history), 1 `chat_messages` read, and 1 `papers` read for reference_sources. Retrieval then costs 1 Gemini embedding plus match_chunks and check_topic_duplication run CONCURRENTLY (chat.py:1981), plus 1-2 more Postgres reads for paper metadata and legacy chunk locations. Generation and the duplication summary also run concurrently (chat.py:1999). Worst case a single turn makes 5 Gemini chat calls: follow-up rewrite, query translation, main generation, duplication summary, and one repair — the rewrite and translation are strictly sequential in front of generation. Every Supabase call is wrapped in asyncio.to_thread, and retry_transient gives 3 attempts with 0.25s exponential backoff (0.5s base for embeddings). GZip kicks in above 1024 bytes, which a 5-source answer always exceeds. hnsw.ef_search is pinned to 100 on the RPC, which also disables SQL-function inlining by the planner.

| 72 | frontend | Render: AiBubble renders message.answer through ReactMarkdown, an 'archive_current' line, and messageNoticeLabel(message) — null here because no_relevant_thesis is false, notice_type is null and messageKind is 'answer'. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:726-752, src/pages/chat/messageNotice.js:29-35` |

### Branches

- Guest vs authenticated: a guest never reaches steps 13/14/17's profile reads, gets effective_department = THESIS_EVALUATION_DEPARTMENT unconditionally (auth.py:107-108), must pass Turnstile when TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY is set, supplies its own guest_history (<=5 questions) and guest_source_ids (<=10 ids, re-scoped server-side), is subject to the guest token budget at four separate charge points, and is never persisted (routers/chat.py:2042).
- Superadmin: resolve_effective_department validates the requested department against the departments table and returns 422 'Unknown department.' if absent (auth.py:121-129). The frontend shows a department <Select> for superadmins only and calls newConversation() on change (Chat.jsx:1306-1319).
- Conversational fast paths (chat.py:2126-2205): model identity, capabilities, thanks/farewell, greeting/identity, system origin. All return notice_type='conversation', zero sources, no retrieval and no generation. varied_message picks the least-recently-used variant from a 10-message pool using the client-supplied conversation_replies (chat_notices.py:143-157).
- Archive catalog path (chat.py:2286-2323): inventory / count / continuation questions bypass the vector pipeline entirely and answer from list_archive_papers — an exact COUNT plus a 10-row alphabetical page ordered (title, id), with the page's own 1..N citation ids. _archive_listing_cursor pages forward by counting distinct titles already shown.
- Author fast path (chat.py:2380-2425): a person-name question answers from papers.authors metadata with no embedding and no generation. A one-word token is confirmed against whole name parts (_author_token_matches, retriever.py:134) to stop 'ai' matching 'Rainier'. Runs twice — once on the raw question, once on a rewritten follow-up.
- Exact-paper branch (referenced_paper_id set): get_paper_overview_context replaces vector search with a metadata read plus up to 200 chunks ranked lexically by rank_paper_chunks. alert_data is None, so no duplication banner is possible on this path, and thesis_category_filter is ignored.
- Plural branch (referenced_paper_id is a list): N concurrent get_paper_overview_context calls, context markers renumbered with an offset, exact_papers_prompt, plus a bounded multi-paper coverage repair when _missing_referenced_papers finds an omitted thesis (chat.py:1910-1946, 2769-2790).
- Ambiguous follow-up with >=2 prior sources answers for every shown thesis; with <2 it either resolves locally (_resolve_referenced_thesis / _resolve_specific_paper_followup) or, with no prior sources at all, makes a full Gemini rewrite call — the only branch where a guest is charged and can still be refused before the first paid call (chat.py:2520-2548).
- Non-English question: query_translation.looks_non_english triggers _translate_for_retrieval, which rewrites only the EMBEDDING query. The generation prompt still receives the question as asked, so PROMPT_VERSION is untouched and the English-only Objective 2 harness never enters this path.
- AGGREGATE question type: per_paper_cap=1 so the 5 context blocks come from 5 distinct theses, and the grounded prompt gains the 'CORPUS-WIDE QUESTION' TASK block forcing 'Of the retrieved studies, ...' framing (prompts.py:255-265).
- Duplication alert present: alert_data['summary'] is seeded to '' and _invoke_generation runs the answer and _summarize_duplication as two concurrent Gemini calls; the summary is folded back into DuplicationAlert after generation (chat.py:2712-2727, 1888-1907).
- Rollback lever: if retrieval_candidate_pool is set equal to retrieval_match_count, search_chunks skips rerank and diversity and returns pure cosine order (retriever.py:810-813).
- Capacity cooldown: _capacity_limit_is_active() short-circuits before any model call and returns CAPACITY_MESSAGE at HTTP 200 for the whole 60s window (chat.py:2427).
- Gateway route: with LLM_BASE_URL set, chat/extract/verdict go to a ChatOpenAI client first with reasoning_effort=thinking_level and max_tokens=llm_gateway_max_output_tokens (6000). Embeddings are never routed. A cooling gateway is skipped entirely rather than merely demoted (gemini_pool.py:88-116, 226-232).
- Prompt edit (edit_from_turn set): _load_chat_history uses _history_before_turn instead of the default 5-row window, and _truncate_session_from_turn deletes the branch AFTER the new answer completes.
- Legacy-schema fallbacks: missing thesis_category / program_id columns (SQLSTATE 42703) trigger reduced-column retries in search_chunks, get_paper_overview_context and list_archive_papers; missing page/section columns trigger a JSON metadata backfill read.

### Failure modes

- Expired/invalid JWT -> get_optional_user raises 401 'Invalid or expired authentication token' with WWW-Authenticate: Bearer. Browser: api.js tries ONE silent supabase.auth.refreshSession() and replays the exact request; if the refresh fails it signs out and hard-navigates to loginPathWithNext(current). (api.js:127-147)
- Profile status != 'approved' -> 403 'This account is pending approval or has been rejected.' UI: apiErrorMessage surfaces it in the red chatError bar; isRetryableFailure() is false for 4xx so the bar's Retry is misleading here (it is shown unconditionally, Chat.jsx:1362).
- profiles read fails inside _ensure_approved_account -> 503 'Account validation is temporarily unavailable.'; inside get_user_scope -> 503 'User department validation is unavailable.'; inside the departments validation -> 503 'Department validation is unavailable.'
- get_user_role lookup failure -> silently degrades to 'student' and is deliberately NOT cached, so a transient failure cannot pin an admin to student for 60s (auth.py:62-68).
- role_features read failure -> get_role_features returns {} and _feature_enabled falls through to DEFAULT_FEATURES, so chat and archive stay open (True by default) while upload and novelty fail closed (auth.py:350-372).
- chat feature disabled for the role -> 403 'Research chat is not enabled for your role.' Frontend also blocks the route: ProtectedRoute isAllowed={canChat} redirects to /dashboard (App.jsx:48, ProtectedRoute.jsx:89).
- Guest without Turnstile token -> 403 'Please complete the security check to continue as a guest.' with header X-Guest-Verification: required. Failed verification -> 403 'Security check failed or expired. Please try the check again.' with X-Guest-Verification: failed. useGuestChatGate.handleChatError reads that header, clears sessionStorage, bumps resetKey and re-arms the widget.
- Guest sends session_id -> 401 'Guest conversations do not have saved sessions.'
- Session not owned / mistyped uuid -> 404 'Session not found' (identifier_not_found maps SQLSTATE 22P02 to the same 404). Department mismatch on an owned session -> 409 'This conversation belongs to a different department. Start a new conversation.'
- edit_from_turn without session_id -> 400 'A prompt edit requires a saved session.'
- prohibited_reason hit (prompt_injection or academic_content_generation) -> HTTP 200 carrying REFUSAL_MESSAGE, zero sources, kind='notice'. Also re-checked on a rewritten follow-up (chat.py:2549-2555). UI shows the 'System message · not a research answer.' chip.
- Guest budget exhausted -> HTTP 200 with GUEST_BUDGET_MESSAGE at any of four stages: pre_retrieval, followup_rewrite, query_translation, pre_generation. Budget store unreachable -> fails OPEN (ALLOWED_UNLIMITED) rather than taking guest chat down (guest_budget.py:116-120,141-143).
- Rate limit exceeded -> slowapi's _rate_limit_exceeded_handler returns 429 with CORS headers intact (CORS is outermost). Frontend does not retry POST; the red chatError bar shows the detail.
- Body over the per-path ceiling -> BodySizeLimitMiddleware answers 413 before FastAPI parses the body or resolves a single dependency (request_limits.py module docstring).
- Archive inventory lookup failure -> 503 'The thesis archive is temporarily unavailable. Please try again in a moment.' Same string for author lookup failure and exact-title lookup failure. Title-FRAGMENT lookup failure is downgraded to a warning and continues (it is a guess, not a stated reference).
- Retrieval raises: if is_capacity_error(e) -> mark_capacity_limited() (60s process-wide) and return CAPACITY_MESSAGE at HTTP 200; otherwise 503 'The thesis archive is temporarily unavailable...' (chat.py:2628-2637).
- check_topic_duplication raises -> caught inside the retriever, logged, returns None. Retrieval proceeds without a novelty banner (retriever.py:959-962).
- match_chunks returns nothing above 0.30 -> ('', [], 0.0) -> HTTP 200 with 'No relevant thesis was found in the <dept> archive for that query...', no_relevant_thesis=True, archive_current=True, sources=[]. UI shows the 'Search completed · no qualifying archive evidence.' chip (messageNotice.js:31).
- Single opaque token not present in the evidence -> HTTP 200 with UNCLEAR_TOPIC_MESSAGE, sources cleared.
- Generation raises: capacity error -> 60s cooldown + CAPACITY_MESSAGE at 200; anything else -> 502 'The research AI service is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.'
- Generation truncated (finish_reason max_tokens/length) -> the whole answer is DISCARDED, not repaired, and replaced by _grounded_retrieval_fallback listing up to 3 closest studies with their markers. Logged with active_output_ceiling() and gateway_enabled().
- Model reports no evidence (sentinel on its own leading line, or any of 25 phrases anywhere in the text) -> the repair ladder is bypassed entirely and only filter_cited_sources runs. If nothing survives, it degrades to get_no_relevant_message + no_relevant_thesis=True.
- Citation validation fails -> one AI _repair_citations call; if that repair is itself truncated it raises TruncatedGeneration; if still invalid, enforce_citation_coverage is tried but is a no-op unless every source is a chunk of ONE paper; if still invalid, _grounded_retrieval_fallback. Any exception in the ladder also lands on the fallback.
- save_chat_exchange raises (any reason, including the SECURITY DEFINER ownership/department check) -> caught, logged at warning, response returned with history_saved=False. Frontend fires toast.warning('Answer received, but chat history was not saved').
- Client aborts (Stop button, prompt edit, session switch, newConversation, unmount) -> AbortController.abort with a reason string; isCancelledRequest short-circuits the catch so no error bar appears. stopWaiting drops the pending user bubble via dropPendingPrompt and sets chatError {title:'Response stopped'}. Server-side, _client_gone skips persistence only if the disconnect actually reached uvicorn.
- Any other request failure -> the optimistic user bubble is removed (messages.slice(0,-1)), the question is restored into the composer, chatError is set to {title:'IskAI could not answer', message, question} and a toast fires. The bar offers Edit / Retry / Dismiss.
- Backend unreachable in dev -> waitForBackend polls Vite's /__backend-ready 40 times at 350ms and then throws 'The backend did not become ready within 14 seconds.'
- Supabase schema drift -> main._verify_database_contract fails; /health reports database:'unavailable_or_incompatible' (15s cache) and /ready returns 503 (never cached).

### What gets persisted

- chat_messages: one row per exchange, inserted by save_chat_exchange with (session_id, question, answer, sources jsonb, duplication_alert jsonb, kind). `kind` is 'answer' or 'notice', decided by chat_notices.response_kind at write time, and is what the history loader filters on. Sources are stored as the metadata-only public_source dicts — never chunk text.
- chat_sessions: created lazily by save_chat_exchange when p_session_id is null, with title = question[:40] (+'...') and department = the server-resolved department. A partial index on (session_id, created_at desc) WHERE kind='answer' backs the history read (migrations/20260804_chat_message_kind.sql:54-56).
- activity_log: one row per turn via the BackgroundTask — action 'chat_query' with {question_length, sources_cited, duplication_flagged, citation_repaired, department, question_type}, or 'chat_query_blocked' / 'chat_query_budget_exhausted'. Fast paths write a `fast_path` discriminator instead ('model_identity', 'capabilities', 'thanks', 'farewell', 'conversation', 'system_origin', 'system_identity', 'self_platform', 'archive_inventory', 'author_metadata', 'query_translated', 'unclear_topic'). Insert failures are swallowed.
- Rate-limit store (memory:// in dev, Redis in production): slowapi counters keyed by rate_limit_key and ip_rate_limit_key.
- Guest token budget: a single counter at key `guest-token-budget:YYYY-MM-DD` in the same store, incremented atomically, expiring just after the UTC day. Not written for authenticated turns.
- In-process only, never persisted: _ROLE_CACHE (60s, 2048 entries), _FEATURES_CACHE (60s), turnstile._verified_guests (TTL turnstile_guest_ttl_seconds), gemini_pool._cooldowns and _clients, chat_notices._CAPACITY_STATE, main._CONTRACT_CACHE (15s).
- Browser: sessionStorage 'iskai_guest_rate_id' (guest id) and 'iskai_guest_gate_ok' (Turnstile pass). React Query caches ['sessions'], ['public-settings'], ['departments']; AuthContext calls queryClient.clear() on every identity change.
- Nothing writes to papers, chunks or paper_index_versions on this path — chat is read-only against the index.

### Surprises

- _answer_reports_no_evidence (chat.py:1297-1313) substring-matches 25 phrases anywhere in the answer — including 'does not mention', 'does not provide', 'does not address'. A perfectly grounded multi-paper answer containing a sentence like 'the study does not mention sample size [3]' is classified as reporting no evidence, which at chat.py:2813 SKIPS validate_citations and the entire repair ladder. It only degrades to the generic notice if no source survives filtering, so the answer ships with citation validation silently disabled.
- enforce_citation_coverage — the 'deterministic structural repair' rung — returns the answer UNCHANGED whenever the context spans more than one paper (citations.py:230-231). The ordinary grounded path routinely spans 2-5 papers, so on the common path that rung is a no-op and a failed AI repair goes straight to the grounded fallback. It only does real work on the overview and exact-paper paths.
- Rate limiting does not key on the user unless SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET is configured (rate_limiting.py:14-27), and api.js sends Authorization OR X-Guest-ID but never both (api.js:83-88). Without that secret an authenticated request has no guest id either, so it falls through to `ip:{address}` — every signed-in user behind one campus NAT shares a single 30/minute chat bucket.
- get_user_role has a 60s cache, but get_user_scope — the read that actually enforces the department boundary on every RAG operation — has none (auth.py:76-102). _ensure_approved_account is a third uncached profiles read. Three reads of one table per turn, with the cache on the least security-critical of them.
- Session ownership is verified twice per turn: _ensure_session_owner (chat.py:2117) and again inside _load_chat_history (chat.py:1491-1495).
- The exact-paper / 'number 2' follow-up path does NO vector search. get_paper_overview_context (retriever.py:549) pulls up to 200 chunks by chunk_index and ranks them with rank_paper_chunks, a hand-tuned lexical scorer carrying literal +20 bonuses for the strings 'objectives of the study', 'scope and delimitation' and '3.2 methods' (retriever.py:541-546). It also skips the paper_index_versions provenance predicates that match_chunks enforces, and ignores thesis_category_filter entirely.
- The 0.85 duplication guard runs on EVERY grounded retrieval, against the user's question embedding (chat.py:1975-1988). A normal research question whose wording happens to sit within 0.85 cosine of an archived chunk renders the orange 'Potential topic duplication' banner on an ordinary chat answer and triggers a second concurrent Gemini call for the summary.
- Citation numbers are assigned BEFORE LongContextReorder (retriever.py:868-869), so the markers in the prompt are deliberately out of order — [1] at the top, [2] at the bottom, [3] second from top. sources[] keeps rank order; the reorder only affects the text the model reads.
- ChatResponse(kind='notice') at chat.py:2670 is dead. _chat_impl overwrites `.kind` unconditionally at chat.py:2094 with response_kind(response), which reaches the same answer by matching UNCLEAR_TOPIC_MESSAGE.
- The same SYSTEM_ORIGIN_MESSAGE is returned two ways with different presentation: the direct path sets notice_type='conversation' (chat.py:2200-2205) so the UI renders it quietly, while the ambiguous-context path omits it (chat.py:2376-2380) so the identical text renders with a 'System message · not a research answer.' chip.
- chat_notices.notice_type() — used only by the session-reload path in sessions.py:97-99 — does not know _model_response()'s text, which interpolates settings.gemini_chat_model (chat.py:854-860). So 'what model do you use' is quiet live (notice_type is sent on the wire) and grows a 'System message' chip after a page reload.
- _author_lookup_response with no match returns 'I could not verify {name} as an author…' (chat.py:1248-1251). response_kind classifies it KIND_ANSWER because GROUNDED_FALLBACK_PREFIX is the longer 'I could not verify a direct answer'. It is a system message stored as a research answer and replayed to the model as conversational context on the next turn — exactly the failure the whole kind column exists to prevent.
- filter_cited_sources drops every block the model did not cite, so the 'Evidence sources' grid regularly shows fewer than the 5 passages retrieval actually sent, ordered by citation number rather than similarity. A reader counting cards is not seeing the retrieval width.
- normalizePercent (utils.js:46-51) multiplies anything in (0,1] by 100, but public_source already returns similarity as round(sim*100,2) (retriever.py:63). The helper is not idempotent — a stored 0.9 (meaning 0.9%) would render as 90.00%. Unreachable through match_chunks only because of the 0.30 floor.
- Pressing Stop aborts the browser fetch but the server finishes the generation regardless. Persistence is skipped only if the disconnect actually reaches uvicorn (_client_gone, chat.py:1556-1599); behind any response-buffering proxy the withdrawn turn IS written and reappears on the next reload. The docstring admits TestClient answers True spuriously, which is why the tests drive _chat_impl directly.
- retrieval_candidate_pool defaults to 15 while retrieval_match_count is 5, so the documented 'pure cosine order' path at retriever.py:810-813 is never taken in a default deployment — the rerank and per-paper cap are always active despite the paper's pipeline being described in cosine terms.
- _is_simple_conversation treats an English greeting followed by exactly ONE word as a pleasantry (chat.py:711-714). 'hello ocr' is answered with a welcome message and never retrieves; 'hello machine learning' (two words) falls through to RAG. The restriction to English greetings is deliberate — extending it to Filipino stems would swallow 'magandang araw ocr'.
- edit_from_turn is a position in the server's FULL ordered chat_messages transcript (rows, notices included) while the client computes it by counting user bubbles (transcript.js:70-75). They agree only because loadSession emits exactly one user bubble per stored row (Chat.jsx:1053-1068). _MAX_TRANSCRIPT_ROWS caps both the read and the delete at 500, so a longer session still truncates from a different boundary than it reads from — the PostgREST db-max-rows hazard moved from 1000 to 500 rather than removed.
- require_chat_access deliberately does NOT call _require_privileged_mfa (auth.py:384-388), unlike require_upload_access and require_novelty_access. An admin on an aal1 session can chat and read the archive while every privileged endpoint refuses them.
- The 401 branch of the axios interceptor replays ANY method, including POST /chat, after one silent token refresh (api.js:133-138). /chat carries no Idempotency-Key. This is currently safe only because every 401 the route can raise (get_optional_user, and the guest-with-session_id check at chat.py:2117) fires before any model call — a future 401 raised after generation would double-charge and double-persist.
- search_chunks skips any chunk whose paper is missing from paper_lookup in both the context loop and the sources loop (retriever.py:892, 912), so a dangling paper_id silently leaves a gap in the citation numbering rather than renumbering — validate_citations still passes because the allowed set is derived from sources.
- The guest budget's pre-retrieval gate is a READ (is_exhausted), not a charge (chat.py:2218). Between it and the real charge at chat.py:2676 a guest turn can still make a follow-up-rewrite call and a translation call, each booked separately through _charge_guest_extra — and the citation repair, multi-paper repair and duplication summary are booked AFTER the fact and can never refuse.

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 15 correction(s), 6 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** normalize_citation_markers at services/citations.py:109-115; validate_citations at services/citations.py:244-263; filter_cited_sources at services/citations.py:127-134; enforce_citation_coverage's multi-paper bail-out at citations.py:230-231 (steps 57, 58, 59 and the 'deterministic structural repair is a no-op' surprise).
  **Actual:** services/citations.py is 165 lines, so 230-231 and 244-263 do not exist. Every citations.py anchor in the trace is offset by exactly +98 lines. Actual: normalize_citation_markers rag-thesis-backend/services/citations.py:11-17, filter_cited_sources :29-36, enforce_citation_coverage :93-143 with the `if len(paper_ids) != 1 or not next(iter(paper_ids)): return repaired` gate at :132-133, validate_citations :146-165. The substance of all four claims is correct. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/citations.py:11,29,132,146`)
- **Wrong:** is_truncated reads finish_reason at services/llm_output.py:303-324; coerce_text flattens multi-part content at services/llm_output.py:336-348 (steps 55, 56).

- **Wrong:** p_embedding_model/p_embedding_dimensions come from index_provenance.retrieval_provenance_params() at services/index_provenance.py:80-85 (step 38).

- **Wrong:** the citation repair, multi-paper repair and duplication summary are booked AFTER the fact and can never refuse (final surprises entry).

It cannot refuse because the returned BudgetDecision is discarded, not because it is booked late. The multi-paper repair (:2783) and citation repair (:2843) are genuinely booked after the answer exists. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2713-2717`)
- **Wrong:** Profile status != 'approved' -> 403 ... isRetryableFailure() is false for 4xx so the bar's Retry is misleading here (it is shown unconditionally, Chat.jsx:1362).
  **Actual:** Chat.jsx never imports or calls isRetryableFailure — it is defined at src/lib/apiErrors.js:38, re-exported from api.js:450, and used only in Novelty.jsx:471. The unconditional Retry button is at Chat.jsx:1370 (Chat.jsx:1362 is the Edit button's onClick). The observation that Retry is offered for non-retryable 4xx holds; the function reference does not. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1370`)
- **Wrong:** enforce_citation_coverage returns the answer UNCHANGED whenever the context spans more than one paper.
  **Actual:** It returns `repaired`, i.e. normalize_citation_markers(answer) (citations.py:127 then :133) — grouped markers are still expanded. Effectively a no-op only because chat.py:2844 already normalized the input. Worth stating precisely since the trace uses the word 'UNCHANGED' as load-bearing. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/citations.py:127,133`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- ChatRequest field validation is absent from the trace entirely. models.py:20-44 enforces question 1-4000 chars, session_id <=64, thesis_category_filter pattern ^(student|faculty)$, edit_from_turn ge=0, guest_history max_length=5 (each item <=4000), guest_source_ids max_length=10 (each <=64), conversation_replies max_length=30 (each <=500). A 4001-char question is a 422 that no step accounts for, and `model_config = ConfigDict(extra='ignore')` (models.py:18) silently drops legacy match_count/match_threshold so an old client cannot influence retrieval policy. The trace's only nod to this is the 500-char cap inside the withinReplyLimit note.
- get_user_scope raises two 403s the failure list omits: 'This account is not approved for application access.' when the profile row exists but status != 'approved' (dependencies/auth.py:83-86), and 'A valid user profile is required for department-scoped access.' when no row comes back at all (auth.py:99-102). On a signed-in turn the second profiles read can therefore 403 with a different string than _ensure_approved_account's.
- A database-level CHECK constraint is a second enforcement layer for `kind` beyond the RPC's p_kind validation: `chat_messages_kind_check check (kind in ('answer','notice'))` at rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:613-616. Relatedly, the RLS policies on both chat tables are explicitly dropped (supabase_setup.sql:1205-1206), which is why save_chat_exchange's SECURITY DEFINER ownership+department check is the only boundary on the write.
- In the happy citation branch, _missing_referenced_papers(answer, sources, plural_paper_ids) runs at chat.py:2835 BEFORE validate_citations at :2836 and can independently set valid=False and append 'missing referenced papers: ...'. The trace jumps straight to validate_citations. It is a no-op on this path only because plural_paper_ids is [] (chat.py:2772).
- services/activity.py:19-29 makes an EXTRA uncached `profiles` read whenever the detail dict has no 'department' key. The grounded path supplies it (chat.py:2895) and the archive-inventory path does (chat.py:2308), so it never fires there — but every conversational fast path's detail (model_identity, capabilities, thanks/farewell, conversation, system_origin, system_identity, self_platform, author_metadata, unclear_topic, query_translated) omits department, so each of those turns makes a fourth profiles read in the BackgroundTask. Worth noting since the trace enumerates each fast path's detail payload.
- _conversation_response (chat.py:862-871) is not a plain varied greeting: it first tests _is_identity_question(question) and returns chat_notices.IDENTITY_MESSAGE instead, so 'hi, who are you' inside the _is_simple_conversation fast path yields the identity text, not a greeting variant. Step 21 describes this path as greeting/identity sets without the branch.

## 3. Follow-up referencing an earlier numbered source, ordinal, or author

<a id="3-followup"></a>

Flow key: `followup`

### Overview

Follow-up resolution in IskAI chat: how a turn like "tell me about number 2", "what are the objectives of the second thesis", "what about Enoy?", or the Ilocano "kayat ko makita dyay specific objectives da" is bound to a paper from an earlier answer, retrieved, generated, validated and persisted.

The machinery is NOT a single resolver. It is a fixed if/elif ladder in `rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2481-2554`, fed by one server-verified list called `reference_sources`, and preceded by two independent fast paths (author metadata at chat.py:2422, archive-listing continuation at chat.py:2283) that can each swallow the turn before the ladder is reached.

`reference_sources` is the load-bearing object. For an authenticated session it is rebuilt every turn from the database: the newest five ANSWER-kind rows of the session (chat.py:1468-1502), the most recent of those that carries a non-empty `sources` array (chat.py:2244-2247), deduplicated to paper ids (chat.py:2248-2250), then re-fetched through `find_papers_by_ids` (retriever.py:185) under the enforced department and `ingestion_status='ready'`. For a guest it comes from `req.guest_source_ids`, which the browser computes as the ids of the last AI bubble that had sources (Chat.jsx:1127-1136). The client's own numbering is never trusted; only the ORDER of ids survives.

Resolution order, once `reference_sources` exists: explicit quoted title -> unique title fragment -> whole-question "number N"/"the second" -> inline "…of number 2" -> plural "their/both/them" -> generic ambiguous follow-up (English + Filipino/Ilocano) -> Gemini rewrite. Numbered and ordinal references are resolved by INDEXING `reference_sources`, never by asking the model. The first four of those produce a `referenced_paper_id`, which makes retrieval bypass pgvector entirely and read that paper's own chunk rows directly (`get_paper_overview_context`, retriever.py:549).

Author lookups are answered deterministically with no model call at all (chat.py:2382-2420), and run BEFORE the numbered ladder, so a person name always wins over a positional reference.

Failure is mostly silent degradation: an unresolvable positional reference is not reported to the user, it is simply embedded verbatim and answered by ordinary semantic search, which usually yields "No relevant thesis was found in the CCSICT archive for that query."

**Trigger:** A user (guest or authenticated) submits a chat message that contains a reference resolvable only from the conversation — a positional reference to a source shown in an earlier answer ("number 2", "#3", "the second thesis", "what are the objectives of number 2"), or a bare person name ("what about Enoy?", "who is Kurt Robin Enoy", "sino si Carlo Gallardo"). Entry is `send()` at rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1096, reached by pressing Send/Enter in the composer, by the "Ask a follow-up" menu item (Chat.jsx:687, which only focuses the input — Chat.jsx:1024), by "Redo response" (Chat.jsx:1001), or by editing an earlier prompt (Chat.jsx:975).

**Actors, in order:** Browser React transcript state (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:882 `messages`) -> rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/chat/transcript.js (branchBeforePrompt / dropPendingPrompt) -> rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:167 chatQuery (axios, JWT or X-Guest-ID) -> Vite proxy (vite.config.js) in dev / nginx in prod -> FastAPI route rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:1985 `chat` (slowapi IP + user rate limits) -> services/turnstile.ensure_guest_chat_verification (chat.py:1993) -> dependencies/auth.require_chat_access (auth.py:398) and resolve_effective_department (auth.py:105) -> routers/chat.py:2097 `_chat_impl_unstamped` — the pipeline body -> services/guards (prohibited_reason, is_ambiguous_followup, fallback_standalone_question) -> Supabase PostgREST: chat_sessions, chat_messages, papers, programs, specializations, chunks -> services/retriever (find_papers_by_ids, find_papers_by_author, find_papers_by_title, find_papers_by_title_fragment, get_paper_overview_context, rank_paper_chunks, search_chunks) -> services/query_translation + services/question_types -> services/prompts (followup_rewrite_prompt, exact_paper_prompt, overview_prompt, exact_papers_prompt, grounded_prompt) -> services/gemini_pool -> ChatGoogleGenerativeAI / OpenAI-compatible gateway (Gemini) -> services/citations (validate_citations, filter_cited_sources, enforce_citation_coverage) -> services/chat_notices (response_kind, capacity cooldown) -> services/activity.log_activity -> activity_log table (BackgroundTasks) -> Postgres RPC public.save_chat_exchange (supabase_setup.sql:626) -> Browser again: AiBubble / SourceCard render (Chat.jsx:726, Chat.jsx:298)

**Latency:** A resolved positional or ordinal follow-up is the CHEAPEST research turn in the system: it makes zero embedding calls and zero pgvector calls, and exactly one Gemini generation. The work is 2-4 PostgREST round trips (session ownership, history page, `find_papers_by_ids` + optional program/specialization labels), then one `papers` row read and one `chunks` read (up to 200 rows when a question is supplied for ranking, 7 when it is an overview), then generation. An author lookup is cheaper still: 1-2 `papers` ILIKE queries and NO model call whatsoever, so it returns in tens of milliseconds. It is placed above the capacity cooldown check precisely so it keeps working while Gemini is exhausted. The expensive shapes are the ones that FAIL to resolve. An unresolved follow-up with no reference sources costs a Gemini rewrite (`_rewrite_followup`), then an embedding, then `match_chunks`, then generation — four network hops before the first token. A non-English question adds a fifth (translation). A plural follow-up fans out one `get_paper_overview_context` per pinned paper concurrently via `asyncio.gather` (chat.py:1919) with no cap, so with 10 remembered papers that is 10 concurrent paper reads plus 10 chunk reads, and a context of up to 50 blocks feeding one very large prompt. Every Supabase call is wrapped in `retry_transient` (3 attempts, 0.25s base exponential backoff, transient errors only), so a flaky lookup adds up to ~0.75s of sleep on a worker thread. All of them are dispatched through `asyncio.to_thread`, so they do not block the event loop. Retrieval and the duplication check run concurrently (`asyncio.gather`, chat.py:1993-ish in `_retrieve_evidence`), as do generation and the duplication summary (`_invoke_generation`, chat.py:1970). The repair ladder is the worst tail: an invalid plural answer can cost generation + coverage repair + citation repair = three sequential Gemini calls before the reply is served, and only the first is charged against the guest allowance.

| 38 | service | MULTILINGUAL VETO + SIGNALS. `_names_its_own_subject` (guards.py:404) vetoes anything carrying an ACRONYM (case-sensitive `\b[A-Z][A-Za-z0-9]*[A-Z0-9]\b` — OCR/YOLO/SECURE/FindMe), a genitive (ng/ni/nina/si/kay/kadagiti), a topical marker (tungkol/maipapan/para sa), a year, a scope word (dito/ditoy/archive/aklatan) or 'pinamagatang/titled'. Only then do the weaker local signals count: `_FIL_GOVERNED_PRONOUN` (an aspect/document noun governing nito/niya/nila/kanila, suppressed by a coordinator), `_FIL_BARE_DOCUMENT_REFERENCE` (ang\|yung\|ti\|dyay\|jay + tesis with nothing qualifying it), `_FIL_ENCLITIC_POSSESSOR` (aspect noun + detached `na`/`da` at the right edge — the positional discriminator that separates turn-2 'specific objectives da' = possessor from turn-1 'objectives da carlo gallardo' = plural article), `_FIL_FUSED_POSSESSOR` (naganna, nagbanaganda), `_FIL_DANGLING_DEMONSTRATIVE` ('ano yun', <=5 words), and `_FIL_BARE_ASPECT` (aspect noun at the right edge, <=6 words, needs a local function word, blocked by a superlative). Last resort: <=5 words AND ends with '?'. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/guards.py:555` |
| 39 | service | Inside the generic branch: with >=2 reference sources it pins ALL of them (and, if `_resolve_referenced_thesis` recognises a 'what is it about' shape, switches to `_overview_question_for_sources`). With 0-1 it tries `_resolve_referenced_thesis` (overview of source[0]), then `_resolve_specific_paper_followup` (prefix 'Regarding the archived thesis titled "X" by Y: <question>'), and only with NO reference sources at all does it pay for `_rewrite_followup`. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2505` |
| 40 | external | `_rewrite_followup` sends `prompts.followup_rewrite_prompt` (prior 5 questions + up to 5 source title/author labels, all inside `<untrusted_turns>` fences) through `gemini_pool.arun`. The reply is accepted only if 3-1000 chars, single-line, and not prefixed 'answer:'/'response:'; any exception or rejection falls back to the deterministic `guards.fallback_standalone_question`, which is literally 'Previous research question: <prev> Follow-up: <q>' truncated to 4000 chars. For a guest this call is charged first and CAN still refuse the turn. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:1794` |
| 41 | service | The rewritten/resolved question is re-run through `prohibited_reason`; a hit returns `REFUSAL_MESSAGE`. | 42 | service | Cross-lingual retrieval: `query_translation.looks_non_english` (a deterministic marker-token test, no model call) decides whether to spend a Gemini call turning the resolved question into an English search query.
