On reload, `sessions.get_session_messages` recomputes `notice_type` from the stored text (sessions.py:97-99), and `chat_notices.notice_type` does not recognise the dynamic 'IskAI uses gemini-3.6-flash...' string — so `messageKind='notice'` with no `notice_type` yields the amber chip. The comment at tests/test_chat_endpoint_flows.py:1101-1103 asserts the opposite ('persistence stores it as an answer') and is stale relative to the `notice_type` branch added to `response_kind`.
- **`kind='notice'` passed at chat.py:2670 is dead code.** `_chat_impl` overwrites `response.kind` unconditionally at line 2094. It is the only return statement in the file that sets `kind` explicitly, which makes it read as load-bearing.
- **The grounded fallback shows three contradictory signals at once.** It is classified a notice (prefix branch, chat_notices.py:267) so it gets 'System message · not a research answer.'; it keeps `archive_current=True` so it also shows 'Searched the current indexed archive'; and it renders full source cards. A reader sees a system-message warning on top of what looks like a cited research answer.
- **The two most diagnostic outcomes are the two that log nothing.** The no-relevant-thesis return (chat.py:2644-2657) and all three capacity returns (2428, 2632, 2736) add no `log_activity` task, while all twelve fast paths do. Analytics can count how many people said hello but not how often the archive came up empty or the provider ran out.
- **`_capacity_response` and `REFUSAL_MESSAGE` leave `archive_current=False`**, so the 'Response details' modal labels a live capacity apology 'Saved response snapshot' (responseActions.js:68) even though it was produced seconds ago.
- **A greeting plus one word bypasses the injection guard.** `_is_simple_conversation` returns True for any English greeting followed by exactly one token (chat.py:717-718), and it runs at line 2175 — 32 lines before `prohibited_reason` at 2207. 'hello jailbreak' is answered with the welcome message and never tested for injection.
- **'help', 'tulong', 'patulong' and 'pahelp' are capability questions, not help requests.** They are exact members of `_CAPABILITY_QUESTIONS_ALL` (chat.py:100, 296) and the capability check runs before the guard — even though `\bpatulong\b` is itself a `_DIRECTED_AT_ASSISTANT` marker (guards.py:221). Bare 'patulong' therefore gets the capabilities blurb; 'patulong sa chapter 1 ko' falls through and is refused.
- **Chat.jsx always offers Retry.** The banner at Chat.jsx:1370 renders the Retry button unconditionally, including for 403 Turnstile refusals and 401s. `isRetryableFailure` exists in lib/apiErrors.js:38-41 precisely to prevent this, with a comment explaining that offering Retry against a 4xx 'advertises a recovery that cannot happen' — and it is wired into exactly one screen, Novelty.jsx:471.
- **The load-test classifier reads latency, not bodies.** `summarize_chat_load.py:72` calls any 200 under 100 ms a capacity notice. A capacity notice served after a real round trip — the first request after the cooldown expires, which re-contacts the provider and gets another 429 — lands in `ambiguous_fast` or, if slow enough, is counted as an answer.
- **`_is_courtesy_message`'s 40-character cap is measured on the as-typed reading only** (`candidates[0]`, chat.py:786), not on the politeness-stripped or addressee-stripped ones. 'maraming salamat po sa inyong lahat' is 35 chars and passes; a slightly longer polite farewell falls through to full RAG retrieval and will usually earn a no-evidence result.
- **`_GREETINGS_BY_LENGTH` is a tuple, not a set, for a measured reason.** Iterating the set made `hi there friend` resolve as a greeting under PYTHONHASHSEED 0/2/7 and as a research question under 1/3 — the same user got a different answer to the same greeting after a restart (chat.py:344-353). The local Filipino stems overlap the same way, which would have made it routine rather than rare.
- **`GROUNDED_FALLBACK_PREFIX` begins with 'I could not verify a direct answer', and 'could not verify' is the first entry in `_answer_reports_no_evidence`'s phrase list.** Both no-evidence signals are therefore computed exactly once at chat.py:2762-2763, before any substitution, specifically so a fallback substituted later is not re-detected and mis-flagged `no_relevant_thesis`. The ordering is the only thing preventing it.
- **The no-evidence sentinel is flagged only when it leads the reply on its own line, but stripped everywhere.** `<retrieved_context>` is untrusted, so a manuscript chunk containing the literal `ARCHIVE_COVERAGE_INSUFFICIENT` would otherwise be an injection primitive that flips the flag on an unrelated question (prompts.py:72-78). The sentinel token is also constrained to contain no `[n]` or `[n, n` substring so it cannot parse as a citation marker.
- **A gateway hop is cooled for 60 seconds by ANY failure, not just a 429.** `arun` calls `_mark_exhausted(api_key)` whenever `_should_continue` returns True, and for the gateway that is unconditional (gemini_pool.py:246-251, 264-265). One malformed-prompt rejection at the gateway takes it out of the chain for a minute.

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 7 correction(s), 6 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** Step 3 places the two slowapi limiters BEFORE step 4 (`require_chat_access`): "slowapi applies two limiters on the route ... Exceeding either raises RateLimitExceeded", listed as the first server-side gate.
  **Actual:** Inverted. `SlowAPIMiddleware` explicitly skips any route carrying a decorator — `_should_exempt` returns True when `name in limiter._route_limits` ("there is a decorator for this route we let the decorator handle it"), so the middleware calls `call_next` without checking. The actual check runs inside the decorator's `async_wrapper` body (`self._check_request_limit(request, func, False)`), which FastAPI invokes only after it has resolved the endpoint signature — including `user: OptionalUser = Depends(require_chat_access)`. So an invalid JWT (401), an unapproved profile (403), a profiles-read failure (503) and a role without the `chat` feature (403) are all decided BEFORE the rate limit is consulted. Steps 4 and 5 precede step 3, not follow it. (`rag-thesis-backend/.venv/Lib/site-packages/slowapi/middleware.py:110-111, 127-128; rag-thesis-backend/.venv/Lib/site-packages/slowapi/extension.py:723-735; rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:75, 2008-2014`)
- **Wrong:** Step 38: "A persistence failure is swallowed into `history_saved = False` (never fails the turn)."
  **Actual:** False for HTTPException. The persistence block re-raises it: `except HTTPException: raise` sits ahead of the broad handler. The block also runs `_truncate_session_from_turn` first on a prompt edit, and that calls `_ensure_session_owner`, which raises 404 `Session not found` or 409 `This conversation belongs to a different department...`; `identifier_not_found` additionally converts a bad uuid cast into a 404. If the session is deleted or moved between the pre-flight check at 2118-2123 and this block, a completed and already-paid-for answer is discarded and the caller gets a 4xx instead. Only non-HTTPException failures degrade to `history_saved=False`. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2042-2065, 1511-1527, 1491-1495`)
- **Wrong:** Step 23: the post-rewrite refusal re-tests "the rewritten wording" produced by `_rewrite_followup`, and logs `after_rewrite: True`.
  **Actual:** Scope is wider than stated. `prohibited_reason(effective_question)` sits at the tail of the whole `elif not explicit_title and is_ambiguous_followup(...)` block, so it re-tests whatever that block produced — including `_overview_question_for_sources(reference_sources)` (2515), `_resolve_referenced_thesis(...)` (2518) and `_resolve_specific_paper_followup(...)` (2524), none of which call Gemini. The `after_rewrite: True` activity label is therefore emitted on turns where no rewrite ever happened. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2505-2553, 2546-2553`)
- **Wrong:** Steps 12-13, 35: `_CAPABILITY_QUESTIONS_ALL` is "10 English + 27 Filipino/Ilocano"; `_THANKS_ALL` is "13 English + 24 local"; `_FAREWELLS_ALL` is "9 + 16"; `_answer_reports_no_evidence` matches "~28 substrings".
  **Actual:** Four of the five counts are wrong. Capability: 10 English + 35 local (not 27). Thanks: 12 English (not 13) + 31 local (not 24). Farewells: 9 English (correct) + 23 local (not 16). The no-evidence phrase list holds 32 substrings, not ~28. `_MODEL_QUESTIONS` (8) is correct. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:97-101, 286-303, 105-109, 304-314, 110-113, 320-328, 1300-1312`)
- **Wrong:** Step 3 describes the two limiters as "per-IP (`ip_rate_limit_key`) and per-key".
  **Actual:** "per-key" reads as an API-key limit; it is not. The undecorated second limiter uses the limiter's default `key_func=rate_limit_key`, which resolves to `user:<sub>` from a locally HS256-verified JWT when `SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET` is set, else `guest:<sha256(ip:guest_id)[:32]>` for a well-formed 16-128 char alphanumeric `X-Guest-ID`, else `ip:<addr>`. The configured values are 30/minute per caller and 300/minute per IP, over a limiter that also carries a 120/minute default. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/rate_limiting.py:12-33, 41-45; rag-thesis-backend/config.py:94-95`)
- **Wrong:** failureModes, guest budget: "Three distinct stages log it: `pre_retrieval`, `followup_rewrite`, `query_translation`, `pre_generation`."
  **Actual:** Self-contradictory — it says three and lists four. Four is correct; all four `chat_query_budget_exhausted` rows exist with those `stage` values. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2221, 2538, 2582, 2688`)
- **Wrong:** Trigger: "`Chat.jsx::send` (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1115)".
  **Actual:** 1115 is the optimistic-bubble `setMessages` line, not the function. `send` is declared at 1096. Minor, but the anchor does not point at what the sentence names. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1096, 1115`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- **A 422 request-validation refusal is missing entirely from the failure table.** `ChatRequest.question` is `Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=4000)` (rag-thesis-backend/models.py:20) and the composer textarea carries no `maxLength` — the only `maxLength` in Chat.jsx is 120 on the rename input (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Chat.jsx:1455; the composer is passed only `textareaRef` at 1436). A pasted over-length question is rejected by FastAPI before any dependency runs, with `detail` as a LIST, which `apiErrorMessage` formats into `body.question: String should have at most 4000 characters` (rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/apiErrors.js:12-14) inside the same 'IskAI could not answer' banner — and, per the trace's own surprise, with an unconditional Retry that will fail identically.
- **`BodySizeLimitMiddleware` is the outermost non-CORS gate and is omitted.** Registered inside CORS but outside everything else precisely so it runs before authentication, it answers a raw 413 `Request body exceeds the N MB limit` from the ASGI layer, never as a ChatResponse (rag-thesis-backend/main.py:174-180; rag-thesis-backend/services/request_limits.py:80-92, 120-125). For /chat the ceiling is the `max_upload_mb`-derived default (request_limits.py:66), so it is practically unreachable — but the trace claims to enumerate every non-answer outcome from the route surface.
- **Step 19 omits half the history filter.** Loaded history is filtered by `prohibited_reason(message['question'])` as well as `_is_stored_non_answer(message['answer'])`, and guest `guest_history` entries are filtered by `prohibited_reason` alone. A prior turn that was refused is dropped from model context, not just notices (rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2236-2240, 2257-2262).
- **The latency claim for an authenticated capacity notice understates the round trips.** Before `resolve_effective_department` (60 s role cache) and `_ensure_session_owner`, `get_optional_user` performs an UNCACHED `sb.auth.get_user(token)` and `_ensure_approved_account` performs an UNCACHED `profiles` select on every single request (rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:243-251, 141-155). That is two network calls before the trace's "2-3 Supabase round trips" even start.
- **Step 7's 403 is unreachable for a guest.** `resolve_effective_department(None, requested)` returns `settings.thesis_evaluation_department` at the first line, before `requested` is compared to anything — so a guest supplying `department_filter` is silently ignored rather than refused (rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:105-108).
- **`_ARCHIVE_INVENTORY_LIMIT`-paged inventory is one more non-answer shape the trace flattens.** Step 20 covers the empty-archive case but not that `list_archive_papers` is called with a `listing_cursor` offset derived from prior history, so a continuation question past the last page returns an inventory response with `no_relevant_thesis` driven by `inventory_total` rather than by what was actually shown (rag-thesis-backend/routers/chat.py:2282-2323).

## 5. Single PDF submitted through the upload wizard

<a id="5-upload-submit"></a>

Flow key: `upload-submit`

### Overview

Single-PDF upload through the wizard: React wizard (Dropzone → MetadataForm → ReviewPanel) → axios multipart POST /upload/paper with Idempotency-Key → ASGI body ceiling → slowapi → require_upload_access (Supabase Auth + profiles + role_features) → resolve_effective_department → _validate_metadata → services/catalog.resolve_academic_selection → reserve_upload_job (row in 'staging') → Supabase Storage private `pdfs` bucket → queue_upload_job ('queued') → 202 UploadAccepted → browser writes sessionStorage and polls GET /upload/status/{job_id} every 1.5 s → a separate worker process claims the job under a lease, runs download → SHA-256 → ClamAV → PyMuPDF/OCR extract → 800/100-token chunk → 768-d Gemini embed → 85% duplication screen → commit_upload_ingestion (papers + paper_index_versions + chunks + job row, one transaction) → next poll returns completed and the UI renders IngestOutcome.

**Trigger:** An authenticated user whose role-feature matrix grants `upload` navigates to /upload, drops a PDF on the Dropzone, confirms the metadata, and presses "Ingest into archive" (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:560-562 → submit() at Upload.jsx:374).

**Actors, in order:** Browser / React 19 SPA (src/pages/Upload.jsx, useReducer state in src/pages/upload/uploadState.js) -> axios instance with request/response interceptors (src/api.js:8-158) -> Vite dev proxy `spaAwareApiProxy` for the /upload prefix (vite.config.js:39-48, 94) — dev only -> FastAPI ASGI stack: CORSMiddleware → BodySizeLimitMiddleware → security_headers → GZipMiddleware → SlowAPIMiddleware (main.py:151-198) -> routers/upload.py (POST /upload/paper, GET /upload/status/{job_id}, POST /upload/jobs/{id}/cancel) -> dependencies/auth.py (Supabase Auth get_user, profiles reads, role cache, role_features cache) -> services/catalog.py (departments/programs/specializations validation) -> Supabase Storage, private `pdfs` bucket -> PostgreSQL RPCs: reserve_upload_job, queue_upload_job, claim_upload_job, heartbeat_upload_job_control, commit_upload_ingestion → commit_paper_ingestion, fail_upload_job, schedule_upload_retry, request_upload_cancellation, expire_upload_jobs -> workers/ingestion_worker.py — a separate OS process with a LeaseHeartbeat thread -> services/ingestion.py pipeline + malware.py (ClamAV INSTREAM), document_processor.py (PyMuPDF + tesserocr), chunker.py (tiktoken cl100k_base), embedder.py (Gemini embeddings), novelty.py (match_chunks screen) -> Postgres triggers: record_upload_job_event, a_papers_hydrate_academic_classification, papers_validate_academic_classification

**Latency:** The HTTP request returns as soon as the PDF is staged and queued; the ingestion itself is fully asynchronous. Inside the 202 path the API pays: 1 Supabase Auth get_user round trip + 1 profiles read (_ensure_approved_account) + 1 profiles read cached 60 s (get_user_role) + 1 system_settings read cached 60 s (role_features) + TWO full resolve_effective_department calls, each doing its own profiles read and, for a superadmin, its own departments read (routers/upload.py:902 and upload.py:664) + up to 3 catalog reads in resolve_academic_selection + the reserve RPC + a storage PUT of up to 25 MB + the queue RPC. PDF parsing, the SHA-256, and the storage upload are all pushed off the event loop with asyncio.to_thread (upload.py:777-785, 826-827) precisely because an `async def` handler would otherwise stall /health and /ready for the whole submission. Axios sets a 180 s instance timeout (api.js:10). Polling costs 4 round trips every 1.5 s per uploader: Supabase Auth get_user, a profiles read, the upload_jobs read, and a separate upload_job_events read for last_event_at (upload.py:1139-1158, 1080-1095). The worker's own cost is dominated by embedding (batches of 64) and by the screen stage, which issues one match_chunks RPC per chunk sequentially — a 200-chunk thesis is 200 serialized database round trips (services/novelty.py:352-362). The lease is 120 s with a 30 s heartbeat, and config.py:244 refuses a configuration where heartbeat*2 >= lease. Retry backoff is 30 s / 120 s / 600 s, raised by any provider Retry-After up to 15 minutes (upload_queue.py:13, 117-123). Jobs stuck in 'staging' are reaped after 15 minutes, terminal jobs expire after 30 days, events after 90.

| 31 | db | Phase 2 commit: queue_upload_job flips 'staging' → status='queued', stage='queued', progress=8, message='Queued for durable processing...', source_stored=true, next_retry_at=now(). Only now does claim_upload_job consider the row (it filters source_stored = true). | `rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1501-1514, supabase_setup.sql:1555-1568` |
| 32 | api | Queue-transition compensation is deliberately asymmetric: if the RPC throws or returns false, the handler RE-READS upload_jobs.status and only deletes the staged object + fails the job when the status is NOT in {queued, processing, retry_wait, completed}. If it already advanced, the except block falls through and the request still returns 202. This is what stops a lost response from deleting a PDF out from under a running worker. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:841-870, upload.py:579-591` |
| 33 | api | Response: HTTP 202 UploadAccepted{job_id, idempotency_key, status:'queued', message:'Upload accepted by the durable worker queue.'}. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:872-877, rag-thesis-backend/models.py:115-119` |
| 34 | frontend | submit() writes sessionStorage['activeUploadJob'] = {jobId, idempotencyKey, title}, sets job = {status: res.status, stage:'download', progress:8, message: res.message}, advances to UPLOAD_STEPS.ingesting and calls startPolling(res.job_id). Any thrown error instead raises toast.error('Upload failed', {description: apiErrorMessage(err)}) and the wizard stays on Review. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:374-395` |
| 35 | frontend | startPolling: a generation counter + jobId guard (isCurrentPoll) makes stale timers no-ops; first tick after 500 ms, then a fixed 1500 ms cadence while the status is non-terminal. A hard 30-minute wall sets pollError 'Status checking paused after 30 minutes. You can resume it safely.' and stops. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:288-353, src/pages/upload/uploadState.js:91-93` |
| 36 | api | Each poll hits GET /upload/status/{job_id}, itself behind require_upload_access, scoped with .eq('owner_id', user.id). It tries the extended column set first and silently retries with the pre-operations legacy set if cancel_requested_at/cancelled_at are missing, then issues a second query against upload_job_events for last_event_at. A 22P02 (malformed uuid) is mapped to 404, any other DB error to 503. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1139-1158, upload.py:1067-1077, upload.py:1080-1095` |
| 37 | api | _job_status_model computes can_cancel server-side: true for staging/queued/retry_wait, or processing when no cancellation is pending. The UI renders the Cancel button straight off that flag. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1117-1136, rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:115-121` |
| 38 | worker | Meanwhile the separate worker process (python -m workers.ingestion_worker) loops at ingestion_poll_seconds (2.0 s default). Before claiming it self-heals: staging rows older than 15 minutes are failed as 'staging_timeout', and processing rows whose lease expired with attempt_count >= max_attempts are failed as 'worker_lease_expired'. It then SELECT ... FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED one eligible job, sets status='processing', stage='download', progress=10, attempt_count+1, lease_owner and lease_expires_at = now() + 120 s. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:291-331, rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1517-1583` |
| 39 | worker | If scanner_status() reports ClamAV 'unavailable' the worker never claims at all — it registers itself 'degraded' and sleeps. With MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled it reports 'disabled' and proceeds. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:299-303, workers/ingestion_worker.py:222-234, rag-thesis-backend/services/malware.py:42-49` |
| 40 | worker | LeaseHeartbeat wraps the job: a daemon thread sends a bare keep-alive every 30 s (carrying no stage/progress/message, so it can never overwrite the pipeline's report) while the pipeline thread reports real stage transitions. Both go through one _rpc_lock so they cannot land out of order, and heartbeat_upload_job_control returns {lease_valid, cancel_requested}. cancel_requested raises CancellationRequested; a lost lease returns False and services/ingestion._require_lease turns that into LeaseLostError. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:60-166, rag-thesis-backend/services/upload_queue.py:46-73, services/ingestion.py:85-87` |
| 41 | worker | Pipeline stage 'download' (progress 12): the staged PDF is downloaded from the private bucket and _validate_staged_pdf re-checks SHA-256 against content_sha256, the %PDF- magic, encryption and the page-count band. Any mismatch is a PermanentIngestionError — no retry. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:101-107, services/ingestion.py:56-82` |
| 42 | worker | 'malware_scan' (16): scan_pdf streams the bytes to ClamAV over zINSTREAM in 64 KiB frames. 'FOUND' → MalwareDetectedIngestionError (permanent, and the worker also upserts a critical operational_alert 'malware:<job_id>' and fires the webhook); a socket/timeout failure → MalwareScannerUnavailable, which carries status_code=503 and is therefore retryable. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:109-117, services/malware.py:19-61, workers/ingestion_worker.py:190-199` |
| 43 | worker | 'extract' (20): extract_pdf_document runs PyMuPDF per page, falls back to tesserocr OCR on image-only pages, injects a FIGURE_PLACEHOLDER on mixed pages, strips repeated headers/footers, redacts PII and removes excluded sections. Empty text → permanent failure. If any scanned page could not be OCR'd and REQUIRE_OCR_FOR_SCANNED_PAGES is on, the job is refused rather than indexed short its pages. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:119-137, services/document_processor.py:258-292` |
| 44 | worker | 'chunk' (40): split_document streams every page into one string (pages joined by a single space, never a newline) and splits at 800 tokens / 100 overlap measured with tiktoken cl100k_base, mapping each chunk back to its page span and section heading. is_noise_chunk drops chunks failing the 15% non-alphanumeric rule; validate_chunk_records renumbers chunk_index and re-verifies the token ceiling. Zero surviving chunks → permanent failure. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:139-153, services/chunker.py:251-291, services/chunker.py:184-212` |
| 45 | worker | 'embed' (58): embed_texts sends batches of 64 through gemini_pool.EMBED with 3-attempt exponential backoff. Count mismatch or any vector whose length ≠ 768 is a permanent failure. Embeddings are never routed through the OpenAI-compatible gateway. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:155-164, services/embedder.py:17-55` |
| 46 | worker | 'screen' (72): screen_new_submission issues ONE match_chunks RPC per chunk (match_count=1, threshold 0.85, department-scoped, with embedding-model/dimension provenance params), aggregates coverage / highest similarity / per-paper concentration and a verdict band, then enriches the top 3 matched papers with title/authors/year via a papers read and stamps screened_at, archive_size, scan_scope='at_upload'. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:166-172, services/novelty.py:347-365, services/novelty.py:126-180` |
| 47 | worker | If every chunk matched at ≥ 0.999 the scan sets exact_duplicate and the job is failed with DuplicateManuscriptIngestionError, whose composed public_error names the archived thesis and its percentages. Anything short of verbatim is advisory and is committed flagged. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:173-183, services/novelty.py:118-123` |
| 48 | db | 'index' (88): commit_upload_ingestion locks the job FOR UPDATE, returns early if already completed, raises if cancel_requested_at is set, re-validates status='processing' + lease_owner + unexpired lease, and requires p_paper->>'id' == p_job_id (the paper id IS the job id). It then calls commit_paper_ingestion, which enforces a non-empty title, a non-empty chunk array, a fully verified provenance fingerprint (768 / document-v1 / token-v1 / cl100k_base / 800 / 100 / verified) and chunk_count == array length, inserts papers (ingestion_status='processing'), one paper_index_versions row, every chunk with its vector, re-counts the inserted chunks, then flips the paper to 'ready' and stamps activated_at. Finally the job row becomes completed / 'done' / 100 with paper_id, chunks, duplication, a 30-day expires_at and a cleared lease. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:236-248, rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1665-1714, supabase_setup.sql:465-570` |
| 49 | db | BEFORE INSERT on papers, a_papers_hydrate_academic_classification re-reads upload_jobs.request_payload by the paper id and overwrites program_id, specialization_id, legacy_track, classification_status and thesis_category from it; papers_validate_academic_classification then re-checks program/specialization ownership and the BSCS/BSIT specialization rule inside the same transaction. | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260819_thesis_category.sql:47-64, migrations/20260725_normalized_academic_catalog.sql:114-141` |
| 50 | worker | Post-commit, outside the transaction: log_activity('paper_upload', …) and refresh_after_ingest(paper_id, department), which re-screens already-archived papers against the new one. Both swallow their own failures so an already-committed ingestion is never undone by audit or screening availability. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:276-300` |
| 51 | frontend | The next poll sees status 'completed': sessionStorage['activeUploadJob'] is cleared, the ['papers'] query is invalidated, toast.success('Thesis indexed!', '<chunks> semantic chunks embedded into the archive.') fires, and if duplication.flagged a second toast.warning('Potential duplication detected') reports highest similarity, coverage, the verdict label and the most similar title. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:314-327` |
| 52 | frontend | isTerminalJob(job) flips the ingesting step from IngestingPanel to IngestOutcome: success renders 'Thesis indexed!' with the chunk count, a 768d / gemini-embedding-001 telemetry strip, the screening panel, four assurance cards and Upload another / View archive. 'Open Novelty Review' is offered only when canScan, because /novelty is gated by the same matrix. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:428, Upload.jsx:565-580, src/components/upload/IngestOutcome.jsx:154-239` |

### Branches

- Superadmin vs everyone else: a superadmin gets a real Department <Select> and may pick any active department (re-validated against `departments`); every other role gets a locked read-only 'Assigned' field and resolve_effective_department 403s any mismatch. (MetadataForm.jsx:263-310, auth.py:113-129)
- Autofill confirm vs cancel: the ConfirmDialog's Confirm runs POST /upload/extract-metadata; Cancel (and backdrop/Escape, which route to onClose) keep the file and leave the form blank. (Upload.jsx:588-606)
- thesis_category 'student' vs 'faculty': student requires a program both client-side and server-side (require_program=True); faculty research may commit with program_id NULL and classification_status 'unclassified', or 'needs_review' for an ambiguous legacy track. (uploadState.js:106-108, catalog.py:84-92)
- Legacy track translation: when no program_id is sent and `track` is one of Data Mining / Web Development / Network Security, the server translates it to a (program, specialization) pair. The current wizard always sends program_id for student theses, so this path is only reachable from older clients or faculty submissions. (catalog.py:68-83)
- Idempotency replay: created=false && status!='staging' returns 202 with the existing job's status and 'This submission is already tracked. Poll its existing job for progress.' created=false && status=='staging' re-uploads the object and re-queues. Same key + different bytes → 409. (upload.py:815-824, supabase_setup.sql:1486-1491)
- Queue-transition failure with the row already advanced: compensation is skipped entirely and the request still returns 202 queued. (upload.py:850-870)
- Page reload mid-ingest: on mount the wizard reads sessionStorage['activeUploadJob'], restores the idempotency key and the title, paints a synthetic {status:'queued', stage:'download', progress:8} job and resumes polling. (Upload.jsx:355-370)
- Cancellation: POST /upload/jobs/{id}/cancel → request_upload_cancellation returns outcome 'cancelled' (pre-lease) or 'cancellation_requested' (mid-flight). The worker honours it only at a stage checkpoint, where LeaseHeartbeat.update raises CancellationRequested and finalize_upload_cancellation runs; commit_upload_ingestion also refuses to commit over a pending cancellation. (upload.py:1212-1258, ingestion_worker.py:124-147, supabase_setup.sql:1688-1692)
- Retry vs permanent failure: is_retryable_ingestion_error walks the __cause__/__context__ chain for status 408/429/5xx or a transient network error, and never retries PermanentIngestionError/LeaseLostError. Retryable + attempts < max_attempts (3) → schedule_upload_retry at 30 s / 120 s / 600 s, bumped by any Retry-After up to 15 min; otherwise fail_upload_job. (upload_queue.py:83-149, ingestion_worker.py:200-204)
- Worker concurrency: --once always runs one job inline; INGESTION_CONCURRENCY > 1 spawns a ThreadPoolExecutor and the loop drains in-flight futures on shutdown. (ingestion_worker.py:276-335)
- Schema-compat branch on status reads: _select_upload_jobs retries with a legacy column list when cancel_requested_at/cancelled_at are absent, and heartbeat_job_control falls back to heartbeat_upload_job on PGRST202/42883. (upload.py:1067-1077, upload_queue.py:57-73)
- Dev vs built app: in dev the /upload prefix goes through spaAwareApiProxy, which bypasses the proxy only for GET requests whose Accept includes text/html; in e2e mode VITE_API_URL is '/__e2e_api' and anything unmocked answers 501. (vite.config.js:39-48, 68-66, 84-87)

### Failure modes

- Dropzone, non-PDF name or MIME → no request; toast 'Unsupported file' / description '<filename>: Only PDF manuscripts are accepted'. (batchState.js:49-50)
- Dropzone, >25 MB → no request; '<filename>: Maximum size is 25 MB'. (batchState.js:51)
- Step-1 validation → inline field errors, wizard blocked: 'Enter the full thesis title', 'Select the academic program', 'Select the program specialization', 'Select the department', 'Enter a valid year'. (uploadState.js:101-117)
- Body over 27 MB with a declared Content-Length → 413 {"detail":"Request body exceeds the 27 MB limit"}, before authentication. Toast: 'Upload failed — Request body exceeds the 27 MB limit'. (request_limits.py:80-92)
- Undeclared/chunked overrun → read stops at the ceiling; FastAPI reports 400 'There was an error parsing the body'. (request_limits.py:107-114)
- 11th POST /upload/paper in a minute → 429 {"error":"Rate limit exceeded: 10 per 1 minute"}. The body has no `detail` and no `message`, so apiErrorMessage falls through and the toast reads 'Upload failed — Something went wrong. Please try again.' (rate_limiting.py:43, apiErrors.js:10-22)
- No/expired token → 401 'Invalid or expired authentication token' + WWW-Authenticate: Bearer. The interceptor attempts exactly one silent refreshSession and replays; on failure it signs out and hard-navigates to loginPathWithNext(current). (auth.py:275-279, api.js:127-147)
- Profile missing / pending / rejected → 403 'This account is pending approval or has been rejected.' (auth.py:151-155)
- profiles read failure inside _ensure_approved_account → 503 'Account validation is temporarily unavailable.' (auth.py:145-150)
- Role without the upload feature → 403 'Administrator privileges are required for this action.' (auth.py:448-451)
- Admin/superadmin on an aal1 session with REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA → 403 'Multi-factor authentication is required for privileged access.'; the interceptor detects the exact prefix and fires reportPrivilegedMfaRequired so the shell offers the second factor in place. (auth.py:224-240, api.js:154)
- Non-superadmin submitting a foreign department → 403 'You can only search your assigned department.' (auth.py:114-118)
- Superadmin naming an unknown department → 422 'Unknown department.'; departments read failure → 503 'Department validation is unavailable.' (auth.py:122-128)
- get_user_scope: status != approved → 403 'This account is not approved for application access.'; read failure → 503 'User department validation is unavailable.'; no row → 403 'A valid user profile is required for department-scoped access.' (auth.py:82-102)
- Metadata → 422 'Title must contain between 5 and 300 characters' / 'Authors must not exceed 500 characters' / 'Abstract must not exceed 10,000 characters' / 'Year must be a valid four-digit completion year'. (upload.py:524-536)
- thesis_category not student|faculty → 422 "thesis_category must be 'student' or 'faculty'." (services/catalog.py:21-26)
- Catalog resolution → 422 'Unknown or archived department.' | 'A valid academic program is required.' | 'Program does not belong to the selected department or is archived.' | 'Specialization does not belong to the selected program or is archived.' | '<CODE> requires a valid specialization.' | '<CODE> does not accept a specialization.' | 'The legacy track program is unavailable.' | 'The legacy track specialization is unavailable.' (services/catalog.py:56-115)
- File > 25 MB reaching the handler → 413 'File exceeds the 25 MB limit'. (upload.py:519-520)
- PDF validation → 415 'Only PDF thesis files are accepted' | 415 'Upload MIME type must be application/pdf' | 400 'Empty file' | 422 'File content is not a valid PDF' | 422 'Encrypted or password-protected PDFs are not accepted' | 422 'Malformed or unreadable PDF' | 422 'PDF must contain at least one page' | 422 'PDF exceeds the 500-page safety limit'. (upload.py:485-511)
- Malformed Idempotency-Key → 400 'Idempotency-Key must be a valid UUID'. (upload.py:652-656)
- Same key, different bytes → 409 'Idempotency-Key was already used for another file' (detected by substring-matching 'different content' in the driver error text). (upload.py:808-810)
- reserve_upload_job otherwise throwing → 503 'The durable upload queue is temporarily unavailable'; empty result → 503 'The durable upload queue did not reserve the submission'. (upload.py:811-813)
- Storage upload failure → object removed, job flipped to failed with message 'Private source staging failed.' and error 'The upload could not be queued safely. Please try again.'; client gets 503 'The private manuscript could not be staged safely'. If the delete also fails, record_storage_cleanup queues a storage_cleanup_queue row and cleanup_status stays 'pending'. (upload.py:826-839, upload.py:551-577, services/cleanup.py:8-34)
- Queue transition unconfirmed and the row has not advanced → source deleted, job failed, 503 'The private manuscript could not be queued safely'. (upload.py:841-870)
- Poll 404 (expired, deleted, or another owner's job) → 'Upload job not found (it may have expired)'; the UI clears sessionStorage and shows 'This upload job has expired or is no longer available.' with no retry. (upload.py:1149-1153, Upload.jsx:339-343)
- Poll 503 / 403 / network error → backoff 1.5 s × failures; after 5 consecutive failures pollError = 'The server could not confirm the upload status. The job was not cancelled.' with a 'Resume status check' button. (upload.py:1148, Upload.jsx:344-350)
- Poll running 30 minutes → 'Status checking paused after 30 minutes. You can resume it safely.' (Upload.jsx:305-308)
- Job stuck in 'staging' >15 min (API died between reserve and queue) → claim_upload_job fails it as 'staging_timeout' with message 'Private source staging did not complete.' / error 'Please submit the manuscript again.' (supabase_setup.sql:1533-1542)
- Worker lease expiring mid-pipeline → heartbeat returns false → LeaseLostError; the worker logs and abandons, and claim_upload_job re-claims the job for another attempt until attempt_count >= max_attempts, then fails it as 'worker_lease_expired'. (ingestion.py:85-87, ingestion_worker.py:184-185, supabase_setup.sql:1544-1553)
- Staged hash mismatch / corrupt / encrypted staged PDF, no extractable text, unresolved OCR pages, invalid chunk records, no clean text, embedding count or dimension mismatch → PermanentIngestionError, no retry, job.error = 'The manuscript could not be processed safely.' UI: toast.error('Ingestion failed') + IngestOutcome 'Ingestion failed'. (ingestion.py:56-164, upload_queue.py:135-149)
- ClamAV reports FOUND → MalwareDetectedIngestionError (permanent), a critical operational_alert 'malware:<job_id>' is upserted and the webhook fires; cleanup_status goes 'pending' so the staged object is deleted by the worker's maintenance tick. (ingestion.py:114-117, ingestion_worker.py:190-199)
- ClamAV unreachable → MalwareScannerUnavailable carries status_code=503 → retryable; the worker also refuses to claim any job while scanner_status()=='unavailable'. (malware.py:13-16, ingestion_worker.py:299-303)
- Every chunk already in the archive at ≥99.9% → DuplicateManuscriptIngestionError; job.error is its own composed sentence naming the archived thesis, and IngestOutcome matches /exact duplicate/i to show the heading 'Already in the archive' plus a 'View archive' button. (ingestion.py:173-183, IngestOutcome.jsx:155-175)
- commit_upload_ingestion raising after Postgres already committed → the worker re-reads upload_jobs + papers and treats status='completed' AND ingestion_status='ready' AND chunk_count==len(chunks) as success, logging 'Recovered successful durable ingestion after an ambiguous RPC response'. (ingestion.py:249-274)
- Retryable failure with attempts remaining → status 'retry_wait', message 'A temporary service problem occurred. The job will retry automatically.'; PipelineTimeline renders 'Temporary service interruption. Automatic retry N/M is scheduled for <time>.' (supabase_setup.sql:1610-1631, PipelineTimeline.jsx:159-164)
- Cancellation → job 'cancelled'; the UI clears sessionStorage, toasts 'Upload cancelled — The staged manuscript is being removed safely.' and renders IngestOutcome 'Upload cancelled'. Cancel RPC missing (pre-operations schema) → 503 'Upload cancellation requires the operations migration'; not owner → 403 'You cannot cancel this upload job'; unknown → 404 'Upload job not found'. (Upload.jsx:331-334, IngestOutcome.jsx:178-187, upload.py:1236-1247)

### What gets persisted

- public.upload_jobs — one row inserted by reserve_upload_job (status 'staging', stage 'store', progress 5, idempotency_key, source_path, original_filename, content_sha256, request_payload jsonb, max_attempts), then mutated by queue_upload_job → 'queued', claim_upload_job → 'processing' (+attempt_count, lease_owner, lease_expires_at, started_at), every heartbeat (stage/progress/message/lease), and finally commit_upload_ingestion → 'completed' with paper_id, chunks, duplication, completed_at, expires_at = now()+30 days. (supabase_setup.sql:1434-1714)
- public.upload_job_events — one row per lifecycle transition ('reserved', 'status_changed', 'stage_changed', 'cleanup_changed', 'cancellation_requested'), written by the record_upload_job_event AFTER INSERT/UPDATE trigger; retained 90 days while jobs expire at 30. (migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:21-137)
- Supabase Storage, private `pdfs` bucket — object at uploads/{owner_id}/{job_id}/{sanitized}.pdf, content-type application/pdf, upsert true. Never publicly reachable; deleted on staging/queue compensation, on cancellation cleanup and on failure cleanup. (routers/upload.py:784, upload.py:599-604)
- public.papers — one row per successful ingestion, id == job_id, inserted by commit_paper_ingestion with title/authors/year/abstract/track/filename/storage_path/chunk_count/duplication_scan/uploaded_by/department/redaction_stats, ingestion_status 'processing' then 'ready'. program_id, specialization_id, legacy_track, classification_status and thesis_category are written by the BEFORE INSERT hydrate trigger, not by the insert. (supabase_setup.sql:513-570, migrations/20260819_thesis_category.sql:47-64)
- public.paper_index_versions — one row carrying embedding_model, embedding_dimensions, preprocessing_version, chunking_version, tokenizer, chunk_size_tokens, chunk_overlap_tokens, provenance_status='verified' and activated_at. (supabase_setup.sql:529-541)
- public.chunks — one row per surviving chunk: paper_id, chunk_index, content, embedding vector(768), metadata jsonb, page_start, page_end, section, index_version. (supabase_setup.sql:543-560, services/ingestion.py:215-234)
- Activity log row via services/activity.log_activity('paper_upload', {paper_id, title, track, chunks, duplication_flagged, matched_chunk_percentage, highest_similarity}). (services/ingestion.py:276-285)
- Refreshed papers.duplication_scan for already-archived neighbours via novelty.refresh_after_ingest → store_rescan, after the commit and swallowing its own errors. (services/ingestion.py:298-300)
- public.ingestion_workers — the worker's registry row (state starting/idle/processing/degraded/stopping, scanner_status, current_job_id, last_seen_at), heartbeated every INGESTION_HEARTBEAT_SECONDS. (migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:48-59, ingestion_worker.py:156-159, 222-252)
- public.operational_alerts — a critical 'malware:<job_id>' alert upserted when a staged manuscript fails the ClamAV scan. (ingestion_worker.py:190-199)
- public.security_audit_events — an 'upload_cancellation' event with {job_id, outcome} on every cancel request. (routers/upload.py:1250-1257)
- public.storage_cleanup_queue — a pending row whenever a staged object could not be deleted during compensation or cleanup (operation 'rollback_upload', resource_path, job_id, error_category; no exception text). (services/cleanup.py:18-27, upload_queue.py:160-178)
- Browser sessionStorage['activeUploadJob'] = {jobId, idempotencyKey, title} — written on 202, read once on mount to restore polling, cleared on completed/failed/cancelled/404/reset. Also sessionStorage['iskai_guest_rate_id'] for unauthenticated traffic. (Upload.jsx:378-384, 355-370, api.js:25-40)

### Surprises

- The API resolves the department TWICE per upload. routers/upload.py:902 calls resolve_effective_department, then passes the already-resolved value into _resolve_staging_context, whose _resolve_upload_scope calls it again (upload.py:664). Each call does a full get_user_scope profiles read, and for a superadmin a second `departments` read as well. The comment at upload.py:900-901 explains the ordering but not the duplication.
- The rate limits are NOT shared the way the code comments claim. api.js:279-281 and upload.py:1044-1048 both justify the batch endpoints by saying the 10/minute upload limit is 'shared by staging, metadata extraction, and cancellation'. slowapi's default key_style is "url", so the bucket key is (rate-limit key, concrete request path) — /upload/paper, /upload/extract-metadata and /upload/batch each get their own 10/minute, and POST /upload/jobs/{job_id}/cancel gets a separate bucket per job id, which makes that limit nearly meaningless. The same applies to the 120/minute default in services/rate_limiting.py:43: it is per URL path, not a global per-user ceiling.
- Rate limiting silently degrades to per-IP. services/rate_limiting.py:12-33 keys by user only when SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET is configured; config.py:186 defaults it to ''. On a campus behind NAT every uploader then shares one `ip:` bucket.
- A 429 on upload shows a meaningless message. slowapi's handler returns {"error": "Rate limit exceeded: …"} with no `detail` and no `message`, and apiErrorMessage (apiErrors.js:10-22) only reads those two keys, so the toast is 'Upload failed — Something went wrong. Please try again.'
- Editing metadata after a failed-but-reserved submit is silently discarded. reserve_upload_job uses INSERT … ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING and then re-selects the existing row (supabase_setup.sql:1470-1484); request_payload is never updated. If a submit fails at the storage or queue phase, the user fixes the title and presses Ingest again with the same idempotency key, the API happily re-stages and queues — but the worker reads the ORIGINAL request_payload, so the archive gets the old title. The digest check only compares file bytes, never the metadata.
- upload_jobs.thesis_category is dead data. The column exists with a CHECK and a default of 'student' (supabase_setup.sql:1340-1343, 1384-1387) but no code path ever writes it: reserve_upload_job does not set it, and nothing updates it. A faculty-research upload has upload_jobs.thesis_category = 'student'. The authoritative value lives only inside request_payload, and only papers.thesis_category gets it (via the hydrate trigger).
- Four of the paper_data fields the worker builds are inert, not one. services/ingestion.py:202-204 documents thesis_category as inert because commit_paper_ingestion's INSERT column list omits it — but program_id, specialization_id, legacy_track and classification_status (ingestion.py:198-201) are equally absent from that INSERT (supabase_setup.sql:513-525) and come only from the hydrate trigger. Removing the trigger would silently drop the whole academic classification while the Python payload still looked correct.
- papers.legacy_track gets a non-legacy value on the happy path. The wizard puts the specialization name (or, for a program with no specializations, the program CODE) into form.track (lib/catalog.js:72, 84); the API forwards it as legacy_track, and services/catalog.py:110-114 stores it verbatim alongside an identical display_track. So a 2026 BSCS upload ends up with track='Data Mining' AND legacy_track='Data Mining', and a BSIS upload with legacy_track='BSIS' — in a column whose stated purpose is the pre-catalog vocabulary. It is exposed on /papers (routers/papers.py:60).
- Dead size check. routers/upload.py:493-494 rejects len(file_bytes) > max_upload_mb. Both callers reach it only after _read_limited_upload (upload.py:515-521), which caps the read at limit+1 and 413s on the extra byte, so the buffer can never exceed the limit here. The branch is unreachable.
- Dead constant in the page. Upload.jsx:34-42 defines STATUS_LABELS for all seven job statuses and nothing in the file (or anywhere else) reads it; the status wording actually rendered comes from stageView/PIPELINE_STAGES and IngestOutcome.
- An entire component is unused. src/components/upload/WizardRail.jsx, and the railMode() and summaryRows() helpers it depends on (wizardSteps.js:102-130), are referenced by nothing outside their own unit tests. Upload.jsx uses the horizontal WizardStepper instead.
- The client's file-type check is looser than the server's. fileValidationError accepts an empty File.type (batchState.js:49); a .pdf whose MIME the OS does not supply is sent by the browser as application/octet-stream and rejected by the server with 415 'Upload MIME type must be application/pdf' — a full 25 MB round trip for a file the Dropzone said was fine.
- Three server-side metadata caps have no client counterpart: authors > 500 chars, abstract > 10 000 chars and title > 300 chars all pass uploadMetadataErrors (which only enforces a 5-char minimum) and come back as a 422 toast after the upload.
- require_upload_access does not use the shared feature resolver. auth.py:445-447 reads get_role_features() directly and indexes features[role].get('upload'), skipping the isinstance guards and the DEFAULT_FEATURES fallback that _feature_enabled (auth.py:350-372) applies for chat/archive. The effective policy still matches (upload defaults to False), but a role_features row holding a non-dict for a role raises AttributeError → 500 instead of 403.
- Privileged MFA applies to the MORE privileged account only. require_upload_access calls _require_privileged_mfa for admin/superadmin but never for a faculty account that has the upload feature enabled (auth.py:441-447). With REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA on, faculty can upload from an aal1 session while an admin cannot.
- The status endpoint is behind the upload feature gate, so turning `upload` off for a role mid-job 403s that role's polling. The client has no branch for 403: it counts it as a generic failure, backs off five times, and then tells the user 'The server could not confirm the upload status. The job was not cancelled.' (Upload.jsx:344-350)
- 'staging' is a real, user-visible status the timeline cannot draw. wizardSteps.js:33 aliases the backend's `store` stage onto `download` precisely because findIndex returned -1 and greyed the whole stepper out; the comment records the bug. UploadJobStatus still advertises `store` as a stage (models.py:128).
- The 202 response's `status` field is hardcoded. upload.py:875 always returns 'queued' on the success path regardless of what the row actually holds; only the idempotent-replay branch (upload.py:822) reports the true status. A replay of a finished job therefore returns status 'completed', and Upload.jsx:386-388 immediately paints IngestOutcome 'Thesis indexed!' with an undefined chunk count until the first poll lands.
- The paper id IS the job id. services/ingestion.py:192 sets paper_data['id'] = job_id and commit_upload_ingestion enforces the equality (supabase_setup.sql:1697-1699). This is load-bearing: the hydrate trigger looks the classification up with `select request_payload from upload_jobs where id = new.id`, so calling commit_paper_ingestion with any other id silently produces an unclassified paper.
- commit_paper_ingestion never writes papers.content — p_paper->>'content' is always NULL because services/ingestion.py builds no 'content' key. The column exists and is documented as intentionally empty at supabase_setup.sql:1189, but the INSERT still lists it.
- Base schema and migration disagree about cancelled cleanup. claim_upload_cleanup in supabase_setup.sql:1717-1739 claims only status='failed'; the 20260724 migration widens it to status in ('failed','cancelled') (migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:450-462). Replaying the base file after the migration silently reverts the body and strands the staged PDFs of cancelled uploads — exactly the CREATE OR REPLACE hazard the project warns about.
- The 409 for a reused idempotency key is detected by substring-matching "different content" in the driver's error string (upload.py:809), not by SQLSTATE, even though services/db_errors.py exists for precisely this classification and is used two functions away for 22P02.
- Error-classification asymmetry in the compensations: _fail_staging_job records failure_category as the Python exception CLASS NAME (type(error).__name__, upload.py:834) rather than a stable category, so the operational dashboards group by whatever exception the Supabase SDK happened to raise.
- A 'Valid Manuscript' badge is shown for a file that has passed only a name/MIME/size check — no %PDF- magic, no page count, no encryption check, none of which run until the bytes reach the server (Dropzone.jsx:142-147).

Omissions the checker caught:

- commit_paper_ingestion has an idempotence/conflict guard the trace's step 48 omits: if a papers row with this id is already ingestion_status='ready' with a matching chunk_count AND a matching live chunk count, it returns that id and inserts nothing; if a row exists in any other state it raises 'Paper ingestion ID already exists in an incomplete state'. This is what makes the worker's ambiguous-RPC recovery (services/ingestion.py:249-274) safe on a genuine re-run, and it is a distinct failure string the trace never lists. (rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:501-510)
- Per-chunk validation inside commit_paper_ingestion's insert loop is missing from the failure modes: each chunk's embedding must be a JSON array of exactly 768 elements ('Invalid embedding dimensions') and its content must be non-empty ('Chunk content is required'). These fire inside the transaction, after the papers and paper_index_versions rows are already inserted, so the whole commit rolls back. (rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:542-548)
- POST /upload/jobs/{job_id}/cancel does an extra, uncached `profiles` read of its own to decide `is_superadmin` before calling the RPC — a fourth profiles round trip on the cancel path, on top of _ensure_approved_account and get_user_role. The trace's latency notes cover the 202 and poll paths but not this. (rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1224-1229)
- heartbeat_upload_job_control deliberately freezes `message` once cancel_requested_at is set (`message = case when cancel_requested_at is null then coalesce(p_message, message) else message end`), so the 'Cancellation requested…' text written by request_upload_cancellation survives the worker's next stage report. The trace describes the keep-alive/stage-update serialization but not this DB-side guard. (rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:234-242)
- GET /upload/status/{job_id} carries NO @limiter.limit decorator — unlike /upload/paper, /upload/batch, /upload/extract-metadata and the cancel route. It is covered only by the 120/minute default, and because slowapi keys that default by concrete request path, every job id gets its own 120/minute bucket. The 1.5 s polling loop (40 req/min per job) is therefore effectively unmetered. This is a direct extension of the trace's own per-URL-keying surprise but is not stated. (rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1142-1143, services/rate_limiting.py:43)
- The worker's ClamAV gate is not evaluated per claim. `_Housekeeping.tick` refreshes `scan_state` only when `now >= self.registry_at`, i.e. once per ingestion_heartbeat_seconds (30 s default), and the claim loop reads that cached value. A worker can keep claiming jobs for up to ~30 s after clamd goes down; those jobs then fail on MalwareScannerUnavailable and retry. (rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:222-234, 299-303)
- MalwareScannerUnavailable is raised for a third case the trace's failure modes omit: a ClamAV reply that is neither '…OK' nor contains 'FOUND' (e.g. a truncated or protocol-error response) also raises it, not just socket/timeout errors. Same 503 status_code, so still retryable. (rag-thesis-backend/services/malware.py:57-61)
- The idempotent-replay 202 can carry status 'failed' or 'cancelled', not only 'completed'. Upload.jsx:386 copies res.status straight into `job`, isTerminalJob accepts all three, and IngestOutcome then paints the 'Ingestion failed' branch with `{job.error}` undefined (empty <p>) or the 'Upload cancelled' branch — until the first poll 500 ms later fills it in. This generalises the trace's own surprise about the hardcoded 'queued'. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:386-388, src/components/upload/IngestOutcome.jsx:155-187)
- FastAPI's own request validation runs before `_validate_metadata`: `title` is `Annotated[str, Form()]` with no default, so a submission missing the title field returns FastAPI's 422 list-shaped `detail` (which apiErrorMessage renders as 'body.title: Field required'), not the handler's 'Title must contain between 5 and 300 characters'. (rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:888, rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/apiErrors.js:12-14)

## 6. Worker picks up a queued job and makes the paper searchable

<a id="6-worker-ingest"></a>

Flow key: `worker-ingest`

### Overview

Durable ingestion: the worker claims a queued upload job, runs the eight-stage pipeline under a leased heartbeat, and atomically commits the paper row + 768-d chunk vectors so match_chunks can retrieve them.

**Trigger:** `queue_upload_job` has flipped an `upload_jobs` row from `staging` to `status='queued', stage='queued', progress=8, source_stored=true, next_retry_at=now()` (rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1501-1515), which happens at the end of POST /upload/paper (rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:842). A separately-started `python -m workers.ingestion_worker` process then polls and claims it. The API never runs the pipeline inline.

**Actors, in order:** workers/ingestion_worker.py — the separate worker process (run_worker loop, _Housekeeping, LeaseHeartbeat) -> services/upload_queue.py — thin service-role RPC wrappers (claim/heartbeat/retry/fail/cleanup/expire) -> services/ingestion.py — process_ingestion_job, the actual eight-stage pipeline -> services/malware.py — ClamAV INSTREAM client -> services/document_processor.py — PyMuPDF extraction + tesserocr OCR fallback + PII redaction -> services/chunker.py — cl100k_base token splitter (800/100) and page/section mapping -> services/embedder.py — GoogleGenerativeAIEmbeddings via services/gemini_pool.run (768-d, batches of 64) -> services/novelty.py — 0.85 duplication screen (screen_new_submission) and post-commit refresh_after_ingest -> services/index_provenance.py — current_index_fingerprint stamped onto the paper -> PostgreSQL/Supabase — claim_upload_job, heartbeat_upload_job_control, commit_upload_ingestion -> commit_paper_ingestion, fail_upload_job, schedule_upload_retry, finalize_upload_cancellation, claim_upload_cleanup, expire_upload_jobs, record_upload_job_event trigger, hydrate_paper_academic_classification trigger -> services/operations.py — ingestion_workers registry, operational_alerts, webhook notify -> rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx + components/upload/PipelineTimeline.jsx — the 1.5 s poll of GET /upload/status/{job_id}

**Latency:** Lease 120 s, heartbeat 30 s, poll 2 s, maintenance 300 s; `config.validate_production_services` refuses to start if `heartbeat * 2 >= lease` (config.py:125-128,244-245). Discovery latency is up to one poll interval (2 s) after queueing. The dominant costs are all inside one lease-covered job: embedding is ceil(N/64) sequential Gemini batch calls (embedder.py:60,92-97) with no concurrency; the novelty screen is ONE `match_chunks` RPC PER CHUNK — a 400-chunk thesis is 400 sequential round trips (novelty.py:352-359) — and there is no heartbeat checkpoint between `embed` (58) and `screen` (72) or inside the screen loop, so the 30 s background keep-alive is the only thing holding the lease for what can be several minutes. After the commit, `refresh_after_ingest` downloads EVERY other department paper's full chunk embeddings over PostgREST (`_paper_chunk_vectors`, novelty.py:434-443) and, for any paper with no stored match table, pays a full `rescan_indexed_paper` that also pulls the entire department index (`_candidate_chunk_vectors`, novelty.py:228-267) — so the first ingestion after this feature shipped is O(N²) in stored vectors, and it runs with the keep-alive thread already dead (the job is `completed`, so `heartbeat_upload_job_control` returns lease_valid=false and the thread exits within 30 s). At the default concurrency of 1 the entire main loop, including cleanup and expiry, is blocked for that whole duration. `count_tokens` runs twice per surviving chunk because `split_document` validates and `process_ingestion_job` validates again after noise filtering. OCR rasterizes at 200 dpi per scanned page. The browser polls every 1500 ms for up to 30 minutes.

### Steps (48)

| # | Layer | What happens | Anchor |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | worker | Process start: `silence_known_third_party_warnings()` runs BEFORE any langchain import (note the explicit `pylint: disable=wrong-import-order` around the supabase import), then config.settings loads. `main()` installs SIGINT/SIGTERM handlers that only set `stop_event`. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:16-25,341-357` |
| 2 | worker | `run_worker` builds its OWN service-role client (`create_client(settings.supabase_url, settings.supabase_key)`) and a worker_id of `hostname:pid:uuid8`. Concurrency is forced to 1 when `--once`, else `settings.ingestion_concurrency` (default 1, max 8); a ThreadPoolExecutor is created only when concurrency > 1, so the default path runs jobs inline on the loop thread. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:270-289, rag-thesis-backend/config.py:136` |
| 3 | worker | Loop top: finished futures are reaped and counted, then `_Housekeeping.tick()`. Every `ingestion_heartbeat_seconds` (30) it calls `scanner_status()` and `register_worker(...)` (RPC `register_ingestion_worker`, upserting `ingestion_workers` with a hardcoded version '2.1.0'). Every `ingestion_maintenance_seconds` (300) it runs `process_one_cleanup` -> `expire_terminal_jobs` -> `evaluate_operations` (the last only when `operations_monitor_enabled`, default false). | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:222-243,291-298, rag-thesis-backend/services/operations.py:52-65` |
| 4 | worker | Scanner gate: if `house.scan_state == 'unavailable'` the worker claims nothing this tick and sleeps `ingestion_poll_seconds` (2.0). `MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled` yields 'disabled', which passes the gate. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:299-303, rag-thesis-backend/services/malware.py:42-49` |
| 5 | db | `claim_job` -> RPC `claim_upload_job(p_worker_id, p_lease_seconds=120)`. Before selecting anything it runs three sweeps in the same call: (a) `staging` rows older than 15 min -> `failed`/`staging_timeout`; (b) `processing` rows with an expired lease AND `cancel_requested_at not null` -> `cancelled`; (c) `processing` rows with an expired lease AND `attempt_count >= max_attempts` -> `failed`/`worker_lease_expired`. | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:351-384, rag-thesis-backend/services/upload_queue.py:23-30` |
| 6 | db | Selection: `queued` OR (`retry_wait` AND next_retry_at <= now) OR (`processing` AND lease expired), filtered by `cancel_requested_at is null AND attempt_count < max_attempts AND source_stored = true`, ordered by `coalesce(next_retry_at, created_at), created_at`, `for update skip locked limit 1`. So an expired-lease job is re-claimable in the same statement that would have failed it — the ordering matters. | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:385-391` |
| 7 | db | Claim write: `status='processing', stage='download', progress=10, message='Durable worker claimed the ingestion job.', attempt_count = attempt_count + 1, lease_owner = worker_id, lease_expires_at = now() + 120s, heartbeat_at = now(), started_at = coalesce(started_at, now())`, RETURNING the whole row (that row is the `job` dict the Python side uses, so `job['attempt_count']` is already incremented). | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:393-399` |
| 8 | db | The AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE trigger `record_upload_job_event` fires on that write and inserts a `status_changed` row into `upload_job_events` with progress/cleanup_status/failure_category/cancel_requested in `details`. This is what `GET /upload/status/{id}` surfaces as `last_event_at`. | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:98-142, rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1080-1095` |
| 9 | worker | `house.register_processing(job_id)` writes `state='processing', current_job_id=...` to the registry, then `process_claimed_job(client, job, worker_id, house.scan_state)` runs — inline at concurrency 1, on the pool otherwise. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:315-323` |
| 10 | worker | `with LeaseHeartbeat(...) as heartbeat`: `__enter__` starts the daemon keep-alive thread and RETURNS THE BOUND `self.update` METHOD (not the object). State is two locks: `_state_lock` guards `_valid`/`_cancel_requested`, `_rpc_lock` serializes control RPCs so a bare keep-alive can never land after and overwrite a real stage update. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:60-122,177-178` |
| 11 | worker | Keep-alive thread: every 30 s it calls `self.update()` with NO kwargs — a bare lease renewal that passes `p_stage/p_progress/p_message` as NULL, which `coalesce` in SQL leaves untouched — then re-registers the worker. On `CancellationRequested` it returns (stops beating); on any other exception it logs and keeps looping. | `rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:149-166` |
| 12 | db | `update()` -> `heartbeat_job_control` -> RPC `heartbeat_upload_job_control`, whose WHERE is `id = job AND status='processing' AND lease_owner = worker AND lease_expires_at >= now()`. It extends the lease 120 s, sets stage/progress via coalesce, and returns `{lease_valid, cancel_requested}`. Note the message column is frozen once cancellation is requested (`case when cancel_requested_at is null then coalesce(p_message,message) else message end`) while stage and progress are not. | `rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:218-248, rag-thesis-backend/services/upload_queue.py:46-73` |
| 13 | service | Pipeline entry `process_ingestion_job`: reads `source_path`, `original_filename`, `content_sha256`, `request_payload` off the claimed row. Missing source_path or hash -> `PermanentIngestionError('The durable upload source is incomplete')`. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:90-99` |
| 14 | service | Checkpoint 1 `_require_lease(stage='download', progress=12, message='Downloading the private staged manuscript...')`. Then `client.storage.from_('pdfs').download(source_path)` — the private bucket copy staged by the API. A non-bytes payload raises `RuntimeError('Private storage returned an invalid payload')` (retryable, not Permanent). | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:56-62,101-106` |
| 15 | service | `_validate_staged_pdf`: SHA-256 of the downloaded bytes must equal the digest the API reserved (`PermanentIngestionError('The staged PDF hash did not match the reserved upload')`), `%PDF-` magic, `fitz.open` must not be `needs_pass`, and `1 <= page_count <= settings.max_pdf_pages` (500). Every failure here is Permanent. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:65-82, rag-thesis-backend/config.py:115` |
| 16 | service | Checkpoint 2 `stage='malware_scan', progress=16`. `scan_pdf` streams the bytes to ClamAV over zINSTREAM in 64 KiB frames. Response suffix 'OK' passes; 'FOUND' raises `MalwareDetected` which is re-raised as `MalwareDetectedIngestionError` (a PermanentIngestionError); anything else raises `MalwareScannerUnavailable` (carries `status_code = 503`, therefore RETRYABLE). `MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled` returns immediately. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:109-117, rag-thesis-backend/services/malware.py:19-61` |
| 17 | service | Checkpoint 3 `stage='extract', progress=20`. `extract_document(file_bytes, filename)` dispatches on the `.pdf` suffix (always true — the API forces it via `sanitize_filename(..., force_suffix='pdf')`). | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:119-124, rag-thesis-backend/services/document_processor.py:300-307, rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:480-482` |
| 18 | service | Per page: `page.get_text()`.
