### Branches

- Superadmin vs everyone else: BatchDefaultsForm renders a department Select for isSuperadmin and a locked read-only field otherwise (UploadBatch.jsx:151-181); resolve_effective_department enforces the same split server-side and 403s a mismatch (dependencies/auth.py:113-119).
- Row program vs request-level fallback: _ClassificationCache.context uses the row's (program_id, specialization_id) when program_id is set, otherwise the request-level pair; the legacy `track` translation is applied only to the request-level default (routers/upload.py:726-752). The frontend no longer sends a request-level program_id at all (api.js:292-297), and the E2E asserts its absence (e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:644).
- student vs faculty: require_program = (category=='student') server-side (routers/upload.py:683) and the mirrored client rule `form.thesis_category !== 'faculty' && !form.program_id` (uploadState.js:106).
- Metadata extraction: local-only when all four of title/authors/year/department were read from the title page, otherwise a Gemini completion under a 3-way semaphore; a completion failure silently returns the local data (routers/upload.py:1450-1452, 1424-1426).
- reserve_upload_job created vs pre-existing: created (or still 'staging') continues to storage; pre-existing non-staging short-circuits to 202 'This submission is already tracked.' (routers/upload.py:818-824).
- Worker concurrency: ingestion_concurrency==1 (default) processes the claimed job inline on the loop thread with no executor; >1 submits to a ThreadPoolExecutor and the loop waits on FIRST_COMPLETED. `--once` forces the inline shape regardless of the setting (workers/ingestion_worker.py:281-285).
- claim_upload_job eligibility: queued, OR retry_wait whose next_retry_at has passed, OR processing whose lease expired — all requiring cancel_requested_at IS NULL, attempt_count<max_attempts and source_stored (migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:385-391).
- Cancellation: 'processing' is cooperative (flag only, worker stops at the next checkpoint); staging/queued/retry_wait are cancelled synchronously in the RPC (migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:183-206).
- Duplication: exact_duplicate (every chunk verbatim) refuses the ingest; anything less is 'flagged' and still committed with the scan stored on papers.duplication_scan (services/ingestion.py:172-183).
- heartbeat_upload_job_control present vs absent: on a pre-operations schema upload_queue falls back to heartbeat_upload_job and reports cancel_requested:false forever (services/upload_queue.py:62-73). The job-status read has the same two-tier fallback for the cancellation columns (routers/upload.py:1066-1077).
- Poll state machine: enabled (2s) / disabled (no active ids or not on the ingesting step) / failure-paused after 5 consecutive fetch failures / ceiling-paused after 30 minutes. resumePolling() re-stamps pollStartedRef, clears ceilingReached and refetches, which resets the failure count (UploadBatch.jsx:1015-1020).
- sessionStorage restore: owner match -> rows restored with file:null; owner mismatch or the legacy bare-array shape -> discarded; storage unavailable (private mode) -> silently skipped (batchState.js:400-424, UploadBatch.jsx:754-769).
- 3D scene: WebGL healthy -> R3F canvas with one of seven stage groups; context lost -> Css3DFallback; tab hidden -> frameloop 'never'; sustained <45fps -> degraded dpr and antialias off (components/three/useSceneRuntime.js).
- Admin tabs: BASE_TABS (Overview, Upload history, System Management) for admin; Operations appended only for superadmin (pages/Admin.jsx:133-135).

### Failure modes

- Non-PDF / >25MB at the dropzone: never leaves the browser. toast.error 'Unsupported file' (or 'N files skipped') with description '<name>: Only PDF manuscripts are accepted' / 'Maximum size is 25 MB' (Dropzone.jsx:66, batchState.js:50-51).
- More than 20 files dropped: addRows slices silently, addFiles toasts warning 'N files were not added' + 'A batch holds at most 20 manuscripts. Ingest these first, then start another batch.' (UploadBatch.jsx:900).
- Same name+size dropped twice: toast.info 'N files are already in this batch' (UploadBatch.jsx:905).
- Batch body over max_batch_files*max_upload_mb+8MB with a declared Content-Length: HTTP 413 {'detail':'Request body exceeds the 508 MB limit'} before authentication and before anything is spooled to disk. An undeclared/chunked overrun is stopped on the receive channel and surfaces as FastAPI's 400 'There was an error parsing the body' (services/request_limits.py:80-92,108-114).
- 11th upload-family request inside a minute (staging, batch extraction and cancellation share the bucket): 429 from slowapi; UI toast.error 'Batch upload failed' with the limiter's message.
- Unapproved / rejected profile: 403 'This account is pending approval or has been rejected.' (dependencies/auth.py:151-155).
- Role without the upload feature: 403 'Administrator privileges are required for this action.' (dependencies/auth.py:448-451).
- Admin/superadmin session not at aal2 with REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA on: the PRIVILEGED_MFA_REQUIRED_DETAIL refusal; the axios interceptor calls reportPrivilegedMfaRequired() so the shell offers the second factor, and the call still rejects (src/api.js:154).
- Empty files list: 422 'A batch must contain at least one file'. Over the cap: 413 'A batch may contain at most 20 files'. WHOLE batch (routers/upload.py:926-930).
- Malformed rows JSON: 422 'rows must be a JSON list of {title, authors, year, idempotency_key} objects'. Length mismatch: 422 'rows lists N entries for M files'. Bad UUID: 422 'rows[i].idempotency_key must be a valid UUID'. Repeat: 422 'rows[i].idempotency_key repeats an earlier key'. Orphan specialization: 422 'rows[i].specialization_id needs that row to name its program_id'. All reject the WHOLE batch (routers/upload.py:933-958).
- Non-superadmin sending a department that is not theirs: 403 'You can only search your assigned department.' — WHOLE batch. Superadmin naming an unknown department: 422 'Unknown department.'; departments-table outage: 503 'Department validation is unavailable.' (dependencies/auth.py:114-128).
- Unusable shared classification AND at least one row omitting program_id: resolve_default() raises before any file is read — e.g. 422 'A valid academic program is required.', 'Program does not belong to the selected department or is archived.', 'BSCS requires a valid specialization.', 'BSIS does not accept a specialization.' WHOLE batch (routers/upload.py:1035-1038, services/catalog.py:90-109).
- Per-file rejections, folded into results[] while the request still returns 202: 422 'Title must contain between 5 and 300 characters'; 422 'Year must be a valid four-digit completion year'; 415 'Only PDF thesis files are accepted'; 415 'Upload MIME type must be application/pdf'; 400 'Empty file'; 413 'File exceeds the 25 MB limit'; 422 'File content is not a valid PDF'; 422 'Encrypted or password-protected PDFs are not accepted'; 422 'Malformed or unreadable PDF'; 422 'PDF must contain at least one page'; 422 'PDF exceeds the 500-page safety limit'. UI: flame badge 'Not accepted', progress 0, the message in Details, and the row joins rejectedRows so 'Retry N rejected' appears (UploadBatch.jsx:471-474, 740-744).
- Idempotency key reused with different bytes: reserve_upload_job raises errcode 22000, mapped per-file to 409 'Idempotency-Key was already used for another file' (routers/upload.py:809).
- Reserve RPC otherwise failing: per-file 503 'The durable upload queue is temporarily unavailable'; an empty RPC result: 503 'The durable upload queue did not reserve the submission'.
- Private-storage upload failing: compensating delete + _fail_staging_job (status failed, error 'The upload could not be queued safely. Please try again.', cleanup_status 'pending' iff the delete also failed), then per-file 503 'The private manuscript could not be staged safely'.
- Queue-transition failing with the job NOT advanced: same compensation, per-file 503 'The private manuscript could not be queued safely'. Advanced (lost response): no compensation, treated as success.
- Resubmitting a row whose earlier attempt left a FAILED job under the same idempotency key: reserve_upload_job returns created=false with status='failed', so the handler answers 202 with job_id set and status 'failed'. The UI treats any job_id as accepted, plants a terminal job, and the row shows 'Failed' with 'This submission is already tracked. Poll its existing job for progress.' — no submitError, therefore no Retry affordance, and claim_upload_job will never pick a failed row up. Permanent dead end for that manuscript in that batch (routers/upload.py:818-824, batchState.js:197-208).
- ClamAV unreachable: the worker loop refuses to claim at all and sleeps (workers/ingestion_worker.py:299-303). Every batch row stays 'Queued' / 'In queue' / 8% with no indication the worker is blocked, until the 30-minute poll ceiling. /health/worker returns 503 and evaluate_operations raises a critical 'scanner_unavailable' alert.
- Malware found: MalwareDetectedIngestionError (a PermanentIngestionError) -> not retryable -> fail_upload_job with the fixed public error 'The manuscript could not be processed safely.', cleanup_status 'pending'; a critical operational alert keyed malware:{job_id} is upserted and webhooked (workers/ingestion_worker.py:190-199).
- Staged-source integrity: sha256 mismatch, missing %PDF- magic, needs_pass, unreadable, or page count out of range -> PermanentIngestionError -> same fixed public error, attempt 1 of 3, no retry (services/ingestion.py:65-82).
- No extractable text / no clean indexable text / invalid chunk records / embedding count or dimension mismatch -> PermanentIngestionError -> same fixed public error (services/ingestion.py:126,150,152,162,164).
- Scanned pages with OCR unavailable and REQUIRE_OCR_FOR_SCANNED_PAGES on: PermanentIngestionError naming the page numbers internally, but the uploader still sees only 'The manuscript could not be processed safely.' (services/ingestion.py:127-137, services/upload_queue.py:139-143).
- Exact duplicate: DuplicateManuscriptIngestionError is the ONE class allowed to publish its own text, so the row's Details reads e.g. 'This manuscript was not indexed because it is an exact duplicate of "<title>" (<year>), which is already in the archive: all N passages matched at X% similarity.' Status 'Failed', flame badge (services/ingestion.py:178-183).
- Transient failure (network, or a 408/429/5xx anywhere on the __cause__/__context__ chain) below max_attempts: schedule_upload_retry -> status retry_wait, message 'A temporary service problem occurred. The job will retry automatically.', next_retry_at = now + 30/120/600s (index by attempt), raised to a Retry-After header up to 15 minutes. UI: gold 'Retrying' badge with Clock3 icon and 'Automatic retry k/3' (services/upload_queue.py:103-132, UploadBatch.jsx:537-539).
- Lease lost mid-pipeline (heartbeat RPC error or the lease moved): LeaseHeartbeat.update returns False -> _require_lease raises LeaseLostError -> the worker logs and abandons silently. The row stays 'processing' until claim_upload_job's sweep either re-claims it (attempts remaining) or fails it with 'Ingestion could not complete after the allowed retries.' / error 'Please submit the manuscript again later.'
- Staging row abandoned for 15 minutes (API died between reserve and queue): claim_upload_job's first sweep fails it with message 'Private source staging did not complete.' and error 'Please submit the manuscript again.', cleanup_status 'pending' when a source path exists.
- Cancellation landing between the index-stage heartbeat and the commit RPC: commit_upload_ingestion raises 'Upload cancellation was requested'. That surfaces as a plain postgrest APIError whose .code ('P0001') is not numeric, so is_retryable_ingestion_error returns False and the job is failed rather than cancelled — with the wrong public string 'The thesis could not be safely indexed after the allowed retries.' on attempt 1 of 3 (workers/ingestion_worker.py:200-204, services/upload_queue.py:83-100,139-143).
- Commit RPC response lost after PostgreSQL committed: the worker re-reads upload_jobs and papers by the deterministic shared id and accepts the commit when status=='completed', ingestion_status=='ready' and chunk_count matches; otherwise it re-raises (services/ingestion.py:249-274).
- GET /upload/jobs with nothing parseable: 422 'ids must list at least one upload job id'; over 50: 422 'ids may list at most 50 upload jobs'; DB error: 503 'Upload status is temporarily unavailable'.
- Five consecutive poll failures: refetchInterval returns false and a role=alert banner appears — 'The server could not confirm the batch status. No job was cancelled.' with a 'Resume status check' button (UploadBatch.jsx:838-840, 725-732).
- 30 minutes of polling: ceilingReached flips and the banner reads 'Status checking paused after 30 minutes. The worker keeps running.' (UploadBatch.jsx:836-837).
- A job vanishing from the poll response (expired server-side, or someone else's id): intended behaviour is three consecutive misses -> synthetic status 'expired' with 'This upload job has expired or is no longer visible.' In practice React Query's default structural sharing usually freezes the counter — see surprises.
- Cancel: unknown id or bad UUID -> 404 'Upload job not found'; someone else's job and not superadmin -> 403 'You cannot cancel this upload job'; already terminal -> 200 with message 'Upload is already <status>.'; missing RPC -> 503 'Upload cancellation requires the operations migration'; generic -> 503 'Upload cancellation is temporarily unavailable'. Success messages are 'Upload cancelled and private-source cleanup queued.' (immediate) or 'Cancellation requested. Processing will stop at the next safe checkpoint.' (cooperative). Failure toasts as 'Cancellation failed' + the detail (UploadBatch.jsx:999-1001).
- Cancelling more than ten rows of a twenty-file batch within a minute: the 11th POST /upload/jobs/{id}/cancel 429s, because cancellation shares rate_limit_upload with staging and extraction (routers/upload.py:1216).
- Normalized-catalog outage: _nested_catalog falls back to _legacy_catalog, which returns programs: [] for every department. Every batch row then fails validation with 'Select the academic program' against an empty <select> — unrecoverable from the UI. A separate departments-query failure shows the banner 'Department metadata is unavailable.' with a Retry button (routers/catalog.py:45-64, UploadBatch.jsx:1060-1065).
- Batch submitted where every file was rejected: still 202, still transitions to the ingesting step, allTerminal is true immediately, and the banner reads 'Batch finished with issues' with '0 of N manuscripts are now part of the semantic archive.' (UploadBatch.jsx:962-971, batchState.js:321-323).
- Network/timeout on POST /upload/batch itself (15-minute axios timeout): toast.error 'Batch upload failed' + apiErrorMessage; the wizard stays on the review step with the files intact and the same idempotency keys, so a retry is safe.
- Files lost across a refresh: restored rows have file:null, so any resubmit path short-circuits with toast.error 'Files unavailable' + 'Select the manuscripts again to resubmit them.' (UploadBatch.jsx:943-946).

### What gets persisted

- public.upload_jobs — one row per manuscript, keyed by the client-minted job uuid, unique on (owner_id, idempotency_key). Written by reserve_upload_job (staging/store/5), queue_upload_job (queued/queued/8), claim_upload_job (processing/download/10, attempt_count+1, lease_owner, lease_expires_at), every heartbeat_upload_job_control (stage/progress/message/lease), and one terminal writer: commit_upload_ingestion (completed/done/100 + paper_id + chunks + duplication), fail_upload_job (failed/error/100 + public error + failure_category), schedule_upload_retry (retry_wait + next_retry_at), request_upload_cancellation / finalize_upload_cancellation (cancelled). request_payload holds the whole classification payload and is read back by a trigger at paper-insert time. Rows carry expires_at = completion + 30 days.
- Supabase Storage, private 'pdfs' bucket: one object per file at uploads/{owner_id}/{job_id}/{sanitized_filename}, content-type application/pdf, upsert true. Never made public; papers.storage_path points at it.
- public.upload_job_events — one row per lifecycle transition, written by the record_upload_job_event AFTER INSERT/UPDATE trigger (event_type reserved | cancellation_requested | status_changed | stage_changed | cleanup_changed) with job_id, owner_id, department, status, stage, attempt_count, lease_owner and a jsonb_strip_nulls detail blob. FK is ON DELETE SET NULL so the 90-day audit trail outlives the 30-day job row.
- public.papers — one row per successfully ingested manuscript, inserted by commit_paper_ingestion with id == job_id, ingestion_status 'processing' then 'ready'. program_id / specialization_id / legacy_track / classification_status / thesis_category are NOT in that INSERT list; the BEFORE INSERT hydrate trigger fills them by re-reading upload_jobs.request_payload.
- public.paper_index_versions — one row per commit, carrying embedding_model, embedding_dimensions (768), preprocessing_version (document-v1), chunking_version (token-v1), tokenizer (cl100k_base), chunk_size_tokens 800, chunk_overlap_tokens 100, provenance_status 'verified', activated_at.
- public.chunks — one row per chunk: paper_id, chunk_index, content, vector(768) embedding, metadata jsonb (title/author/track/year/department/pages/section/token_count), page_start, page_end, section, index_version.
- public.activity_log — one 'paper_upload' row per successful ingest with paper_id, title, track, chunks, duplication_flagged, matched_chunk_percentage, highest_similarity. Best-effort; failures are logged only.
- public.papers.duplication_scan of OTHER papers — refresh_after_ingest rewrites the stored screening card of every other paper in the department that the new manuscript now beats. Post-commit and failure-swallowing.
- public.ingestion_workers — upserted by register_ingestion_worker on every housekeeping tick and every in-flight heartbeat (worker_id, state, scanner_status, version, current_job_id, last_seen_at); stop_ingestion_worker on shutdown.
- public.operational_alerts — deduplicated by dedupe_key, upserted/resolved by evaluate_operations, plus a per-job 'malware:{job_id}' critical alert raised directly by the worker.
- public.security_audit_events — one row per cancellation request (event_type 'upload_cancellation', actor_id, department, {job_id, outcome}).
- public.storage_cleanup_queue — one pending row whenever a staged-source delete fails, carrying operation 'rollback_upload', resource_path, job_id and only the exception class name.
- Browser sessionStorage['activeUploadBatch'] — {ownerId, rows:[{id, name, size, title, idempotencyKey, jobId}]}, rewritten on every rows change while on the ingesting step and removed once every row is terminal or on reset.

### Surprises

- The batch page never uses PipelineTimeline. It is imported only by the single-upload wizard (Upload.jsx:29,106). The batch page has a second, independent stage vocabulary: batchState.js:361 STAGE_LABELS ('Securing source', 'Chunking (800 tokens)', 'Embedding (768d)', 'Screening novelty (85%)') versus wizardSteps.js:18 PIPELINE_STAGES ('Secure source', 'Chunk (800 tokens)', 'Embed (768d)', 'Screen novelty (85%)'). Same seven backend keys, two sets of labels, neither importing the other. Only wizardSteps.js has the `store`/`queued`/'' -> download aliasing; batchState maps `store` and `download` both to 'Securing source' by duplicating the entry, and has no alias for `''`.
- The 3D scene's phase is derived from the batch's MEAN progress, not from any worker stage, and the bucket boundaries land exactly on the worker's own progress values — so it is off by roughly one phase almost everywhere. Worker stage/progress pairs are extract=20, chunk=40, embed=58, screen=72, index=88 (services/ingestion.py). The ternary at UploadBatch.jsx:668 is `progress<20?'download':progress<40?'malware_scan':progress<60?'extract':progress<80?'embed':'index'`. For a single-file batch that means: real malware_scan (16) shows 'PHASE 01 // SECURE SOURCE INGESTION'; real extract (20) shows 'PHASE 02 // BIOMETRIC MALWARE SCAN'; real chunk (40) shows 'PHASE 03 // PYMUPDF + TESSERACT OCR'; real embed (58) ALSO shows PHASE 03; real screen (72) shows 'PHASE 05 // 768D NEURAL SYNAPSE EMBED'. Only index is right. The ProgressTable directly underneath, on the same screen, prints the correct stage label from job.stage.
- Two of IngestScene3D's seven stage scenes are unreachable from the batch page. The ternary can only ever emit download, malware_scan, extract, embed and index, so StageChunking and StageNoveltyScreen — and their PHASE 04 / PHASE 06 HUD copy — never render in a batch. (They do render from the single wizard, which passes the real stage key.)
- The `stage` prop of IngestScene3D is dead at both call sites. normalizeStageKey(stageKey || stage) short-circuits, and both callers always pass a truthy stageKey (UploadBatch.jsx:668, PipelineTimeline.jsx:107). The strings 'Batch Ingestion' and 'Completed' are computed and never displayed anywhere.
- 'Concurrent Workers Active' (UploadBatch.jsx:680) is false in the default configuration. ingestion_concurrency defaults to 1 (config.py:136) and the README tells evaluation runs to leave it there. Backend staging is explicitly sequential too (routers/upload.py:1043).
- The comment justifying that sequential loop is misleading: 'staging them concurrently would hold every manuscript of the batch in memory at once'. Starlette's MultiPartParser has already spooled all N parts (each past 1MB going to the container's temp disk) before the handler body runs — that is precisely what BodySizeLimitMiddleware exists to bound (services/request_limits.py:1-23). The loop bounds resident `bytes`, not the request's footprint.
- The admin 'Upload history' tab shows papers, not jobs. It calls listPapers (UploadHistoryTab.jsx:39-42) and has no column for status, stage, progress, attempt, failure or cancellation — a failed or cancelled batch is simply invisible there. The only job listing is Operations, which Admin.jsx:133-135 appends for superadmin only. A department admin who runs a 20-file batch and closes the tab has no way to find out what happened to it.
- The 'RAG Ingestion Pipeline' diagram on that tab is entirely static: every PipelineNode is passed `active` as a bare literal (UploadHistoryTab.jsx:92-111), so the prop's inactive branch is dead code and the six nodes always glow.
- Missing-job expiry probably never fires in the wired path. applyJobStatuses increments row.missingPolls and flips to 'expired' at 3 (batchState.js:223-228), but the dispatch is gated on `useEffect(..., [polledJobs])` (UploadBatch.jsx:842-844) and TanStack Query v5's structural sharing is on by default (main.jsx:35-53 does not disable it). Once a job disappears, every subsequent poll returns a deeply-equal array, so the reference is preserved, the effect does not re-run, and the counter sticks at 1. It only advances while some OTHER job in the same poll is still changing. A batch down to its last, vanished job spins at 'Queued / In queue / 8%' until the 30-minute ceiling. The unit test passes because it dispatches the reducer directly with a changing array each time (batchState.test.js:296-316).
- Resubmitting a row under the same idempotency key after a staging failure is a permanent dead end. reserve_upload_job returns the existing FAILED row with created=false, the handler answers 202 with job_id set and status 'failed' plus the message 'This submission is already tracked. Poll its existing job for progress.', the UI counts that as accepted (batchState.js:197-208), the row shows Failed with no submitError and therefore no Retry button, and claim_upload_job will never re-pick a failed row. Nothing in the UI re-mints the idempotency key.
- A cancellation that lands in the window between the index-stage heartbeat and commit_upload_ingestion ends the job as FAILED, not cancelled, with the wrong copy. The RPC raises 'Upload cancellation was requested'; postgrest's APIError.code is 'P0001', which is not numeric, so is_retryable_ingestion_error returns False and fail_job writes 'The thesis could not be safely indexed after the allowed retries.' — on attempt 1 of 3, for a job the user deliberately cancelled (upload_queue.py:83-100,139-143; ingestion_worker.py:200-204).
- The single-upload wizard still has the sessionStorage leak the batch page fixed. batchState.js:380-398 documents a 2026-09-12 audit: 'activeUploadBatch' is now owner-stamped because sessionStorage survives sign-out and one reader's manuscript titles were rendering into the next reader's table. Upload.jsx:378-385 writes 'activeUploadJob' with {jobId, idempotencyKey, title} and no owner, and Upload.jsx:355-372 restores it on mount with no idea who is signed in.
- GET /upload/jobs carries no @limiter.limit decorator, unlike every sibling in the module — it falls under the 120/minute global default. The 2s poll spends a quarter of that budget for one page, shared with every other unlabelled endpoint the app calls.
- _last_event_map performs an unbounded read of upload_job_events for all polled job ids every 2 seconds, then throws away everything but the newest per job — and the field it produces, last_event_at, is never rendered by the batch page at all (routers/upload.py:1098-1114; UploadJobStatus.last_event_at is unused in UploadBatch.jsx).
- The worker's ingestion pipeline uses TWO different Supabase clients. run_worker creates its own and passes it down for claim/heartbeat/storage/commit, but screen_new_submission and refresh_after_ingest use the module-global `sb` imported from services.retriever -> dependencies.auth (services/novelty.py:28). The duplication screen therefore runs on a connection the worker did not create and cannot see.
- papers.content is never populated by this flow. commit_paper_ingestion inserts `p_paper ->> 'content'` (supabase_setup.sql:519) but services/ingestion.py's paper_data dict has no 'content' key, so every durably-ingested paper has a NULL content column.
- The academic classification of a paper is set by a trigger that re-reads a DIFFERENT table. commit_paper_ingestion's INSERT column list contains no program_id, specialization_id, legacy_track, classification_status or thesis_category; hydrate_paper_academic_classification looks them up from upload_jobs.request_payload WHERE id = new.id (migrations/20260819_thesis_category.sql:47-64). ingestion.py:198-205 puts them in paper_data anyway, and the comment there admits they are inert.
- claim_upload_job exists twice with materially different bodies. The base-schema version (supabase_setup.sql:1517) has no cancellation awareness at all; the migration version (20260724_operations_security.sql:351) adds the cancel_requested_at guards and the expired-lease-plus-cancel-requested sweep. Because both are `create or replace`, replaying supabase_setup.sql after the migration silently reverts cooperative cancellation. Same pattern for schedule_upload_retry, commit_upload_ingestion, claim_upload_cleanup and expire_upload_jobs.
- On a pre-operations schema the cancel button works and does nothing. heartbeat_job_control falls back to heartbeat_upload_job and hardcodes cancel_requested:false (upload_queue.py:62-73), so the worker never learns of the request; POST /upload/jobs/{id}/cancel itself returns 503 'Upload cancellation requires the operations migration', but can_cancel in the job status is still computed and the button is still rendered.
- Dead exports in batchState.js that only the test file consumes: batchRailMode (line 27) is never called by UploadBatch.jsx, which hides the stepper at the ingesting step instead; state.pollError and the 'set-poll-error' action (lines 107, 265) are never used by the batch page, which derives pollError locally from the React Query state (UploadBatch.jsx:834-840). Both live on in uploadState.js/Upload.jsx, which is presumably where they came from.
- MAX_BATCH_FILES = 20 is hardcoded in the frontend as a mirror of the server default (batchState.js:30-31). Lowering MAX_BATCH_FILES server-side gives the uploader a page that happily accepts 20 files and a 413 'A batch may contain at most N files' rejecting the whole batch.
- Cancellation shares the 10/minute upload rate limit (routers/upload.py:1216). Cancelling an 11-file run of a 20-file batch 429s partway through, and the UI's only recovery is a toast.
- POST /upload/batch takes a request-level program_id/specialization_id/track that the current client never sends (api.js:292-297, asserted absent by e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:644). They survive as the documented fallback for a pre-per-row client, and _ClassificationCache.resolve_default() still runs the extra catalog round trip for them whenever any row omits its own program.
- If the normalized-catalog migration is absent or its read fails, _nested_catalog degrades to _legacy_catalog, which returns `programs: []` for every department (routers/catalog.py:45-64). The batch review step then demands 'Select the academic program' on every student row against an empty dropdown, with no way forward from the UI.

**Additional notes returned by the reader** (fields outside the schema, preserved verbatim)


**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 6 correction(s), 5 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** Step 46: "The Canvas is aria-hidden, so none of the phase copy is exposed to assistive tech."
  **Actual:** False, and it makes the off-by-one phase bug worse rather than harmless. Only the `<Canvas>` carries aria-hidden (rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/IngestScene3D.jsx:733). The phase copy — `cfg.badge` ("PHASE 02 // BIOMETRIC MALWARE SCAN") and `cfg.spec` — is rendered in a plain sibling DOM overlay OUTSIDE the Canvas at IngestScene3D.jsx:749-766, carrying only `pointer-events-none`. It is in the accessibility tree and is read out, so the mislabelled phase reaches assistive tech. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/three/IngestScene3D.jsx:733 (aria-hidden) vs :749-766 (HUD overlay)`)
- **Wrong:** Surprise: "The `stage` prop of IngestScene3D is dead at both call sites … (UploadBatch.jsx:668, PipelineTimeline.jsx:107)." And step 45's parenthetical crediting the single wizard's scene to PipelineTimeline.
  **Actual:** There are THREE call sites, and the one cited is dead code. PipelineTimeline.jsx:104 is gated on `{!hide3D && (…)}` at :95, and its only consumer passes `hide3D`: `<PipelineTimeline job={job} hide3D />` (Upload.jsx:106). So that call site never executes. The live second call site the trace missed entirely is Upload.jsx:73, inside `IngestingPanel`, which passes `stageKey={activeStage?.key || 'download'}` from `stageView(job)` — that is what actually renders StageChunking/StageNoveltyScreen. The conclusion (`stage` is dead) still holds at all three, since every caller passes a truthy stageKey. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:73 and :106; src/components/upload/PipelineTimeline.jsx:95,104; src/pages/UploadBatch.jsx:665`)
- **Wrong:** Step 45 / surprise 2: "The ternary at UploadBatch.jsx:668 is `progress<20?'download':progress<40?'malware_scan':progress<60?'extract':progress<80?'embed':'index'`."
  **Actual:** Incomplete quotation. The expression is `stageKey={finished ? 'index' : (progress < 20 ? … )}` — a `finished` short-circuit wraps the five-way ternary. The off-by-one mapping the trace derives is correct for a live batch, and PHASE 01/02/03/03/05/07 for malware_scan/extract/chunk/embed/screen/index all check out against PHASE_CONFIGS (IngestScene3D.jsx:23-65), but the trace prints the ternary as if it were the whole prop. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/UploadBatch.jsx:668`)
- **Wrong:** Step 31: "Three housekeeping sweeps first — staging older than 15 min -> failed 'staging_timeout' …"
  **Actual:** The migration version of that first sweep additionally requires `cancel_requested_at is null`, which the base-schema version does not. A staging row whose owner already requested cancellation is left for the second sweep rather than being failed with 'staging_timeout'. The trace states the predicate unqualified. (`rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260724_operations_security.sql:366-367 vs rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1548-1558`)
- **Wrong:** Step 35: "issues ONE match_chunks RPC per chunk (match_count 1, threshold 0.30 -> settings.duplication_threshold 0.85 …)"
  **Actual:** Reads as if two thresholds are involved. Only one value is passed: `threshold = settings.duplication_threshold` (0.85) is handed straight to `match_threshold`. The 0.30 retrieval threshold never appears in this call path. (`rag-thesis-backend/services/novelty.py:349,355`)
- **Wrong:** Step 30: "run_worker builds its own Supabase client, registers in ingestion_workers, and loops."
  **Actual:** There is no registration before the loop. `run_worker` constructs `_Housekeeping` (ingestion_worker.py:275) but the first `register_ingestion_worker` RPC only fires from inside the loop body, on the first `house.tick()` call. (`rag-thesis-backend/workers/ingestion_worker.py:275,298 → :224-234`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- A SECOND before-insert trigger on `papers` that the trace never names, and which can abort the commit. `hydrate_paper_academic_classification` is deliberately attached as `a_papers_hydrate_academic_classification` so it sorts first (migrations/20260725_normalized_academic_catalog.sql:110-112); `papers_validate_academic_classification` then fires on the same INSERT (same file, :138-140, function at :114-136) and re-checks the values the hydrate trigger just copied out of `upload_jobs.request_payload` against the live catalog — raising 'Unknown or archived program', 'Specialization does not belong to program', '% requires a specialization' or '% does not accept a specialization'. A program archived between staging and the worker's commit therefore aborts `commit_paper_ingestion` at stage index/88, and the uploader sees only the generic 'The thesis could not be safely indexed after the allowed retries.' Step 38 describes the hydrate trigger as if it were the only one.
- `BatchRow._blank_is_absent`, the field_validator that makes the whole request-level fallback reachable. The client sends `program_id: row.program_id` and `specialization_id: row.specialization_id` as EMPTY STRINGS for an unset row (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/UploadBatch.jsx:954-955), and a `mode='before'` validator coerces `''` -> `None` (rag-thesis-backend/models.py:176-184). Without it, `_parse_batch_rows`' orphan-specialization check and `_ClassificationCache.context`'s `if program_id` key selection would both behave differently. The trace's steps 17 and 19 assume the None-shaped values without saying where they come from.
- Three pre-insert guards inside `commit_paper_ingestion` that step 37 skips, one of which is the real idempotency floor under the lost-response recovery of step 36/failureModes: `'Paper chunk count does not match staged chunks'` when `p_paper->>'chunk_count'` disagrees with `jsonb_array_length(p_chunks)`; an early `return v_paper_id` when the paper is already `ready` with matching chunk counts; and `'Paper ingestion ID already exists in an incomplete state'` for a half-written papers row. Step 37 begins at the papers INSERT.
- `_stage_and_queue_one` discards the locally computed storage path and uses the one the RPC returned: `source_path = str(reserved['stored_source_path'])` overwrites the `uploads/{user_id}/{job_id}/{safe_filename}` it built, before the storage PUT at :827. On a re-reserve of a row still in 'staging' under the same idempotency key with a different filename, the bytes are written to the FIRST reservation's path. Step 24 describes the freshly computed path as the upload target.
- `GET /upload/status/{job_id}` (rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1142-1163) — the single-job sibling that shares `_job_status_model` and `_last_event_at`, is likewise undecorated by `@limiter.limit`, and maps an unparseable uuid to 404 via `is_invalid_identifier` rather than 503. The batch page does not use it, but it is the endpoint the single wizard polls, and it sits directly beside the `/jobs` read the trace does cover.

## 8. On-demand novelty / duplication scan

<a id="8-novelty-scan"></a>

Flow key: `novelty-scan`

### Overview

Topic-novelty / duplication scan: `POST /duplication/scan` from the React Novelty page, traced through auth, the per-chunk `match_chunks` loop, concentration scoring, the Gemini verdict, the `scan_history` row, the rendered result and the JSON report — then contrasted with the ingest-time `screen_new_submission` screen inside the worker pipeline (and the third, unrelated `check_topic_duplication` path used only by chat).

**Trigger:** A faculty/admin/superadmin user drops or picks a `.pdf` or `.txt` file in the Novelty Check dropzone (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Novelty.jsx:84` file input / `:90` drop handler), which calls `runScan` (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Novelty.jsx:377`). Secondary triggers on the same page: mount-time `GET /duplication/history`, `POST /duplication/chat` follow-ups, and the two DELETE endpoints.

**Actors, in order:** Browser: Novelty.jsx + ProtectedRoute + AuthContext (canScan) -> axios instance in src/api.js (JWT interceptor, 180 s timeout, 401 refresh, privileged-MFA reporter) -> Vite dev proxy (vite.config.js:99) or nginx in the container -> BodySizeLimitMiddleware (services/request_limits.py) -> SlowAPIMiddleware + limiter (services/rate_limiting.py, 5/minute) -> FastAPI multipart parser (runs BEFORE dependencies) -> dependencies/auth.py: require_novelty_access -> get_current_user -> _ensure_approved_account -> get_user_role -> _require_privileged_mfa -> dependencies/auth.py: resolve_effective_department -> get_user_scope -> services/malware.scan_pdf (ClamAV INSTREAM) + PyMuPDF structural check -> services/document_processor.extract_document (PyMuPDF + tesserocr OCR, or utf-8 decode for .txt) + redact_pii -> services/chunker.split_document + validate_chunk_records (800/100 tokens, cl100k_base) -> services/embedder.embed_texts -> services/gemini_pool.run(EMBED) -> models/gemini-embedding-001, 768d, batches of 64 -> Postgres RPC public.match_chunks (hnsw.ef_search=100, called once per chunk with match_count=1) -> services/novelty: percent, concentration_percentage, verdict_for_concentration -> services/gemini_pool.arun(VERDICT) -> gemini-3.5-flash-lite (gateway first if LLM_BASE_URL set) -> Supabase tables: papers (read), scan_history (insert/select/update/delete), activity_log (insert) -> --- ingest-time contrast --- -> workers/ingestion_worker.process_claimed_job + LeaseHeartbeat -> services/ingestion.process_ingestion_job (stage 'screen', progress 72) -> services/novelty.screen_new_submission / aggregate_matches / is_exact_duplicate -> RPC commit_upload_ingestion (writes papers.duplication_scan, upload_jobs.duplication) -> services/novelty.refresh_after_ingest -> rescan_indexed_paper / apply_new_paper_to_matches -> store_rescan

**Latency:** Client budget is the 180 s axios instance timeout (src/api.js:10) with no per-call override for the scan, so a long manuscript can time out in the browser while the server completes and commits the scan_history row — the reviewer then sees 'Scan failed' for a scan that exists in the sidebar. Server cost is dominated by two serial stages: embedding in batches of 64 (services/embedder.py:49) and then ONE match_chunks RPC per chunk, sequentially, each with hnsw.ef_search=100 (routers/duplication.py:134). Nothing is batched or parallelised, so cost is linear in chunk count with a full network round trip per chunk. The Gemini verdict adds up to gemini_timeout_seconds=60 s with max_retries=1, and the gateway hop (when LLM_BASE_URL is set) can add another full timeout before falling back to Google — the gateway is skipped while cooling for exactly this reason (services/gemini_pool.py:228). Every blocking step is wrapped in asyncio.to_thread specifically because the handler is `async def`; the comment at duplication.py:218 records that without it one scan froze /health and the readiness probe for its whole duration. The frontend dropzone copy warns 'This can take a minute for long drafts' (Novelty.jsx:111). The ingest-time screen runs the identical loop but inside the worker process under a LeaseHeartbeat at stage 'screen'/progress 72, so its latency costs a lease renewal rather than an HTTP timeout; refresh_after_ingest afterwards is O(new paper chunks x each other paper's chunks) in pure Python, done post-commit and best-effort.

Otherwise the last 5 turns are fence-escaped, the stored excerpt pairs are HTML-escaped into the prompt, and gemini_pool.run(VERDICT) is called synchronously; failure -> 502 'The AI reviewer is temporarily unavailable. | 39 | worker | CONTRAST, ingest path. After download -> SHA-256 -> ClamAV -> extract -> chunk -> embed, the worker sets stage 'screen' at progress 72 under an active lease and calls screen_new_submission(embeddings, department). | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:167` |
| 40 | service | screen_new_submission runs the SAME per-chunk match_chunks loop at the SAME 0.85 threshold with match_count=1 and the same provenance params, but keeps only {paper_id, similarity} per chunk — no excerpt text is retained at all. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/novelty.py:347` |

The job ends status='failed' and the manuscript is never indexed. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:173 -> services/upload_queue.py:135 and :83` |
| 43 | db | Otherwise duplication_scan rides inside p_paper into the commit_upload_ingestion RPC, landing in papers.duplication_scan and (via the same RPC) upload_jobs.duplication. The ingestion commits flagged — screening never blocks anything short of verbatim. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/ingestion.py:212 -> supabase_setup.sql:514 and :1707` |
| 44 | worker | After the commit, refresh_after_ingest folds the new paper into every OTHER ready paper's screening in the same department. It bypasses match_chunks entirely, pulling raw chunk vectors and scoring cosine in Python (_best_archive_match), keeping only each chunk's single closest neighbour so one new paper can change a chunk only by beating what is there. Papers with no usable chunk_matches table pay for a full rescan_indexed_paper once. Results are nested under duplication_scan.rescan by store_rescan so the at-upload figures are never overwritten. It swallows every exception — a stale screening must not fail a committed ingestion. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/novelty.py:446 and :319 and :416 -> services/ingestion.py:298` |
| 45 | frontend | Ingest-time UI consequence: on job completion the Upload page toasts 'Potential duplication detected' when status.duplication.flagged, and IngestOutcome renders UploadScreening (counts only, no excerpts) plus a link into Novelty Review. A DuplicateManuscriptIngestionError failure is detected by regex /exact duplicate/i on job.error and gets its own 'Already in the archive' outcome screen. The Archive grid/detail reads currentScreening(paper.duplication_scan), preferring the nested rescan over the at-upload layer. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:320 -> src/components/upload/IngestOutcome.jsx:103 and :160 -> src/pages/Archive.jsx:303` |

### Branches

- Role: admin/superadmin bypass the novelty feature toggle entirely but must satisfy _require_privileged_mfa (aal2) when REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA is on; faculty/student are allowed only by an explicit stored `novelty: true` (dependencies/auth.py:419).
- Department: superadmin may send a `department` form field, validated against the departments table (422 'Unknown department.' if absent); everyone else is pinned to their profile department and gets 403 'You can only search your assigned department.' if they send a different one. The frontend only ever sends the field for a superadmin (Novelty.jsx:381).
- File type: .pdf takes the PyMuPDF structural gate (encryption, page count) + magic-byte check; .txt skips all of it and only gets a MIME check plus ClamAV, then is utf-8-decoded with errors='ignore' into a single page with page_number=None (routers/duplication.py:232, services/document_processor.py:304).
- Zero matches -> hardcoded '### Advisory: Clear' markdown, no LLM call, top_matches=[] (routers/duplication.py:308).
- Matches exist but every matched paper was deleted mid-scan -> '### Advisory: matched studies no longer in the archive', no LLM call, matched_chunks=[] (routers/duplication.py:345).
- Matches exist and survive -> Gemini verdict prompt over the top-5 excerpt pairs of the single most-matched paper (routers/duplication.py:361).
- Verdict model fails -> deterministic '### Advisory explanation unavailable' fallback, still HTTP 200 (routers/duplication.py:409).
- Gateway: when LLM_BASE_URL is set the VERDICT call goes to the OpenAI-compatible gateway first and ANY failure falls back to Google; on Google hops only capacity errors rotate to reserve keys (services/gemini_pool.py:235).
- Follow-up chat: prohibited_reason hit -> 200 with REFUSAL_MESSAGE, persisted to chat_log; otherwise a VERDICT call whose failure is 502 (routers/duplication.py:483 / :541).
- Verdict bands: concentration <25 clear, 25-70 review_suggested, >=70 high_overlap. `exact_duplicate` is reachable only on the ingest path — the on-demand scan calls verdict_for_concentration with the default exact_duplicate=False, and scan_history's CHECK constraint would reject the value anyway (services/novelty.py:92, supabase_setup.sql:705).
- Ingest screen: exact_duplicate (all chunks >= 0.999) -> job fails, nothing indexed; anything else -> committed flagged (services/ingestion.py:173).
- Ingest refresh: a paper with a usable chunk_matches table takes the incremental apply_new_paper_to_matches path; one without pays for a full rescan_indexed_paper (services/novelty.py:497).

### Failure modes

- Body > 27 MB declared: 413 JSON {'detail':'Request body exceeds the 27 MB limit'} from BodySizeLimitMiddleware before auth (services/request_limits.py:127). Chunked overrun surfaces as 400 'There was an error parsing the body'.
- Body between 25 and 27 MB: 413 'File exceeds the 25 MB limit' from the handler (routers/duplication.py:229).
- 6th scan in a minute: 429 with body {"error": "Rate limit exceeded: 5 per 1 minute"}. apiErrorMessage reads only `detail` then `message`, so the UI shows the generic 'Scan failed — Something went wrong. Please try again.' and loses the real reason (.venv slowapi/extension.py:81 vs src/lib/apiErrors.js:10).
- Expired token: 401 -> axios interceptor does one silent supabase.auth.refreshSession() and replays; if that fails it signs out and hard-navigates to loginPathWithNext(current) (src/api.js:127).
- Privileged account without aal2: 403 with the exact string 'Multi-factor authentication is required for privileged access.' The interceptor matches the lowercase prefix and fires reportPrivilegedMfaRequired, raising the PrivilegedMfaGate prompt; the promise still rejects so Novelty also shows toast.error('Scan failed', {description: <that string>}) (dependencies/auth.py:224, src/lib/privilegedMfa.js:16, src/api.js:154).
- Faculty with novelty disabled (or an unreachable/absent system_settings row): 403 'Faculty or administrator privileges are required for this action.' -> toast.error('Scan failed', ...).
- Unapproved/pending/rejected profile: 403 'This account is pending approval or has been rejected.' (dependencies/auth.py:151).
- profiles read fails inside get_user_scope: 503 'User department validation is unavailable.' (dependencies/auth.py:95). Note get_user_role's own failure path instead degrades silently to 'student', which for a faculty account turns into the novelty 403 above.
- Wrong extension: 415 'Only PDF or UTF-8 text manuscripts are accepted'. Wrong .txt MIME: 415 'Text manuscripts must use a plain-text MIME type'. .pdf without the %PDF- magic: 422 'File content is not a valid PDF'.
- Encrypted PDF: 422 'Encrypted or password-protected PDFs are not accepted'. Unparseable: 422 'Malformed or unreadable PDF'. 0 pages: 422 'PDF must contain at least one page'. >500 pages: 422 'PDF exceeds the 500-page safety limit'.
- ClamAV hit: 422 'The uploaded manuscript failed malware scanning'. ClamAV unreachable while enabled: 503 'Malware scanning is unavailable. Please retry in a moment.' (fail closed, matching ingestion).
- Extraction throws: 400 'Could not process the uploaded manuscript'. Extraction returns only whitespace: 400 'Could not extract text from file'. All chunks filtered as noise: 400 'The document contained no clean, indexable text'.
- Embedding provider returns the wrong count: 502 'The embedding provider returned an incomplete scan result'. Wrong vector length: 502 'The embedding provider returned an invalid scan result'.
- UNHANDLED: validate_chunk_records raises ValueError ('Chunk N exceeds the 800-token limit' / 'An empty chunk was produced' / 'Incomplete page range' / 'Invalid page range') and _clean_chunk_records does not catch it, so it escapes asyncio.to_thread as a bare 500 Internal Server Error. services/ingestion.py:149 catches exactly this and turns it into a clean permanent failure — the scan path does not (routers/duplication.py:110).
- UNHANDLED: any Postgres/PostgREST error inside the per-chunk match_chunks loop propagates as a 500. Unlike every other vector call in services/retriever.py, this loop is not wrapped in retry_transient, so one transient blip aborts a scan that may already have made hundreds of round trips (routers/duplication.py:135).
- Gemini verdict failure (all keys + gateway): caught, 200 with the deterministic fallback summary.
- scan_history insert succeeds but PostgREST returns no representation: 502 with a message telling the reviewer to reopen the scan from history (routers/duplication.py:453).
- Follow-up chat on someone else's / a deleted scan: 404 'Scan not found'. Blank or >4000-char question: 422 from DuplicationChatReq. Model failure: 502 'The AI reviewer is temporarily unavailable. Please try again later.' -> the optimistic user bubble is rolled back with chatLog.slice(0,-1) (src/pages/Novelty.jsx:150).
- History fetch failure: the sidebar renders a role=alert panel with the server's own detail text, and a Retry button only when isRetryableFailure (status 0 or >=500) — a 403 therefore shows the refusal with no retry (src/pages/Novelty.jsx:462).
- Delete with a malformed uuid: 404 'Scan record not found' via is_invalid_identifier; any other DB error re-raises to 500 (routers/duplication.py:588).
- Ingest-time, exact duplicate: job status 'failed' with the composed public error; the Upload page shows 'Already in the archive' plus a View-archive button (services/ingestion.py:178, IngestOutcome.jsx:160).
- Ingest-time, refresh_after_ingest failure: swallowed per-paper and globally; the commit stands with stale neighbour screenings (services/novelty.py:485 and :514).

### What gets persisted

- public.scan_history — one row per scan: user_id, filename (sanitized), department (server-resolved), duplication_percentage AND matched_chunk_percentage (both the same coverage number), highest_similarity, matched_chunk_count, total_chunks, verdict_level (CHECK-constrained to clear|review_suggested|high_overlap), top_matches jsonb (top 3 papers with match_count + similarity), verdict_summary text (markdown), matched_chunks jsonb (top-5 uploaded/archived 320-char excerpt pairs for the PRIMARY paper only — never returned to any client), chat_log jsonb, created_at. RLS enabled with all grants revoked from anon/authenticated; only the service role touches it. (routers/duplication.py:418, supabase_setup.sql:690)
- public.scan_history.chat_log — rewritten in place on every follow-up turn, trimmed to the newest 20 entries, including on the refusal path (routers/duplication.py:77).
- public.activity_log — 'novelty_scan' with filename, rounded coverage, highest_similarity, counts, verdict_level and flagged = coverage>0; plus 'duplication_chat_blocked', 'scan_history_delete', 'scan_history_clear'. department is NOT passed, so it is back-filled from the caller's own profile (routers/duplication.py:437, services/activity.py:19).
- NOT persisted by the scan path: the concentration figure the verdict is graded on, the threshold used, and any archive_size — all three exist on the ingest-time record and have no column here.
- --- ingest path ---
- public.papers.duplication_scan jsonb — the full at-upload screening including top_paper_percentage, threshold, matched_papers[] with enriched citation metadata, most_similar_paper, screened_at, archive_size and scan_scope='at_upload' (services/novelty.py:159, supabase_setup.sql:514).
- public.papers.duplication_scan.rescan — a nested, later screening written by store_rescan; the at-upload layer is never overwritten because it is the only record of what the archive held on acceptance day (services/novelty.py:416).
- public.upload_jobs.duplication jsonb — the same screening copied by commit_upload_ingestion, which is what the Upload page polls (supabase_setup.sql:1707).
- Nothing is written at all when the ingest screen finds an exact duplicate: the job row goes to status='failed' via fail_upload_job and no papers/chunks rows are created (services/ingestion.py:178).

### Surprises

- check_topic_duplication is NOT in this flow. Despite the name and the SQL comment 'query-time 85% novelty guard', that RPC is called only by routers/chat.py via services/retriever.check_topic_duplication (retriever.py:925, chat.py:1971). The novelty scanner uses match_chunks once per chunk instead. There are three distinct duplication implementations in the repo — the scan loop, screen_new_submission, and the chat guard — and they share only the 0.85 constant.
- The headline number is NOT the one stored under 'duplication_percentage'. The server writes coverage into both duplication_percentage and matched_chunk_percentage, but grades the verdict on concentration (largest single paper's chunk share). Concentration is never persisted and never sent; the browser re-derives it from top_matches[].match_count in scanMetrics (utils.js:103). Every UI figure the reader reacts to — the ProgressRing, the 'Closest single thesis' tile, both toasts, the history-row percentage — is a client-side derivation from two other fields.
- The ProgressRing colour bands disagree with the verdict bands. The ring is red at >=60 and gold at >=30 (ProgressRing.jsx:23); the verdict is high_overlap at >=70 and review_suggested at >=25 (novelty.py:80). So 25-30% concentration shows a green ring beside a 'Review suggested' badge, and 60-70% shows a red ring beside the same 'Review suggested' badge. The history row was explicitly fixed for exactly this class of disagreement (Novelty.jsx:499) but the ring above it was not.
- The downloaded report omits the graded figure entirely. buildNoveltyReport exports matched_chunk_coverage_percent and verdict_code side by side, with no concentration value anywhere — so the JSON hands a reviewer a verdict and a number that did not produce it (report.js:18).
- report.js reads scan.threshold, which the API never sends. `threshold` is not a scan_history column and is not in _PUBLIC_SCAN_FIELDS, so normalizePercent(scan?.threshold || 85) is a dead expression that always yields 85. The only place `threshold: 85` exists is the Playwright fixture (e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:403), which is why no test catches it.
- require_novelty_access does not use the shared _feature_enabled helper. It reads get_role_features() raw and demands features[role]['novelty'] is True (auth.py:428). get_role_features returns {} when system_settings is unreachable or the role_features row is absent. Meanwhile GET /settings/features returns DEFAULT_FEATURES in the same situation (settings.py:60) and the browser's canUseFeature falls back to the same defaults (permissions.js:26). Result: with no role_features row, a faculty member sees the Novelty nav, passes ProtectedRoute, and gets a 403 on every scan. chat and archive go through _feature_enabled and do not have this hole.
- The exact_duplicate verdict is unreachable on the on-demand path. verdict_for_concentration is called without the second argument (duplication.py:304), and scan_history's CHECK constraint only permits clear|review_suggested|high_overlap anyway (supabase_setup.sql:705). Scanning a verbatim copy of an already-archived thesis reports 'High overlap', not 'Exact duplicate' — the opposite of what uploading the same file does.
- A partially deleted match set silently desynchronises the verdict from the number. top_papers_json is built only from papers that still exist (duplication.py:327). If the #1 paper is deleted but #2 survives, verdict_level was computed from #1's count while the browser's concentration is derived from #2's. If ALL are gone, top_matches=[] so the UI shows 0.00% concentration under a 'High overlap—faculty review required' badge.
- The per-chunk search is one serial RPC round trip per chunk with no batching and no retry wrapper. A 300-chunk manuscript issues 300 sequential match_chunks calls, each running an HNSW probe with ef_search=100. The code comments that batching was 'deliberately deferred because that would alter a scored pipeline stage' (duplication.py:126). Every other vector call in the codebase goes through retry_transient; these two loops (duplication.py:135 and novelty.py:353) do not.
- GET /duplication/history and POST /duplication/scan return different shapes. The history field list includes user_id (duplication.py:555); the _PUBLIC_SCAN_FIELDS allowlist deliberately drops it (duplication.py:92). Both feed the same activeScan state, so whether a scan carries user_id depends on whether it was just run or clicked out of the sidebar. The allowlist's own docstring cites publishing user_id as the flaw it was fixing.
- The scan is never category-scoped. retrieval_provenance_params() returns only model and dimensions, so p_thesis_category defaults to null and a student proposal is compared against faculty theses too — while the chat path does pass thesis_category when a filter is active (retriever.py:948).
- A .txt upload gets almost no content validation. There is no magic-byte check, so PDF bytes sent as application/octet-stream with a .txt name go down the plain-text branch, are utf-8-decoded with errors='ignore', and typically die at the 15% non-alphanumeric noise rule as 400 'The document contained no clean, indexable text' rather than as a type error.
- The activity_log department for a superadmin cross-department scan is wrong. scan_history records effective_department, but log_activity receives no department and back-fills the caller's own profile department (duplication.py:437, activity.py:19-29), so the audit trail attributes a CCSICT-superadmin scan of another college to CCSICT.
- The follow-up chat only ever sees one archived thesis. matched_chunks stores primary_pairs_saved — the top-5 pairs for the single highest-ranked paper — so asking 'which chapter overlaps the most?' about the #2 or #3 listed match is answered with no evidence for it at all (duplication.py:363, :430).
- sanitize_filename's docstring says the scan endpoint needs force_suffix left None 'because it accepts both PDF and TXT manuscripts' (filenames.py:17), but the scan endpoint passes force_suffix=suffix (duplication.py:243). The comment describes a caller that no longer exists.
- Rate-limit keying degrades to IP when SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET is unset (rate_limiting.py:14). In that configuration the 5/minute scan budget is shared by every authenticated user behind one campus NAT — and _token_aal has a parallel dependency on the same setting for MFA assurance.
- Two DELETE endpoints are rate-limited at 20/minute but GET /duplication/history is not decorated at all and rides the global 120/minute default — the read is 6x cheaper to hammer than the delete.
- errors(400, 413, 415, 422, 502, 503) on /scan omits 401, 403 and 429, all of which the route actually produces, so the OpenAPI schema understates the failure surface (duplication.py:210).

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 5 correction(s), 10 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** surprises: "The only place `threshold: 85` exists is the Playwright fixture (e2e/critical-flows.spec.js:403), which is why no test catches it."
  **Actual:** False as stated. A second fixture sits directly beside the file: rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/novelty/report.test.js:11 also sets `threshold: 85` on the scan object it passes to buildNoveltyReport. The conclusion survives (neither test asserts on `threshold_percent`, so the dead `scan?.threshold || 85` is still uncaught), but the stated reason is wrong — it is not that only an E2E fixture supplies the field, it is that no assertion anywhere reads the output. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/novelty/report.test.js:11 and :27-30 vs rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/novelty/report.js:19`)
- **Wrong:** surprises: "require_novelty_access does not use the shared _feature_enabled helper... chat and archive go through _feature_enabled and do not have this hole." Framed as an accidental oversight unique to novelty.
  **Actual:** Two problems. (a) The divergence is documented as deliberate policy, not an oversight: _feature_enabled's own docstring says "`get_role_features` returns `{}` when `system_settings` is unreachable, and the two guards below treat that as a refusal. That is right for `upload` and `novelty`, which default to False: failing closed matches the policy." (b) require_upload_access uses the identical raw `features = get_role_features(); if role in features and features[role].get('upload') is True` pattern, so novelty is not the only guard that behaves this way. The observable consequence the trace describes (faculty sees the nav, passes ProtectedRoute, eats a 403) is real and correctly traced — only the framing as an unintended hole is wrong. (`rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:350-366 (docstring), :428-434 (novelty), :441-447 (upload)`)
- **Wrong:** surprises: "The allowlist's own docstring cites publishing user_id as the flaw it was fixing."
  **Actual:** The docstring explicitly annotates user_id as NOT the flaw: "The previous denylist excluded only `matched_chunks`, so it published `user_id` (harmless — always the caller's own) and would have published any column added later by default." The flaw it names is default-publishing future columns. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/duplication.py:99-107`)
- **Wrong:** step 37: "the last 5 turns are fence-escaped"
  **Actual:** `for msg in chat_log[-5:]` iterates the last 5 chat_log *entries*, and each exchange writes two entries (one 'user', one 'ai'), so the prompt carries 2.5 exchanges, not 5 turns. The trace reads _CHAT_LOG_TURNS = 20 correctly as 20 entries in the same step, so the two readings are inconsistent with each other. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/duplication.py:500 and :74/:87`)
- **Wrong:** failureModes: "The interceptor matches the lowercase prefix and fires reportPrivilegedMfaRequired"
  **Actual:** It is a substring test, not a prefix test: `detail.toLowerCase().includes(PRIVILEGED_MFA_MARKER)`. (The file's own comment at :13 says "prefix", which is where the trace picked it up — the comment is also wrong.) (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.js:22 vs :13`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- A THIRD unhandled 500 on the scan path, alongside the two the trace found. `scan_history.department` carries FK `scan_history_department_fkey` -> departments(name), while a non-superadmin's department is returned straight out of profiles with no validation at all (only the superadmin branch validates against the departments table). A profile whose department is not a departments row therefore passes auth, pays for extraction + embedding + N match_chunks round trips + the Gemini verdict, and dies on the final INSERT with an uncaught PostgREST error. (profiles.department has a matching `not valid` FK, so this only bites rows written before it was added.) — supabase_setup.sql:807-810, dependencies/auth.py:119, routers/duplication.py:433-435
- resolve_effective_department has a 503 the failureModes list omits entirely: 'Department validation is unavailable.' when the departments lookup itself throws for a superadmin. The trace lists only the 422 'Unknown department.' — dependencies/auth.py:122-126
- get_user_scope raises two more 403s with strings distinct from _ensure_approved_account's, both reachable from the exact handler line the trace anchors at step 12: 'This account is not approved for application access.' (status != approved) and 'A valid user profile is required for department-scoped access.' (no profile row at all) — dependencies/auth.py:82-86 and :99-102
- _ensure_approved_account can also 503 with 'Account validation is temporarily unavailable.' when the profiles read throws; the trace lists only its 403 — dependencies/auth.py:145-150
- The partial-deletion desync corrupts the stored EVIDENCE too, not just the displayed number. `primary_match = top_papers_json[0]` is the top *surviving* paper, so when the #1 paper is deleted mid-scan the excerpt pairs persisted into matched_chunks (and therefore every follow-up chat answer) describe #2, while verdict_level was graded on #1's chunk count — routers/duplication.py:361-365 and :430
- The downloaded report also drops `match_count`. matched_studies[] maps only title/authors/year/track/similarity, so the JSON does not merely omit the concentration figure — it strips the one field a reader would need to re-derive it, which the browser itself uses. The export is unreconstructable, not just incomplete — rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/novelty/report.js:27-33
- scanMetrics has fallback branches the trace does not describe: largestMatchCount prefers `matched_papers` (the ingest shape) over `top_matches`; concentration falls back to `normalizePercent(record.top_paper_percentage)` when chunk counts are missing; and `verdict` falls back to `matchedChunks === 0 ? 'clear' : 'review_suggested'` when verdict_level is absent — rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/utils.js:73-77, :103-105, :108
- `errors()` cannot declare 429 even if someone wanted to: _DESCRIPTIONS has entries only for 400/401/403/404/409/413/415/422/500/502/503, so errors(429) would raise KeyError at import. The trace's point about the understated OpenAPI surface stands, but the 401/403 omission and the 429 omission have different causes — rag-thesis-backend/routers/openapi_responses.py:11-23
- _persist_turn writes back with `.eq('id', scan_id)` and no user_id filter, unlike the two DELETE routes whose own comment ("owner-scoped on the read *and* on the write... so a guessed id cannot remove another account's row even if the check above were ever reordered away") states the opposite posture for the same table. Safe today only because the select at :476 was owner-scoped — routers/duplication.py:88 vs :569-575 and :598
- Middleware execution order detail the trace's step 8 half-covers: registration is SlowAPI -> GZip -> security_headers -> BodySizeLimit -> CORS, and Starlette reverses it, so the 413 written by _send_too_large short-circuits ABOVE the security_headers http middleware. That response carries CORS headers (CORS is outermost) but no X-Content-Type-Options / X-Frame-Options / Referrer-Policy / Permissions-Policy — main.py:155-201, services/request_limits.py:80-92

## 9. Account lifecycle: sign-up, approval, MFA, privileged access

<a id="9-account-lifecycle"></a>

Flow key: `account-lifecycle`

### Overview

Account lifecycle: sign-up → email/OTP verification → profile row creation → approval gate → sign-in + second-step verification → role/feature authorization on every API call → privileged aal2 enforcement → idle expiry → sign-out

**Trigger:** A visitor opens /login, switches to the "Create account" tab and submits SignUpForm (rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/SignUpForm.jsx:104). Everything downstream of that submit runs in Supabase Auth and Postgres — the FastAPI backend never sees a sign-up request.

**Actors, in order:** Browser: Login.jsx step machine (signin/signup/otp/verifyEmail/verify/forgot/reset/success) -> supabase-js anon client (rag-thesis-frontend/src/supabaseClient.js:14) -> Supabase Auth (GoTrue) — signUp, verifyOtp, signInWithPassword, signInWithOtp, mfa.*, refreshSession, signOut -> Postgres trigger on_auth_user_created → public.handle_new_user() (rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:47) -> public.profiles + RLS policies + protect_profile_security_fields trigger -> Gmail SMTP with the HTML in rag-thesis-backend/email-templates/ -> React AuthContext (rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:23) -> axios instance src/api.js:8 with its request/response interceptors -> FastAPI dependencies/auth.py guards (service-role client, RLS bypassed) -> routers/settings.py role-feature matrix + system_settings.role_features -> routers/analytics.py user administration -> IdleSessionGuard + PrivilegedMfaGate -> Cloudflare Turnstile (optional, fails open)

**Latency:** Per authenticated API call the floor is two blocking round trips before any handler body executes: sb.auth.get_user() against Supabase Auth (dependencies/auth.py:266, no local JWT verification and no cache) plus the uncached profiles status read in _ensure_approved_account (:144). A guard adds a third read for the role unless the 60 s _ROLE_CACHE hits (:57), and the feature guards add a system_settings read unless the 60 s _FEATURES_CACHE hits (:340). Every RAG endpoint adds a fourth via resolve_effective_department → get_user_scope (:79), and a superadmin naming a department adds a fifth against public.departments (:123). routers/chat.py:2028 exists specifically to resolve the department once in the route wrapper and thread it into _chat_impl_unstamped (:2112), which the comment says saved two round trips per turn and four for a superadmin. GET /analytics/users issues two profiles queries (analytics.py:312, :316); PUT /users/{id}/details issues three plus an optional departments check. On the client, sign-in serialises checkMfa → setUser → Promise.all([fetchProfile, loadFeatures]) before loading clears (AuthContext.jsx:144-158), deliberately trading first-paint latency for correct route gating. supabase.auth.signInWithOtp dispatches SMTP inside the request (~10 s measured per the comment at VerifyMethodStep.jsx:57-63), so the OTP input is revealed optimistically before the send resolves. /health caches its four-read schema contract for 15 s (main.py:_cached_database_contract) while /ready pays for it every time. IdleSessionGuard ticks once per second and throttles activity writes to 1 Hz.

### Steps (46)

| # | Layer | What happens | Anchor |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | frontend | Login mounts with step='signin' unless the URL hash/search contains type=recovery, in which case it opens directly on 'reset'. A PASSWORD_RECOVERY auth event also forces step='reset'. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Login.jsx:35, :95, :120` |
| 2 | frontend | SignUpForm collects full_name, email, password, confirm, and an Account Type select limited to student \| faculty. Department is NOT an input: it renders a locked 'CCSICT / Assigned' row. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/SignUpForm.jsx:186-204` |
| 3 | frontend | Client validation: name >= 2 chars, EMAIL_RE, and isStrongPassword (8+, uppercase, digit, symbol). Blur-then-live validation; failure sets per-field errors and returns without a network call. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/authUtils.js:5-41, SignUpForm.jsx:43-102` |
| 4 | frontend | useSecurityGate blocks submit only while turnstileEnabled && !token && !unavailable. A hung Turnstile is promoted to 'unavailable' after 12 s and the gate FAILS OPEN — the form submits with no captchaToken and Supabase decides. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/security/useSecurityGate.js:45, SecurityCheck.jsx:18, :156-160` |
| 5 | external | supabase.auth.signUp({email, password, options:{data:{full_name, requested_role}, emailRedirectTo: origin+'/login', captchaToken?}}) goes browser → Supabase Auth directly. requested_role is untrusted client-supplied user_metadata. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/SignUpForm.jsx:110-120, authUtils.js:93` |
| 6 | db | AFTER INSERT ON auth.users fires handle_new_user(): inserts public.profiles(id,email,full_name,role,department,status) with role = faculty only when requested_role='faculty' else student; department hardcoded 'CCSICT'; status = 'pending' for faculty else 'approved'. ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING. SECURITY DEFINER, search_path=public, execute granted only to supabase_auth_admin. | `rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:47-75, migrations/20260828_allow_any_email_signup.sql:9-37` |
| 7 | db | Backfill: supabase_setup.sql also re-inserts a profile for every auth.users row that has none, using the same role/status mapping plus a blank-name fallback of 'ISU User'. Standalone copy in migrations/20260720_backfill_missing_profiles.sql:6. | `rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:80-97` |
| 8 | frontend | Duplicate-address branch: Supabase returns 200 with data.user.identities === [] rather than an error. SignUpForm detects the empty array and shows the non-enumerating panel 'Account creation could not be completed. Try signing in or recovering access.' with a 'Sign in instead →' link, and never advances to the code screen. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/SignUpForm.jsx:128-131, :249-269` |
| 9 | frontend | If the project has email confirmation OFF, signUp returns a session: toast 'Welcome to the archive!' and AuthContext picks the session up. Otherwise onVerifyNeeded fires and Login moves to step='verifyEmail'. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/SignUpForm.jsx:132-137, Login.jsx:172` |
| 10 | external | Supabase sends the Confirm-signup email. Body is email-templates/confirm-signup.html: subject and title carry {{ .Token }}, the code chip renders {{ .Token }}, a de-emphasised VML/anchor button carries {{ .ConfirmationURL }}, greeting shows {{ .Email }}. Logo is a hardcoded public Supabase Storage PNG, not a template variable. | `rag-thesis-backend/email-templates/confirm-signup.html:8, :61, :99, :132, README.md:38-67` |
| 11 | frontend | VerifyEmailStep renders a six-box OtpInput and calls supabase.auth.verifyOtp({email, token, type:'signup'}). Resend uses supabase.auth.resend({type:'signup'}) behind a 60 s cooldown seeded at mount. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/VerifyEmailStep.jsx:25, :42, :19` |
| 12 | frontend | A verified signup creates a session; supabase-js emits SIGNED_IN, AuthContext.syncSession runs, and Login's render-time resolver sends the user through the success ceremony (awaitingVerify is false on this path, so no second-step picker). | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:187-193, Login.jsx:128-133` |
| 13 | frontend | Password sign-in: SignInForm calls signInWithPassword, then onPasswordSuccess() sets awaitingVerify=true, then awaits reloadSession(). Rate-limit errors start a countdown ('Try again in Ns'); 'email not confirmed' routes to step='verifyEmail'. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/auth/SignInForm.jsx:98-119, Login.jsx:211` |
| 14 | frontend | syncSession: forgetPreviousAccount(nextId) clears the whole React Query cache when the identity changes → await checkMfa → setUser → await Promise.all([fetchProfile, loadFeatures]) → setLoading(false). Permissions are awaited before loading clears so ProtectedRoute cannot bounce a legitimate user off a gated route. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:139-159, :72-80` |
| 15 | frontend | checkMfa reads supabase.auth.mfa.getAuthenticatorAssuranceLevel(); needsMfa = (nextLevel==='aal2' && nextLevel!==currentLevel). currentLevel==='aal2' also resets privilegedMfaRefusals to 0. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:82-108` |
| 16 | frontend | fetchProfile reads public.profiles (role, full_name, email, department, status, avatar_url) DIRECTLY through the anon key under RLS policy profiles_select_own (auth.uid() = id). Failure sets profile=null and profileError=true. The backend's GET /analytics/me is never called by the SPA. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/context/AuthContext.jsx:110-128, rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1150-1152` |
