### Branches

- Guest vs authenticated: no Authorization header → get_optional_user returns None → require_chat_access allows → resolve_effective_department(None) pins CCSICT. Supplying an invalid token instead of none is 401, never a downgrade to guest (dependencies/auth.py:243-260).
- requested_role='faculty' → profiles.role='faculty', status='pending'. Anything else (including 'admin' or 'superadmin') → role='student', status='approved'. Department is hardcoded and never client-selectable (supabase_setup.sql:60-62).
- Email confirmation ON → signUp returns no session and Login goes to verifyEmail; OFF → a session arrives immediately and the app skips the OTP screen entirely (SignUpForm.jsx:132-137).
- Duplicate email: with confirmation ON, Supabase returns identities=[] (handled at SignUpForm.jsx:128); with confirmation OFF, it returns the 'user already registered' error, which friendlyAuthError maps to the identical sentence (authUtils.js:54).
- Post-password verification: admin/superadmin WITH a verified factor → TOTP forced; every other combination → picker with 'Email me a code' available (VerifyMethodStep.jsx:52-55).
- Magic-link sign-in (OtpSignInStep) skips the picker entirely — awaitingVerify is never set, so Login resolves straight to 'success' unless needsMfa is true.
- REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA off → aal1 admins pass every privileged guard server-side, but /admin's own client gate still demands aal2 (Admin.jsx:51-58).
- _token_aal: HS256 + configured secret → verified decode; any other alg, or no secret, + a Supabase-validated expected_user_id → unverified decode bound by the sub check; otherwise 'aal1' (dependencies/auth.py:179-213).
- Role cache: hit within 60 s → cached role; miss → profiles read then cache; exception → 'student' returned and NOT cached (dependencies/auth.py:52-73).
- Feature matrix: chat/archive read through DEFAULT_FEATURES on an empty matrix; novelty/upload do not and deny (dependencies/auth.py:350-372 vs :419-451).
- Department scope: non-superadmin pinned to profile department; superadmin may request any name present in public.departments; unknown name → 422 (dependencies/auth.py:113-129).
- Admin vs superadmin in the directory: non-superadmins see only their own department minus superadmins and approve/reject via PUT /users/{id}/role; superadmins see everyone and use PUT /users/{id}/details (analytics.py:311-324, SystemManagementTab.jsx:452-475).
- Idle limit 15 min (isAdmin) vs 30 min (everyone else), plus the 12 h absolute cap on both (idleSession.js:7-17).
- Turnstile: enabled + solved → token forwarded to Supabase; enabled + hung/error/unsupported → gate opens and submission proceeds tokenless; disabled (no VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY) → SecurityCheck renders null (SecurityCheck.jsx:162).
- E2E mode (import.meta.env.MODE==='e2e'): AuthContext reads a localStorage fixture, api.js sends 'Bearer deterministic-e2e-token', and IdleSessionGuard is inert (e2eSession.js:5, api.js:74-82, IdleSessionGuard.jsx:75).

### Failure modes

- Client-side signup validation: 'Please enter your full name' / 'Enter a valid email address' / 'Use 8+ characters with uppercase, number, and symbol' / 'Passwords do not match' — no network call (SignUpForm.jsx:43-60).
- Duplicate address: inline gold panel 'Account creation could not be completed. Try signing in or recovering access.' plus a 'Sign in instead →' link (SignUpForm.jsx:261).
- signUp returns an empty error object: 'Supabase could not complete account creation. Check its Auth or Postgres logs; email delivery is only one possible cause.' (authUtils.js:75-77).
- Captcha enabled in Supabase but no matching key here: 'Authentication security is misconfigured. CAPTCHA is enabled in Supabase, but this app has no matching verification key.' Other captcha errors: 'The security check expired or failed. Complete it again and retry.' (authUtils.js:79-84).
- Supabase per-IP throttling: RateLimitAlert with a countdown parsed from 'after N seconds' (default 60), both submit buttons disabled, label becomes 'Try again in Ns' (SignInForm.jsx:42-47, :216-224).
- Wrong OTP: 'That code didn’t match. Codes rotate every 30 seconds — try the current one.' Expired: 'That code has expired. Request a fresh one and try again.' Both shake the OtpInput and clear it (authUtils.js:56-57).
- Sign-in with an unconfirmed address: routed to step='verifyEmail' plus toast 'Almost there / Verify your email to finish setting up.' (SignInForm.jsx:109-112).
- Bad credentials: 'Incorrect email or password. Double-check and try again.' (authUtils.js:52).
- MfaChallengeStep with no verified factor: 'No authenticator found on this account. Sign in again or contact an admin.' (MfaChallengeStep.jsx:35).
- TOTP enrollment disabled on the project: 'TOTP enrollment is turned off for this Supabase project — enable it under Authentication → Multi-Factor Authentication, then retry.' Factor limit: 'This account has reached Supabase’s factor limit. Remove an existing factor and retry.' (MfaEnrollDialog.jsx:169-178).
- Pending/rejected/missing profile at the API: HTTP 403 'This account is pending approval or has been rejected.' (dependencies/auth.py:151-155).
- profiles unreachable during the approval check: HTTP 503 'Account validation is temporarily unavailable.' (dependencies/auth.py:146-150).
- Invalid or expired bearer token: HTTP 401 'Invalid or expired authentication token' with WWW-Authenticate: Bearer; the browser silently refreshes once, replays, then signs out and hard-navigates to /login?next=… (dependencies/auth.py:275-279, api.js:127-147).
- aal1 session hitting a privileged guard with REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA on: HTTP 403 'Multi-factor authentication is required for privileged access.' → PrivilegedMfaGate modal 'Two-factor authentication required' (dependencies/auth.py:224-240, PrivilegedMfaGate.jsx:63).
- Role refusals, deliberately worded so the browser does not chase a second factor: 403 'Administrator privileges are required for this action.' / 'Faculty or administrator privileges are required for this action.' / 'Superadmin privileges are required for this action.' (dependencies/auth.py:288-321, pinned at tests/test_auth_authorization.py:225-241).
- Feature refusals: 403 'Research chat is not enabled for your role.' / 'Archive access is not enabled for your role.' (dependencies/auth.py:408, :415).
- Department scope violations: 403 'You can only search your assigned department.' / 422 'Unknown department.' / 503 'Department validation is unavailable.' / 403 'A valid user profile is required for department-scoped access.' / 403 'This account is not approved for application access.' (dependencies/auth.py:83-128).
- Admin self-modification: 400 'You cannot change your own role.' (role endpoint) and 400 'You cannot change your own role, department, or status.' (details endpoint); self-delete 400 'You cannot delete your own account.' (analytics.py:331, :429, :372).
- Cross-department or superadmin targets: 403 'You can only modify users in your own department.' / 'Administrators cannot assign or modify superadmins.' / 'Administrators cannot delete superadmins.' (analytics.py:345-348, :392-395, :439-442).
- Deleting a user still referenced by FK-restricted rows: 409 'This account is still referenced by records that must be kept. Reassign or remove them before deleting the account.'; any other failure 500 'The user could not be deleted safely' (analytics.py:388-404).
- Malformed feature matrix: 422 'Feature settings must define student and faculty roles' / 'Invalid feature settings for {role}' / 'Feature settings must use boolean values' (routers/settings.py:26-36).
- profiles read fails in the browser: AuthContext sets profileError → ProtectedRoute renders 'Profile temporarily unavailable' with a Reload button and blocks every shell route (AuthContext.jsx:117-127, ProtectedRoute.jsx:29-45).
- Pending account in the browser: 'Pending Approval' card, copy 'Faculty accounts always need approval, and an administrator can place any account under review.', only a Sign out action (ProtectedRoute.jsx:47-69).
- Rejected account: 'Application Rejected' card naming the profile's current role — 'Your application for the {role} role was rejected by an administrator.' (ProtectedRoute.jsx:71-86).
- /admin factor lookup failure: 'Security status unavailable' card with a Retry security check button; no admin data is fetched (Admin.jsx:63-68).
- Idle expiry: toast 'Signed out for inactivity / Your session was closed to keep this account secure on shared devices.'; absolute cap: 'Session expired / For security, sessions last 12 hours. Sign in again to continue.' (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:91-99).
- Production misconfiguration: the pydantic model_validator refuses to construct Settings — 'Production requires MFA for privileged accounts', 'Production requires a shared Redis rate-limit storage URI', 'Production requires ClamAV malware scanning', 'Production requires a global daily guest token budget' — so the process never starts (config.py:246-253).
- Avatar path not owned by the caller: 422 'Avatar must be an image uploaded to your account' at the API, and the DB trigger independently raises 'Avatar must be an existing object owned by the profile owner' (analytics.py:546, supabase_setup.sql:129-135).

### What gets persisted

- auth.users row — created by Supabase Auth on signUp; email changes propagate to profiles via trigger on_auth_user_email_changed → sync_profile_email (supabase_setup.sql:99-114).
- public.profiles row (id, email, full_name, avatar_url, role, department, status, created_at, updated_at) — written by handle_new_user on insert, by the supabase_setup.sql backfill, by admin UPDATEs from routers/analytics.py under the service-role key, and by the user themselves for full_name/avatar_url only (column-level GRANT at supabase_setup.sql:1160 plus protect_profile_security_fields at :1162).
- auth.mfa_factors — Supabase-managed; written by mfa.enroll / challengeAndVerify / unenroll from MfaEnrollDialog.jsx:226, :254, :275. No application table records enrollment.
- public.activity_log — one row per admin action only: action='role_change' on both user-update endpoints (analytics.py:354, :462), 'user_delete' (:406), 'settings_update' for the feature matrix (routers/settings.py:79). Department is stamped from the actor's profile when not supplied (services/activity.py:19-29).
- public.system_settings where key='role_features' — seeded by supabase_setup.sql:746 and replaced by PUT /settings/features (routers/settings.py:67-76).
- public.chat_sessions.department — stamped at creation from get_user_scope, i.e. the profile's department at that moment (routers/sessions.py:57-60).
- storage 'avatars' bucket object at <user_id>/<name> — referenced by profiles.avatar_url and validated to exist and be owner-prefixed by the validate_editable_profile_fields trigger (supabase_setup.sql:118-145).
- Browser localStorage: the supabase-js session (sb-*-auth-token), 'isu-idle-last-activity', 'isu-session-started-at' (idleSession.js:20-21), plus 'isu_e2e_auth_fixture' and 'isu_e2e_turnstile' in E2E builds.
- Browser sessionStorage: 'iskai_guest_rate_id' — the per-tab X-Guest-ID (api.js:26).
- In-process only, never durable: _ROLE_CACHE (60 s, 2048 entries), _FEATURES_CACHE (60 s), and the React Query cache, which is wiped on every identity change.

### Surprises

- Sign-up never reaches FastAPI. The browser talks straight to Supabase Auth, so the entire account-creation policy — role, department, approval status — lives in one Postgres trigger, public.handle_new_user (supabase_setup.sql:47). Nothing in routers/ or dependencies/ can see or influence a registration.
- The institutional-domain gate is DEAD. migrations/20260803_institutional_signup_domain.sql:26-51 holds off-domain sign-ups at 'pending' via an app.institutional_email_domain GUC; migrations/20260828_allow_any_email_signup.sql:9-28 replaces the same function with no domain logic at all, and filename order makes 20260828 authoritative. supabase_setup.sql:47 matches the later version, so tests/test_schema_consistency.py sees no drift. '@isu.edu.ph' now survives only as a webmail-provider hint (OtpSignInStep.jsx:15) and in comments. Any address on the internet gets an auto-approved student account.
- 20260803 also drops the `grant execute on function public.handle_new_user() to supabase_auth_admin` that both the base file (supabase_setup.sql:75) and 20260828 include. Applying that migration in isolation can leave the signup trigger unexecutable by the auth role.
- The emailed 'second factor' is not a second factor. VerifyMethodStep calls signInWithOtp + verifyOtp({type:'email'}) (VerifyMethodStep.jsx:69-93), which mints a brand-new aal1 session for the same account. The pass is recorded only as client state (`mfaBypass`, AuthContext.jsx:37) and is never sent to, checked by, or persisted for the server.
- Because needsMfa is exposed as `needsMfa && !mfaBypass` (AuthContext.jsx:245), a STUDENT OR FACULTY account with a verified TOTP factor can skip the authenticator by choosing the emailed code. Only admin/superadmin with an enrolled factor are forced through TOTP (privilegedMfa.js:85-89). The comment there states this is intentional, but it means enabling 2FA on a non-privileged account does not make the authenticator required.
- VerifyMethodStep's `totpEnrolled` prop is `needsMfa`, not a factor list (Login.jsx:184). The picker's 'Authenticator app — Not set up on this account' line is therefore derived from the session's assurance level, not from whether a factor exists.
- /admin enforces aal2 for every admin CLIENT-SIDE regardless of REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA (Admin.jsx:51-58). With MFA disabled server-side, the API would serve /analytics/overview to an aal1 admin, but the page refuses to render it.
- The mirror image: _require_feature deliberately does NOT call _require_privileged_mfa for admins (dependencies/auth.py:384-395), so an aal1 admin can still use chat and the archive. Two different MFA policies coexist on the same session.
- require_novelty_access and require_upload_access read get_role_features() raw and have no DEFAULT_FEATURES fallback (auth.py:428-430, :445-447), while GET /settings/features returns DEFAULT_FEATURES when the row is absent (routers/settings.py:60). If system_settings.role_features is missing or briefly unreadable, a faculty member sees the Novelty nav item, passes ProtectedRoute, and gets 403 from the API — the README's 'Novelty scanning is granted to faculty by default' (README.md:190) is only true because supabase_setup.sql:746 seeds the row.
- PUT /analytics/users/{id}/role defaults status to 'approved' when the body omits it (analytics.py:350). Changing a pending user's role — or re-saving the same role — silently approves the account.
- _FEATURES_CACHE is a plain dict with no lock, unlike _ROLE_CACHE which has one (auth.py:33 vs :324). Both invalidation helpers are process-local, so with more than one uvicorn worker a role change or a feature-matrix edit takes up to 60 s to reach the others; the realtime broadcast in AuthContext.jsx:204 only refreshes browsers, not sibling API processes.
- _prune_role_cache evicts oldest-FIRST by dict insertion order (auth.py:44-47), but re-writing an existing key does not move it. A continuously-active admin keeps its original position and is evicted before a colder, more recently inserted entry.
- get_user_role's status check (auth.py:59) is unreachable through any guard — _ensure_approved_account in get_current_user already 403s on a non-approved profile before any role is read.
- _token_aal reads the `aal` claim with verify_signature=False whenever SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET is unset (auth.py:205-208). The sub check binds it to the identity Supabase validated, but the assurance level itself is then unauthenticated client-controlled data. The branch is selected from the unverified `alg` header, so a non-HS256 token takes the unverified path even when the secret IS configured — pinned deliberately at tests/test_auth_authorization.py:147-151.
- Every authenticated request costs at least two network round trips before any handler runs: sb.auth.get_user (no local JWT verification, no cache) plus an uncached profiles read for the status. RAG endpoints add get_user_scope, a third profiles read.
- The SPA reads profiles directly through the anon key under RLS (AuthContext.jsx:112) and never calls GET /analytics/me. src/api.js exports updateMyProfile (PUT) but no getter — the GET endpoint (analytics.py:511) is dead code from the frontend's perspective, and its legacy-column fallback with it.
- A missing profile row tells two different stories: 403 'This account is pending approval or has been rejected.' from the API versus 'Profile temporarily unavailable' in the browser, because AuthContext maps a failed read to profileError and status='unavailable' (AuthContext.jsx:224), not to 'pending'.
- Nothing in activity_log records sign-up, sign-in, sign-out, MFA enrollment, or MFA removal. log_activity is only called for role_change, user_delete, settings_update, and the paper/scan/cleanup actions — the account lifecycle itself is unaudited outside Supabase's own logs.
- GET /sessions, POST /sessions and the message endpoints are guarded by get_current_user + get_user_scope only (routers/sessions.py:15, :45-60). Turning the `chat` feature off for a role blocks POST /chat but still lets that role list, create, rename and delete conversation sessions.
- logo-host.md:6 documents the templates as using `{{ .SiteURL }}/isu-thesis-ai-mark.png`, and README.md:56-60 lists `{{ .SiteURL }}` among the variables used. The shipped HTML hardcodes https://bpxkbeyyxocfvxsxbzgy.supabase.co/storage/v1/object/public/public-assets/isu-thesis-ai-mark.png (confirm-signup.html:61 and the two others), baking the project ref into the email; `{{ .SiteURL }}` appears only inside the HTML comment block.
- reset-password.html and reset-password-preview.html exist and are wired to the recovery OTP, but the email-templates README table and its deploy sections cover only confirm-signup and magic-link — there is no documented step for pasting the reset template into Supabase.
- Password reset redirect targets are inconsistent: ForgotPasswordStep.jsx:44 uses `${origin}/login` (which the isRecoveryUrl check at Login.jsx:35 expects), while the in-app password change in SecuritySection.jsx:111 uses `window.location.origin` — the site root, which has no recovery handler.
- Turnstile fails OPEN by design (useSecurityGate.js:45): a Cloudflare outage lets sign-up, sign-in, OTP request and password reset submit with no captcha token, deferring entirely to Supabase's own enforcement.
- IdleSessionGuard deletes both localStorage clocks whenever `active` is false, and `active` requires !needsMfa (IdleSessionGuard.jsx:75, :120-121). Sitting on the MFA challenge therefore resets the 12-hour absolute-session clock.
- 'Sign out other devices' uses supabase.auth.signOut({scope:'others'}) (SecuritySection.jsx:186) — it revokes refresh tokens but cannot clear the idle/session localStorage keys on those devices, and those tabs only discover the logout on their next token refresh.
- Login is unreachable while signed in: the render-time resolver sends any authenticated visitor to the success ceremony and then to ?next= or /dashboard (Login.jsx:131-133). The only exits are the 'Back to sign in' control in VerifyMethodStep, which calls supabase.auth.signOut() first (Login.jsx:185-189), and the recovery/forgot steps.

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 6 correction(s), 7 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** Step 37: 'Both refuse ... superadmin targets (403 "Administrators cannot assign or modify superadmins.")' — i.e. the /role and /details endpoints share one refusal string.
  **Actual:** The two endpoints use different wordings. PUT /analytics/users/{id}/role raises 'Administrators cannot assign or modify superadmins.'; PUT /analytics/users/{id}/details raises 'Administrators cannot modify or assign superadmins.' (verb order swapped). The DELETE endpoint likewise has its own cross-department string 'You can only delete users in your own department.', not the 'modify' one the trace lists. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:346 vs :433; :384`)
- **Wrong:** Surprise: '_prune_role_cache evicts oldest-FIRST by dict insertion order, but re-writing an existing key does not move it. A continuously-active admin keeps its original position and is evicted before a colder, more recently inserted entry.'
  **Actual:** Refuted — the pathology is unreachable. The write at auth.py:72 is only reached on a cache MISS, and a miss means the key is absent or expired (:54). _prune_role_cache runs first and deletes every entry with expires_at <= now (:38-41), so an expired admin entry is removed and then re-inserted at the END of the dict. Insertion order therefore tracks least-recently-refreshed, and a continuously-active admin is the LAST thing evicted, not the first. There is no other writer (invalidate_role_cache only pops). (`rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:38-47, :52-55, :69-72`)
- **Wrong:** Surprise: 'README.md:56-60 lists {{ .SiteURL }} among the variables used' and logo-host.md:6 documents the templates as using it.
  **Actual:** Half wrong. email-templates/README.md:54-60 is the 'Template variables used (keep verbatim)' table and lists exactly {{ .Token }}, {{ .ConfirmationURL }}, {{ .Email }} — SiteURL is absent. Lines 62-64 then state outright: 'The logo is **not** a template variable: it is a hardcoded public Supabase Storage URL (see logo-host.md)'. The only {{ .SiteURL }} in that file is at :90, inside the preview-regeneration PowerShell snippet. In logo-host.md the stale snippet is at :7 (not :6) and its own next section, ':10-17 How it works today (chosen option)', immediately gives the hardcoded URL. So the documentation already says what the trace claims it contradicts; only one illustrative snippet is stale, and it is self-corrected two lines later. (`rag-thesis-backend/email-templates/README.md:54-64, :90; logo-host.md:7, :10-17`)
- **Wrong:** Step 19 / the branch list: 'Login's render-time resolver: user && needsMfa → verify'.
  **Actual:** The first branch carries its own step exclusions the trace drops: `if (user && needsMfa && step !== 'reset' && step !== 'success')`. A signed-in aal1 account with a pending factor sitting on the reset or success step is NOT forced to 'verify'. The trace records the exclusions only for the second branch. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Login.jsx:129`)
- **Wrong:** Step 42: '/admin adds its own client-side gate ... for any admin, unconditionally'.
  **Actual:** Not unconditional — adminSecurityState short-circuits to 'ready' when isE2ETestMode, and the factor query is `enabled: isAdmin && !isE2ETestMode`. Under `--mode e2e` the aal2 requirement is off entirely. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Admin.jsx:52, :119`)
- **Wrong:** failureModes anchors for the analytics user-management refusals and the avatar/sign-out-others lines.
  **Actual:** Six are wrong: 'Administrators cannot delete superadmins.' is analytics.py:386 (guard :385-386), not :392-395 (that range is the FK-conflict comment inside the delete try/except). The /details cross-department and superadmin refusals are :430-435, not :439-442 (that is the `departments` existence select). 'You cannot change your own role, department, or status.' is :418, not :429 (:429 is `if current_profile.get('role') != 'superadmin':`). 'You cannot delete your own account.' is :371, not :372 (blank). 'Avatar must be an image uploaded to your account' is :548, not :546. supabase.auth.signOut({scope:'others'}) is SecuritySection.jsx:189, not :186. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:371, :386, :418, :430-435, :548; rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/settings/SecuritySection.jsx:189`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- Middleware: the trace's per-request account starts at get_current_user, but every call in this flow passes SlowAPIMiddleware first, with `default_limits=['120/minute']`. The key function does its OWN verified HS256 decode when SUPABASE_JWT_SECRET is set; with no secret, or a non-HS256 token (exactly the asymmetric-key case _token_aal's unverified fallback exists for), it degrades to `guest:sha256(ip:X-Guest-ID)` or `ip:<addr>` — and api.js sends X-Guest-ID only when there is no session, so every signed-in user behind one campus NAT then shares a single 120/min bucket. Also unmentioned: the security_headers middleware, BodySizeLimitMiddleware (registered outside auth deliberately, since FastAPI reads the whole body before resolving a dependency), and CORS, whose allow_headers list is what makes Authorization / X-Guest-ID usable cross-origin. (rag-thesis-backend/main.py:153-155, :161-171, :180, :187-201; rag-thesis-backend/services/rate_limiting.py:12-33, :41-45)
- DB trigger interaction the trace's step 6/7 skips: handle_new_user's name is `coalesce(raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', split_part(email,'@',1))` — coalesce only replaces NULL, so an explicit empty-string full_name (reachable via the raw Auth API; SignUpForm trims and requires >=2 chars) inserts ''. That INSERT fires validate_editable_profile_fields, which raises 'Full name cannot be empty' inside the AFTER INSERT trigger and aborts the auth.users insert with an opaque signup error. The trigger has no 'ISU User' / nullif-trim guard — only the backfill does. (rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:59, :84-88, :118-126, :143-145)
- require_faculty_or_admin calls get_user_role(user.id) twice — once for the role check and again to decide whether to demand aal2. The second is normally a cache hit, but on the transient-failure path get_user_role returns 'student' WITHOUT caching, so the two calls can disagree within one request. (rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:301, :306)
- RoleUpdate.role permits 'admin'. A department admin can promote any approved user in their own department to `admin` through PUT /users/{id}/role; only 'superadmin' is blocked. The trace's approval step treats that endpoint as approve/reject only. (rag-thesis-backend/models.py:243; routers/analytics.py:345-346)
- Two /details refusals the failureModes list omits: 403 'Administrators cannot reassign users to a different department.' and 422 'Unknown department' — note the latter has NO trailing period, unlike dependencies/auth.py:128's 'Unknown department.', so a client matching on the string sees two variants. (rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:435, :446 vs dependencies/auth.py:128)
- Per-admin-call profile reads are worse than the latency note says because the codebase is inconsistent: _admin_scope exists as the helper and is used by two endpoints (:214, :299), while list_users, update_user_role, delete_user, update_user_details and get_system_logs each inline the identical `profiles.select('role,department').eq('id', user.id)` read instead — on top of the two reads require_admin already paid. (rag-thesis-backend/routers/analytics.py:41-46, :214, :299, :316, :333, :373, :420, :475)
- Minor but load-bearing for the trace's step 41: isPrivilegedMfaRefusal uses `detail.toLowerCase().includes(...)`, a substring match anywhere in the detail, not the 'prefix' the trace (and the file's own comment) calls it. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.js:22)

## 10. Browsing, filtering and reading the archive

<a id="10-archive-browse"></a>

Flow key: `archive-browse`

**Trigger:** A signed-in user navigates to `/archive` — by clicking the "Thesis Library" nav item (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/AppShell.jsx:26`), the Dashboard "Browse archive" quick action or "View all" (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Dashboard.jsx:207,234`), the post-upload "View archive" button (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Upload.jsx:578`, `UploadBatch.jsx:1168`), or by typing the URL. Hover/focus on the nav item pre-fires the same three queries (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/routePrefetch.js:21-25`).

**Actors, in order:** Browser SPA (React 19 / react-router / React Query) -> AuthContext + Supabase JS (anon key, direct-to-Supabase profile read) -> Vite dev proxy (dev) or nginx static server (prod) -> FastAPI app (main.py) + SlowAPIMiddleware -> dependencies/auth.py guards (service-role Supabase client `sb`) -> routers/papers.py -> routers/catalog.py -> routers/upload.py::list_tracks -> Supabase PostgREST -> Postgres (RLS bypassed by service-role key) -> services/novelty.py (writes duplication_scan at ingest — read back here) -> Supabase Storage 'pdfs' private bucket (never touched on this flow)

**Latency:** One archive page load costs **4 Supabase round trips before the papers query even runs**: `sb.auth.get_user` (Auth), `profiles.select('status')` in `_ensure_approved_account`, `profiles.select('role,status')` in `get_user_role` (60 s cached, so usually free after the first), and `profiles.select('role,department')` in `list_papers` (never cached). Then the unpaged `papers` select and one `profiles.in_()` for uploader names. Plus two more HTTP requests to the unauthenticated `/upload/tracks` (three table reads inside `_nested_catalog`) and `/catalog/departments/legacy` (three more table reads) — so a cold `/archive` load performs roughly a dozen PostgREST queries. `_nested_catalog` is called twice, once per endpoint, with no shared cache. The whole result set is transferred on every load; filtering, sorting and pagination are entirely client-side, so paging through results is free but the first byte scales with the department's corpus. Axios timeout is 180 s. `staleTime` is 30 s with `refetchOnWindowFocus: false`, so tab-switching back is instant from cache. `prefetchRoute` warms the archive chunk and all three queries on nav hover/focus, once per app session.

### Steps (40)

| # | Layer | What happens | Anchor |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | frontend | `App.jsx` matches `/archive` and wraps the lazy `Archive` chunk in `RouteSuspense` + `ProtectedRoute isAllowed={canArchive}`. There is no `allowGuest`, so `allowGuest` defaults false. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/App.jsx:44-47` |
| 2 | frontend | `ProtectedRoute` gates: `loading` -> `<RouteSkeleton/>`; `!user` -> `<Navigate to="/login" replace/>` (this is the guest exit); `needsMfa` -> `/login`; `profileError` -> "Profile temporarily unavailable" card; `isPending` -> "Pending Approval" card; `isRejected` -> "Application Rejected" card; finally `isAllowed === false` -> `<Navigate to="/dashboard" replace/>`. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ProtectedRoute.jsx:17-89` |
| 3 | frontend | `canArchive` comes from `canUseFeature(role, features, 'archive')`: admin/superadmin are unconditionally true; otherwise `(features ?? DEFAULT_ROLE_FEATURES)[role].archive`, where the default grants `archive: true` to student and faculty. `features` is fetched once at sign-in via `GET /settings/features` and refreshed by a Supabase Broadcast on channel `global_feature_updates`. A guest has `role === null`, so `canArchive` is false — but step 2's `!user` branch fires first. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/permissions.js:10-28` |
| 4 | frontend | `Archive()` reads `{ isAdmin, isSuperadmin, department: userDepartment }` from `AuthContext` and calls `useArchiveCatalog({ isSuperadmin, userDepartment })`. `department` defaults to the string literal `'CCSICT'` when the profile row has none. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx:511-513` |
| 5 | frontend | `useArchiveCatalog` fires exactly three React Query reads on mount: `['papers'] -> listPapers(null)`, `['tracks'] -> getTracks()`, `['departments'] -> getDepartments()`. Global query defaults: `staleTime 30s`, `gcTime 30min`, `refetchOnWindowFocus: false`, and a retry predicate that refuses to retry anything `<500`, `503`, `502` or `504`. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/archive/useArchiveCatalog.js:33-35` |
| 6 | frontend | `listPapers(null)` builds zero query params and issues `GET /papers`. Its `department` and `thesisCategory` parameters are never used by this page — every filter on this screen is applied client-side. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:215-222` |
| 7 | frontend | Axios request interceptor: in dev it first awaits `/__backend-ready`; then `supabase.auth.getSession()` and sets `Authorization: Bearer <access_token>`, or, with no session, `X-Guest-ID: <sessionStorage uuid>`. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/api.js:72-90` |
| 8 | frontend | Transport: in dev, `vite.config.js` proxies `/papers`, `/catalog` and `/departments` verbatim to `http://localhost:8000`; `/upload` uses the HTML-aware `spaAwareApiProxy` because `/upload` is also a React route. `/archive` is NOT an API prefix, so no bypass is needed for it. In prod the built bundle calls `VITE_API_URL` directly, allowed by `connect-src ... ${API_ORIGIN}` in the nginx CSP. | `rag-thesis-frontend/vite.config.js:92-105` |
| 9 | api | `GET /papers` -> `routers/papers.py::list_papers`, declared with `user: ArchiveUser = Depends(require_archive_access)`. SlowAPIMiddleware applies the global `default_limits=['120/minute']` keyed by verified JWT `sub`, or `sha256(ip:guest_id)`, or bare IP. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:26,40-47` |
| 10 | api | `require_archive_access` -> `get_current_user` -> `HTTPBearer(auto_error=True)`. FastAPI 0.139.2 raises **401** `{"detail":"Not authenticated"}` with `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer` when the header is absent — not 403. | `rag-thesis-backend/.venv/Lib/site-packages/fastapi/security/http.py:87-92` |
| 11 | api | `get_current_user` calls `sb.auth.get_user(token)` (a network round trip to Supabase Auth), then `_ensure_approved_account(user.id)` which does a second read: `profiles.select('status').eq('id', …).limit(1)`. Non-`approved` -> 403; a read failure -> 503. | `rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:263-279,141-155` |
| 12 | api | `_require_feature(user, 'archive', …)` calls `get_user_role` (third profiles read, 60 s in-process cache with an LRU-ish prune at 2048 entries). admin/superadmin short-circuit to allowed. Otherwise `_feature_enabled` reads `system_settings.role_features` (60 s cache) and only an explicitly stored `false` denies; a missing key or an unreachable table falls through to `DEFAULT_FEATURES` where `archive: true`. Privileged MFA is deliberately NOT required here. | `rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:389-416,350-372` |
| 13 | api | Inside the handler, `thesis_category` (if supplied) is normalized/validated first: `normalize_thesis_category` lowercases and 422s on anything other than student/faculty. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:49-50` |
| 14 | api | Department scoping, re-implemented inline rather than through `resolve_effective_department`: a fourth profiles read `select('role,department').eq('id', user.id)`, then `if role != 'superadmin': department = profile.department or 'CCSICT'`. The caller-supplied `?department=` is silently discarded for non-superadmins (no 403), and for superadmins it is passed through unvalidated. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:51-55` |
| 15 | db | `_ready_papers_query(fields, department)` builds `papers.select(fields).eq('ingestion_status','ready').order('created_at', desc=True)` plus `.eq('department', …)` when scoped. Optional `.eq` for `program_id`, `specialization_id`, `thesis_category`. There is **no `.limit()`, no `.range()`, no pagination** — one unpaged `.execute()`. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:30-37,62-70` |
| 16 | db | Columns selected: `id,title,authors,year,track,abstract,chunk_count,duplication_scan,created_at,uploaded_by,department,program_id,specialization_id,legacy_track,classification_status,thesis_category`. Deliberately excluded: `content`, `filename`, `storage_path`, `active_index_version`, `redaction_stats`. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:57-61` |
| 17 | db | Postgres serves this through the **service-role** key, so RLS is bypassed. `public.papers` has RLS on with every client policy explicitly dropped and all grants revoked from `anon`/`authenticated`, so the anon key cannot read the table at all — the indirect-access delimitation is enforced by there being no other route to the row. | `rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:1092,1134-1140,1193-1197` |

Otherwise it logs a warning and retries with a reduced legacy field list (`id,title,authors,year,track,abstract,chunk_count,duplication_scan,created_at,uploaded_by,department`) — the pre-PI-04 shape. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:71-86` |
| 19 | api | Uploader display names: the distinct non-null `uploaded_by` ids are collected and resolved with one `profiles.select('id,full_name,email').in_('id', ids)`; each row gets `uploader_name = full_name or email`, or the literal `'Unknown / System'`. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:93-106` |
| 20 | api | `response_model=list[PaperOut]` re-serializes each row. `uploaded_by` is not a `PaperOut` field so the raw uploader UUID is stripped; `uploader_name` IS a field and is returned. `thesis_category` defaults to `'student'` and `classification_status` to `'classified'` so legacy-fallback rows still validate. | `rag-thesis-backend/models.py:94-112` |

On any exception it serves the frozen `models.CCSICT_TRACKS` constant. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1274-1305` |

If the `programs`/`specializations` tables are missing it degrades to `_legacy_catalog()` (departments with the DB's own `tracks`/`track_label`, `programs: []`). The response is passed through `normalizeDepartments` twice — once in `api.js` and again via a `queryClient.setQueryDefaults(['departments'], { select })`. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/catalog.py:67-115,187-190` |

The delete trash button renders only when `isAdmin`. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx:301-381` |
| 33 | frontend | The screening badge's tone comes from `verdictTone(verdict)` (critical for exact_duplicate/high_overlap, warning for review_suggested, neutral otherwise) and its number is **concentration** (largest `match_count` / `total_chunks`), falling back to coverage only when the stored record carries no chunk counts. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/utils.js:79-109,258-279` |
| 34 | frontend | Clicking a card calls `setDetail(paper)` — no second network request. `ArchiveDetailModal` re-renders the exact same object: category/track/department/year badges, "Indexed <date>", authors, the full abstract in a `max-h-56` scroller, the `ScreeningDetail` panel, and the fixed notice "Full text is available only through AI-mediated synthesis in Chat — this protects the author's intellectual property." | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx:475-509,579,609` |
| 35 | frontend | `ScreeningDetail` renders only when `isScreeningFlagged` (either the at-upload record or a nested `rescan`). It shows the verdict label + plain-English explanation, a `ConcentrationMeter` banded at 25% and 70%, a ranked `MatchedTheses` list of other archived theses (title, year, `N of M passages`, closest-passage %), a four-cell figure grid, and — when a rescan disagrees with the upload — a collapsed `<details>` with the historical run. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx:239-299,83-237` |
| 36 | db | That matched-thesis metadata is not joined at read time: `services/novelty.py::_enrich_matched_papers` denormalized `title/authors/year/track/department` of the matched papers into `papers.duplication_scan` JSONB at ingest, and `GET /papers` hands the whole blob back verbatim. | `rag-thesis-backend/services/novelty.py:183-199,126-181` |
| 37 | frontend | Pagination: a `sticky bottom-28 z-20` `GooglePagination` (= `Pagination`) over `filtered.length` with `limit=6`, `showJump`, `hideOnSinglePage={false}`. `changePage` clamps, then snapshots and restores `window.scrollX/Y` twice (rAF + setTimeout 0) so the page does not jump. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx:585-605; rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/ui/Pagination.jsx:43-61` |
| 38 | api | Admin delete branch: the trash button -> `setDeleteTarget` -> `ConfirmDialog` -> `submitDelete` -> `DELETE /papers/{id}` behind `require_admin`, which DOES enforce `_require_privileged_mfa`. The handler re-reads the paper (wrapped in `identifier_not_found` so a non-UUID id answers 404, not 500), 403s if a non-superadmin's department differs, sets `ingestion_status='deletion_pending'`, removes the private object from the `pdfs` bucket, then hard-deletes the row and writes an activity log entry. | `rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:110-164` |
| 39 | frontend | On delete success: `queryClient.invalidateQueries({queryKey:['papers']})` — prefix matching, so it also invalidates the admin tabs' `['papers','all']` and `['papers', role, dept]` — then `toast.success('Thesis removed from the archive')` and the dialog closes. On failure: `toast.error('Delete failed', { description: apiErrorMessage(error) })`, the dialog stays open, and **no invalidation happens**. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/archive/useArchiveCatalog.js:94-107` |
| 40 | frontend | Flow terminates with the rendered grid + `aria-live="polite"` counter "Showing {filtered.length} of {papers.length} indexed thesis/theses", plus a "Clear active filters" ghost button whenever any filter value is truthy. Nothing is written server-side by a browse; no activity-log row, no signed URL, no storage access. | `rag-thesis-frontend/src/pages/Archive.jsx:522,558-565` |

### Branches

- **Guest (no session)**: `AppShell` hides the Thesis Library nav item entirely (`show: !!user && canArchive !== false`, AppShell.jsx:26). Typing `/archive` hits `ProtectedRoute`'s `!user && !allowGuest` branch -> `<Navigate to="/login" replace/>`. `prefetchRoute` warms the chunk but skips all data prefetches when `signedIn` is false (routePrefetch.js:51). A raw `GET /papers` with no bearer answers 401 `Not authenticated`. Guests therefore see the archive nowhere — unlike `/chat`, which is explicitly `allowGuest`.
- **Student**: `canArchive` true by default. Sees every ready paper in their own profile department. No department select (superadmin-only, ArchiveFiltersBar.jsx:226), no delete buttons (`isAdmin` false). Program/specialization/track/year/category filters are all present. Critically, students DO see the duplication-screening badge on cards and the full "Duplication screening" panel in the modal, including the ranked titles of the archived theses each paper matched.
- **Faculty**: identical to student for this flow — `DEFAULT_FEATURES` differs only on `novelty`.
- **Admin (non-superadmin)**: same department pin as a student (papers.py:54-55), plus a red trash button per card and the `ConfirmDialog`. `_require_feature` short-circuits at auth.py:391 so the `archive` toggle cannot lock an admin out. Delete is re-checked server-side against their own department.
- **Superadmin**: `list_papers` skips the department pin entirely, so the response contains every department's ready papers. The extra "All departments" select appears and filtering by department is done **in the browser** (`filterArchivePapers` only honours `filters.department` when `filters.superadmin` is true). With no department selected, `resolveArchivePrograms` finds no department and returns empty `programs`/`specializations`, so the bar falls back to the flat track select built from `GET /upload/tracks` — which is scoped to the evaluation department only.
- **Catalog degradation**: if `programs`/`specializations` are missing, `_nested_catalog` falls back to `_legacy_catalog` (programs: []), the program/specialization selects disappear and the bar shows the department's own `tracks` column under its own `track_label`. In parallel `GET /papers`'s first select fails on `program_id` and falls back to the legacy column list, so `program_id`/`specialization_id`/`thesis_category` come back absent and `PaperOut` defaults `thesis_category` to `'student'` — the Faculty Research tab then matches nothing.
- **Track-label branch**: `unscopedTrackLabel` uses the catalog's own `track_label` when every department agrees on one, otherwise the neutral word 'track'. With the normalized catalog that yields the select label "All program / specializations".
- **Rescan branch**: when `duplication_scan.rescan` exists, `currentScreening` returns the rescan and `atUploadScreening` strips it. If the two verdicts/counts match, the panel prints a one-line "Unchanged since this thesis was uploaded on …"; if they differ, the at-upload run is demoted into a collapsed `<details>`.
- **Zero-match screening branch**: `ScreeningRun` with `matchedChunks === 0` skips the meter and the figure grid and prints "No passage reached the X% threshold across all N passages."
- **Reduced motion**: `usePreferences().reducedMotion` replaces the sliding filmstrip with a static grid and drops the Radix/Framer `layoutId` travelling pill in the pagination control.

### Failure modes

- **No Authorization header** -> HTTP 401 `{"detail":"Not authenticated"}` with `WWW-Authenticate: Bearer`, emitted by FastAPI's `HTTPBearer` itself (fastapi/security/http.py:87-92). Not 403 — the older FastAPI behaviour people expect.
- **Invalid/expired token** -> `sb.auth.get_user` throws -> HTTP 401 `Invalid or expired authentication token` (auth.py:275-279). Client side: the response interceptor sets `__authRetried`, calls `refreshSessionOnce()` (concurrent 401s collapse onto one refresh); on success it replays the request, on failure it calls `supabase.auth.signOut()` and hard-navigates to `/login?next=%2Farchive` via `loginPathWithNext` (api.js:127-147).
- **Account not approved / rejected** -> HTTP 403 `This account is pending approval or has been rejected.` (auth.py:151-155). React Query does not retry (<500). UI: "Archive unavailable" card — the friendlier "Pending Approval" screen only shows when `AuthContext` itself read `status === 'pending'` from the profile row.
- **`profiles` unreadable during `_ensure_approved_account`** -> HTTP 503 `Account validation is temporarily unavailable.` React Query explicitly excludes 503 from retry (main.jsx:46) and the Axios interceptor only retries 502/504, so exactly one request is made -> "Archive unavailable" card with a manual Retry button.
- **`archive` explicitly disabled for the role** -> HTTP 403 `Archive access is not enabled for your role.` (auth.py:412-416). A student already inside the app keeps `canArchive === true` until the superadmin's `features_updated` broadcast lands or they re-authenticate, so the window between client and server truth ends at the "Archive unavailable" card rather than a redirect.
- **Bad `thesis_category` query param** -> HTTP 422 `thesis_category must be 'student' or 'faculty'.` (services/catalog.py:25). Unreachable from this page, which never sends the param.
- **Catalog/category columns absent AND a normalized filter was requested** -> HTTP 503 `Program and category filtering are unavailable until the academic catalog and thesis category migrations are applied.` (papers.py:73-77). Note this fires on *any* exception, including a transient one.
- **Catalog columns absent with no normalized filter** -> no error surfaces at all: a warning is logged and the legacy field list is served (papers.py:78-86). The page renders, silently missing program/specialization/category data.
- **Legacy fallback also raises** -> uncaught, so FastAPI returns HTTP 500. Same for the `profiles` read at papers.py:51, which sits *outside* the try block — a Supabase blip there is a 500, even though the route documents only 503.
- **Rate limit** -> HTTP 429 from SlowAPI. `/papers` inherits the global `120/minute`; `/upload/tracks` and `/catalog/departments/legacy` carry `rate_limit_public` = `30/minute`. A 429 on the two catalog reads degrades silently: `const { data: tracks = [] }` and `= []` for departments mean the filter bar just renders fewer chips with no error.
- **502 / 504** -> the Axios interceptor sets `backendReady = false`, retries the GET up to twice with a 350 ms / 700 ms backoff, then rejects; React Query does not retry again -> "Archive unavailable" card.
- **Network failure / timeout (180 s)** -> no `error.response`; `apiErrorMessage` returns `error.message`; the query errors -> "Archive unavailable" card.
- **Delete, mistyped id** -> `identifier_not_found` converts SQLSTATE 22P02 into HTTP 404 `Paper not found`; an absent row gives the same 404.
- **Delete, wrong department** -> HTTP 403 `You can only delete papers from your own department` (papers.py:127-131).
- **Delete, storage removal fails** -> the row has ALREADY been flipped to `ingestion_status='deletion_pending'`, a `storage_cleanup_queue` entry and a `paper_delete_pending` activity row are written, then HTTP 503 `Private-file deletion is pending. The paper was hidden and can be retried safely.` The frontend toasts the detail but does **not** invalidate `['papers']`, so the card stays on screen for up to 30 s of staleTime describing a paper the server has already hidden.
- **Delete, row deletion fails after storage removal** -> HTTP 503 `Database deletion is pending. Retrying this deletion is safe.`
- **Delete by an admin whose session is only aal1 (REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA on)** -> HTTP 403 `Multi-factor authentication is required for privileged access.` The interceptor's `isPrivilegedMfaRefusal` matches the lowercase prefix and fires `reportPrivilegedMfaRequired()`, which bumps `privilegedMfaRefusals` in AuthContext and raises `PrivilegedMfaGate` in place — the page is not navigated away. Browsing itself is untouched because `require_archive_access` skips the MFA check by design.
- **More ready papers than PostgREST's `db-max-rows` (1000 by default)** -> no error at all. `list_papers` does a single unpaged `.execute()`, so the archive silently truncates and "Showing N of M indexed theses" reports a wrong M.

### What gets persisted

- Nothing. A browse, a filter, a sort, a page change and opening the detail modal write zero rows — no activity_log entry, no chat/session row, no storage access. `GET /papers` is a pure read.
- Delete only: `papers.ingestion_status -> 'deletion_pending'` (papers.py:133), then object removal from the private `pdfs` bucket, then `DELETE FROM papers` (which cascades `chunks` and `paper_index_versions` via `on delete cascade`, and nulls `upload_jobs.paper_id`), then an `activity_log` row `paper_delete` with `{paper_id, title}` (papers.py:163).
- Delete failure path only: a `storage_cleanup_queue` row via `record_storage_cleanup` plus an `activity_log` row `paper_delete_pending` (papers.py:142-149).
- Client-side only: `sessionStorage['iskai_guest_rate_id']` may be written by the request interceptor for an unauthenticated caller — never on this flow, since it requires a session. Filters, sort, page and the open detail row are all React `useState`, lost on remount/refresh. The React Query cache holds `['papers']`, `['tracks']` and `['departments']` for 30 minutes and is `queryClient.clear()`ed whenever the signed-in identity changes (AuthContext.jsx:72-80).

### Surprises

- **The 'All X' option in every filter dropdown is unreachable.** `Select` (Radix) filters out any `<option value="">` — `placeholderOption` is used only as the trigger's placeholder text and `selectableOptions` (value !== '') is what the portal renders (`src/components/ui/Input.jsx:336-339`, `247-251`). So "All programs", "All years", "All departments", "All specializations" and "All …s" are written in `ArchiveFiltersBar` and never appear in the list. Once a filter is set, the only way to clear it is the active-chip X, "Clear all", or "Clear active filters".
- **There is no server-side filtering, sorting or pagination on this page.** `list_papers` accepts `department`, `program_id`, `specialization_id` and `thesis_category`, but `Archive.jsx` calls `listPapers(null)` with no arguments (`useArchiveCatalog.js:33`). Every predicate in `archiveFilters.js` runs in the browser over the whole result set.
- **`list_papers` is the one read that never got the PostgREST row-cap fix.** `routers/analytics.py:64-100` has an explicit `_fetch_all` pager with a comment saying single unpaged reads produced silently wrong numbers past `db-max-rows`. `routers/papers.py:30-37` still does one unpaged `.execute()`, so the archive truncates at 1000 ready papers with no error and a wrong "Showing N of M" count.
- **`list_papers` does not use `resolve_effective_department`.** It re-implements the department boundary inline with its own `profiles` read (papers.py:51-55). Two divergences follow: a non-superadmin passing `?department=OTHER` gets a silent pin to their own department instead of the 403 `You can only search your assigned department.` that `resolve_effective_department` raises, and a superadmin's `?department=` is never validated against `departments`, so a bogus name returns `[]` instead of 422 `Unknown department.`
- **A profile with a NULL department falls back to the hardcoded string `'CCSICT'`** (papers.py:55), not to `settings.thesis_evaluation_department` as `get_user_scope` does (auth.py:89). Two different sources of truth for the same default.
- **`papers.py:71` catches bare `Exception` to trigger the legacy-column fallback.** `services/db_errors.is_missing_column` exists precisely for this and its docstring says "catching every exception turned a transient read failure into a second identical attempt and then an unhandled 500." `routers/analytics.py:202` and `services/retriever.py` both gate on it; `papers.py` does not. (`retriever.py:476` also carries a private duplicate `_is_missing_column_error` rather than importing the shared one.)
- **`uploader_name` is served to every archive reader and falls back to the uploader's email address** (`uploader.get('full_name') or uploader.get('email')`, papers.py:103-106). `PaperOut` exposes it; `Archive.jsx` never renders it — only the admin-only `UploadHistoryTab.jsx:194` does. So a student's `GET /papers` response carries staff email addresses that the UI does not show.
- **Duplication screening is not role-gated.** The badge (Archive.jsx:355) and the whole `ScreeningDetail` panel (Archive.jsx:500) render for students and faculty as well as admins, including the ranked titles and passage counts of the other archived theses each paper matched. Those titles are denormalized snapshots frozen in `papers.duplication_scan` at ingest (`services/novelty.py:183-199`), so a retitled or deleted thesis still appears under its old name.
- **`duplication_scan.flagged` is true for a single 85% chunk match** (`aggregate_matches` sets `'flagged': bool(matches)`, novelty.py:160). So the badge appears on papers whose verdict is `clear`; the colour is driven separately by `verdictTone` over *concentration*, not by `flagged`. A card can carry a screening badge in neutral green.
- **`supabase_setup.sql` contains no `programs`, `specializations`, `papers.program_id`, `papers.specialization_id`, `legacy_track` or `classification_status`, and no `hydrate_paper_academic_classification` trigger** — all of that lives only in `migrations/20260725_…` and `20260819_thesis_category.sql`. `tests/test_schema_consistency.py::test_no_undeclared_drift` iterates `set(base) & set(newest)`, so a function that exists *only* in migrations is never compared. A project built from the base file alone quietly falls onto the legacy archive path.
- **`papers.thesis_category`, `program_id` and `specialization_id` are written by a BEFORE INSERT trigger, not by the Python payload.** `commit_paper_ingestion`'s INSERT column list omits all three (supabase_setup.sql:512-524); `hydrate_paper_academic_classification` re-reads `upload_jobs.request_payload` keyed on `papers.id = upload_jobs.id`. `services/ingestion.py:204-206` even comments that its own `thesis_category` key is "inert". Any paper inserted with an id that is not an upload-job id gets `thesis_category = 'student'` and NULL program.
- **`GET /catalog/departments/legacy` and `GET /upload/tracks` — the archive's whole filter vocabulary — are unauthenticated.** The department/program/specialization catalog is public. Only `/papers` needs a token.
- **A superadmin on "All departments" gets a track filter built from one department.** `resolveArchivePrograms` finds nothing for `selectedDepartment === ''`, so the bar falls back to `activeTracks = tracks` from `GET /upload/tracks`, which `list_tracks` deliberately scopes to `settings.thesis_evaluation_department` (upload.py:1296-1298) to keep CAS program codes off the public marquee. The filter options therefore cover CCSICT while the results cover every department.
- **`GET /departments/` (routers/departments.py:60-74) is dead from the SPA's perspective.** `api.js::getDepartments` reads `/catalog/departments/legacy` instead. The `/departments/` list endpoint is still public and still returns *inactive* departments, unlike `_nested_catalog`'s active-only filter.
- **`const displayPapers = paginated || filtered` (Archive.jsx:436) is dead code** — `paginateItems` always returns an array, and `[]` is truthy.
- **Page state can outrun the page count after a delete.** `page` is only reset by `setFilter`/`setSortBy`, never by the papers query shrinking. `paginateItems` and `Pagination` both clamp independently, so the grid renders the clamped page's cards while `AnimatePresence`'s `key={page}` still holds the stale number — the slide animation is skipped for that transition.
- **The detail modal flashes defaults while closing.** `ArchiveDetailModal` is always mounted with `open={Boolean(detail)}`; `onClose` sets `detail` to `null` immediately, so during Framer's ~200 ms exit the dialog renders title "Dialog" (sr-only), the `Student Thesis` badge from `thesisCategoryLabel(undefined)` and an empty "Indexed " badge.
- **`normalizeDepartments` runs twice** on every departments response — once in `api.js:423` and again through `queryClient.setQueryDefaults(['departments'], { select: normalizeDepartments })` in `main.jsx:57`. Idempotent, but redundant.
- **Abstracts are served in full.** The modal's "Full text is available only through AI-mediated synthesis in Chat" notice is true of the manuscript body (which lives in `chunks.content`, never exposed) but the complete abstract is returned verbatim by `GET /papers` and rendered in a scroller. There is no signed-URL or PDF-download code path anywhere in the app: the only `sb.storage.from_('pdfs')` calls are `download` (worker/scripts), `upload` and `remove`, and `storage.objects` carries a restrictive policy `using (bucket_id <> 'pdfs')` for `anon`/`authenticated`.

**Additional notes returned by the reader** (fields outside the schema, preserved verbatim)

- `branches_note`: n/a

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 5 correction(s), 7 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.

- **Wrong:** Network failure / timeout -> 'no error.response; ... the query errors -> Archive unavailable card' (implying, as the 503 entry states explicitly, one request).
  **Actual:** For a transport failure there is no `error.response`, so `status` is `undefined`: the guard `if (status && (status < 500 || status === 503)) return false` is skipped, `INTERCEPTOR_RETRIED_STATUSES.has(undefined)` is false, and the predicate falls through to `return failureCount < 1`. React Query retries once, so a network error (and a genuine 500) produces TWO requests, not one. Only the <500 / 503 / 502 / 504 statuses get the 'exactly one request' behaviour the trace describes. (`rag-thesis-frontend/src/main.jsx:42-49`)
- **Wrong:** latencyNotes: 'One archive page load costs 4 Supabase round trips before the papers query even runs' — auth.get_user, profiles status, profiles role, profiles role/department.
  **Actual:** For a student or faculty caller there is a FIFTH read: `_require_feature` -> `_feature_enabled` -> `get_role_features()` selects `system_settings.value where key='role_features'` (60 s cached, same TTL as the role cache). It is skipped only for admin/superadmin, who short-circuit at auth.py:391 before `_feature_enabled` is reached — i.e. exactly the roles the trace does not use as its baseline. (`rag-thesis-backend/dependencies/auth.py:334-348, 389-395`)
- **Wrong:** Step 21: `GET /upload/tracks` 'On any exception it serves the frozen models.CCSICT_TRACKS constant.'
  **Actual:** The frozen constant is also served when the live catalog succeeds but returns nothing: the handler ends `return {'tracks': tracks or CCSICT_TRACKS}`. So an evaluation department with no active programs/specializations silently publishes the five pre-catalog names (Data Mining, Web Development, Network Security, Intelligent Systems, Information Management), four of which match no `papers.track` value the normalized catalog stamps — the filter bar offers options that can never match a result, with no error anywhere. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/upload.py:1294-1305`)
- **Wrong:** Surprise: '`list_papers` is the one read that never got the PostgREST row-cap fix.'
  **Actual:** Overstated as a superlative. `_nested_catalog` reads `departments`, `programs` and `specializations` with three single unpaged `.execute()` calls; `GET /departments/` does the same; and the uploader lookup inside `list_papers` itself is an unpaged `.in_()`. `routers/analytics.py`'s `_fetch_all` pager is the exception, not the rule. The substantive half of the claim is right — `list_papers` is the one where truncation is user-visible and produces a wrong 'Showing N of M' — but it is not the only unpaged read left in the codebase. (`rag-thesis-backend/routers/catalog.py:70-83; rag-thesis-backend/routers/departments.py:70-73; rag-thesis-backend/routers/papers.py:96-99`)
- **Wrong:** Catalog-degradation branch: on a project without the catalog migrations, '`thesis_category` come[s] back absent and `PaperOut` defaults `thesis_category` to `'student'`'.
  **Actual:** Direction is right, mechanism is wrong on a base-schema-only project. `supabase_setup.sql` DOES carry `papers.thesis_category` (column, check constraint and a partial index) — it is only `programs`, `specializations`, `papers.program_id`, `papers.specialization_id`, `legacy_track`, `classification_status` and the hydrate trigger that live exclusively in the migrations. So the column exists and is selected, but with no `hydrate_paper_academic_classification` trigger nothing ever writes it (commit_paper_ingestion's INSERT list omits it), and every row sits on the `default 'student'`. Same end state for the Faculty Research tab, different cause; and `GET /papers`'s first select still fails on `program_id`, so the legacy fallback does drop the column from the response. (`rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:261,268-276,512-524; rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260725_normalized_academic_catalog.sql:94-112; rag-thesis-backend/migrations/20260819_thesis_category.sql:47-61`)

Omissions the checker caught:

- Middleware: the trace names only SlowAPIMiddleware. The real chain on `GET /papers` is, outermost first, CORSMiddleware -> BodySizeLimitMiddleware -> `security_headers` (X-Content-Type-Options / X-Frame-Options / Referrer-Policy / Permissions-Policy, plus HSTS on https) -> GZipMiddleware(minimum_size=1024) -> SlowAPIMiddleware. GZip matters for this flow specifically: the whole unpaged corpus is the response body, and it is compressed. (rag-thesis-backend/main.py:155-201)
- `IdleSessionGuard` is mounted above the whole route tree and is active for the entire browse; a tiered idle timeout can navigate the reader to `loginPathWithNext(...)` mid-flow. It is not in the actor list or the branches. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/App.jsx:86)
- `AppShell` mounts a `['health']` query with `refetchInterval: 30_000, retry: false`, so `/archive` also issues `GET /health` every 30 s for as long as the page is open — a recurring request the latency note omits. (rag-thesis-frontend/src/components/AppShell.jsx:33-39)
- `aggregate_matches` truncates the ranked list to `_TOP_MATCHED_PAPERS = 3` before it is enriched and stored, so `MatchedTheses` can never list more than three matched theses no matter how many archived papers the manuscript actually hit — the panel's ranked list is a top-3, not the full set. (rag-thesis-backend/services/novelty.py:37,143-156)
- Step 9's rate-limit key description is conditional: `rate_limit_key` only derives `user:<sub>` when `settings.supabase_jwt_secret` is configured AND the token is HS256-verifiable; otherwise even an authenticated archive request is keyed by `guest:sha256(ip:guest_id)` or bare `ip:`. On a project using Supabase's asymmetric signing keys, every signed-in reader on one campus NAT shares the 120/minute bucket. (rag-thesis-backend/services/rate_limiting.py:12-33)
- The delete persistence list names `upload_jobs.paper_id` but not `storage_cleanup_queue.paper_id`, which is also `on delete set null` — relevant because the storage-failure path writes exactly such a row immediately before the paper may later be hard-deleted. (rag-thesis-backend/supabase_setup.sql:410)
- Minor anchor drift, all still inside the cited construct: `scanMetrics` is utils.js:79-110 and `verdictTone` is utils.js:275-279 (the cited 258-279 opens mid-docstring); the papers-table 'no client policies' comment block is supabase_setup.sql:1188-1197 rather than 1193-1197; the 'inert' comment in ingestion.py is 202-205, not 204-206. Everything else checked exact, including fastapi/security/http.py:87-92 (fastapi==0.139.2), models.py:94-112, novelty.py:126-181 and 183-199, papers.py:30-37/49-55/71-86/142-149, and Input.jsx:337-339/247-251.

## 11. Admin console: analytics, operations, maintenance, settings

<a id="11-admin-ops"></a>

Flow key: `admin-ops`

### Overview

Admin console (/admin): the Overview / Upload history / System Management / Operations tabs, from the React route guard through routers/analytics.py, routers/maintenance.py + services/operations.py, routers/settings.py + routers/departments.py, down to the Supabase RPCs, the operations tables, and the out-of-band PowerShell backup tooling.

**Trigger:** An authenticated admin or superadmin navigates to /admin (or switches tabs inside it). Secondary triggers: OperationsTab's four polling intervals (15 s / 30 s), which fire `evaluate_operations` on every poll, and therefore write alert rows and can send webhooks purely from a browser tab being left open.

**Actors, in order:** Browser (React 19 / Vite, TanStack Query, axios) -> supabase-js (auth + MFA factor check + realtime broadcast, direct to Supabase, bypassing the API) -> FastAPI app (main.py) — routers/analytics.py, routers/maintenance.py, routers/settings.py, routers/departments.py, routers/papers.py, routers/catalog.py -> dependencies/auth.py (service-role Supabase client `sb`, role cache, feature cache, MFA guard) -> services/operations.py, services/activity.py, services/cleanup.py, services/db_errors.py -> Postgres/PostgREST via the service-role key (RLS bypassed; every boundary is Python) -> SQL RPCs: upsert_operational_alert, apply_operations_retention, record_backup_run, register_ingestion_worker, stop_ingestion_worker -> workers/ingestion_worker.py (_Housekeeping) and main.py's optional _operations_monitor thread — both call evaluate_operations independently -> Optional HTTPS webhook receiver (HMAC-signed) -> PowerShell backup tooling on a separate machine (scheduled_backup.ps1 -> record_backup_run.ps1)

**Latency:** "Opening the Overview issues three API calls that together do: 1 profile read for require_admin's role cache, 1 more in _admin_scope, then N+1 paged reads for papers, profiles and scan_history (page size 1000, ceiling 50 pages each) plus 2 exact counts. Every figure is aggregated in Python, so cost scales with archive and user table size, not with what is displayed. OperationsTab is the expensive surface: with the tab open, GET /maintenance/operations/summary runs every 30 s and each run does 2 table reads plus up to 7 alert upserts/resolves and possibly an HTTP webhook with three attempts (0/0.25/1.0 s sleeps inside the request thread, bounded by OPERATIONS_ALERT_TIMEOUT_SECONDS, default 5 s) — so a single poll can block for ~16 s worst case. jobs poll every 15 s, workers and alerts every 30 s. The same evaluate_operations also runs in the ingestion worker's maintenance tick and optionally in an API daemon thread, so three writers can contend on the same dedupe_keys. axios timeout is 180 s globally; React Query staleTime is 30 s with refetchOnWindowFocus off. OverviewCharts is code-split so the stat tiles paint before Recharts (~109 kB gz) loads. All admin list pagination (users, papers, departments, workers, alerts, jobs) is client-side over the full response — /analytics/logs/system alone can return 1000 rows and /papers is unpaginated entirely."
